### [1] [Virtual defect mediated quantum melting in supersolids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34299)
*Lazaros Tsaloukidis,Matheus Grossklags,Luca Salasnich,Alejandro Mendoza-Coto*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文证明软核准一维超固态可通过虚孤子–反孤子对增殖在零温发生Kosterlitz–Thouless型熔化，属本质超越平均场效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对超固态熔化的理解主要基于平均场理论，认为熔化源于结构能量的最小化机制；作者希望超越该图像，寻找由拓扑缺陷驱动的、平均场无法描述的熔化机制。

Method: 研究由浅外势晶格稳定的软核准一维超固体，指出其可通过虚孤子–反孤子对的增殖，在零温熔化进入具有准长程序位置的超流相；从第一性原理推导对应的重整化群（RG）方程，并用以确定系统的临界性质。

Result: 熔化对应Kosterlitz–Thouless转变；临界附近拓扑缺陷的增殖强烈重整化密度调制与超流行为；平均场层面外势晶格仅给出弱调制与强调制态间的平滑过渡，故所描述的转变为本质上的超越平均场效应。

Conclusion: 软核准一维超固体的零温熔化是由虚孤子–反孤子对增殖驱动的KT型转变，需借助RG方法刻画，超出了平均场结构能量最小化框架。

Abstract: The current understanding of supersolid melting is largely based on mean-field theories predicting that such transitions arise from a structural energy minimization mechanism. Here, we show that soft-core quasi-one-dimensional supersolids stabilized by a shallow external lattice can instead melt at zero temperature into a superfluid phase with quasi-long-range positional order through the proliferation of virtual soliton--antisoliton pairs, leading to a Kosterlitz--Thouless transition. We derive the corresponding Renormalization Group equations from first principles and use them to determine the critical properties of the system. Remarkably, the proliferation of topological defects near criticality strongly renormalizes both the density modulation and the superfluidity behavior. The transition described is intrinsically a beyond mean-field effect, since the external lattice produces only a smooth crossover between weakly and strongly modulated states at the mean-field level.

</details>

### [2] [The Cost of a Real Grover Oracle: Minecraft Seed Search from Software to Quantum Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33139)
*Evan M. Reeves,Elias Sopkin,Thomas A. Searles*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文对Minecraft 1.13.1种子恢复的Grover oracle做资源估计，证明已知常数算术特化可显著降低量子线路宽度与门数并提升标记态概率增幅。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将量子搜索用于程序化内容生成（PCG），评估已知常数算术对Grover oracle成本的影响，量化算术特化在PCG派生oracle中的资源收益与硬件编译行为依赖。

Method: 构建由结构放置谓词与线性同余生成器可逆逆变换组成的oracle，用于32位残差搜索；采用直接傅里叶基常数算术、相位标记、uncomputation与完整Grover循环；以独立Java参考校验缩减宽度线路，对生产宽度线路做结构审计；按后端无关的Qiskit level-0 {u, CX}公共基计数，并在IBM处理器与Quantinuum H2-1上执行缩减宽度放置电路的完整Grover循环。

Result: 常数算术将放置谓词宽度由6n+3降至4n+3量子比特，双量子比特门数的结构缩放由Θ(n³)降至O(n²)；代表性格局下residual-search构造为275量子比特、48,896个CX门、深度4,482，基线为467量子比特、2,038,804个CX门、深度186,328；多放置合取呈现宽度—重计算权衡，精确枚举发现候选缩减的平台区；硬件上专用构造单次迭代的标记态概率增幅大于基线，且IBM实验显示显著的路由敏感性。

Conclusion: 已知常数算术特化可大幅降低Grover oracle的量子比特宽度、CX门数与深度，但其编译后硬件表现强烈依赖路由与执行上下文。

Abstract: We present the resource estimation of how known-constant arithmetic changes the cost of a Grover oracle for Minecraft 1.13.1 seed recovery, an application of quantum search to procedural content generation. The oracle developed here combines a structure-placement predicate with a reversible inverse transform of the linear congruential generator for the 32-bit residual search. Reduced-width arithmetic, phase marking, uncomputation, and complete Grover loops are verified against an independent Java reference, while production-width circuits are structurally audited. Direct Fourier-basis constant arithmetic reduces the placement-predicate width from 6n+3 to 4n+3 qubits and the structural scaling of the two-qubit gate counts from Theta(n^3) to O(n^2). Under backend-agnostic common-basis Qiskit level-0 {u, CX} accounting, the representative nonzero-r folded residual-search construction uses 275 circuit qubits, 48,896 CX gates, and depth 4,482, versus 467 qubits, 2,038,804 CX gates, and depth 186,328 for the original register-to-register baseline. Multi-placement conjunctions expose a width-recomputation tradeoff, and exact enumeration identifies a plateau in candidate reduction for the benchmark. We executed reduced-width placement circuits within complete Grover loops on IBM processors and Quantinuum H2-1 and show larger one-iteration increases in marked-state probability for the specialized construction than for the baseline. These results quantify the resource benefits of arithmetic specialization and show substantial routing sensitivity in the reduced-width IBM hardware experiments. Together, the results quantify the effect of specializing known-constant arithmetic within a PCG-derived oracle and show that its compiled hardware behavior strongly depends on routing and execution context.

</details>


### [3] [Direct tests of conformal symmetry with Rydberg atoms: assessment and proposals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33156)
*Junchen Rong,Slava Rychkov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出利用里德堡原子阵列直接检验共形对称性的方案：圆周排布原子并测量等时两点关联函数，估算所需实验快照数，并优化Rabi频率Ω与失谐Δ的绝热路径以提升临界态制备保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形场论预言的关联函数特征函数形式此前缺乏直接的实验检验手段；现有里德堡实验中临界态制备时间长、保真度有限，制约了对称性检验的精度。

Method: 将原子排布在圆环上，测量等时两点关联函数以直接对比CFT预言的形式；通过统计分析估算达到足够统计精度所需的实验快照（测量次数）；对用于绝热制备目标临界态的含时Rabi频率Ω与失谐Δ的ramp profile进行优化。

Result: 优化后的ramp profile在远短于既往实验的演化时间内实现了更高的态保真度；并给出了分辨CFT关联行为所需的快照数量估计。

Conclusion: 结果表明，基于当前里德堡原子技术，对共形对称性进行直接实验检验是可行的。

Abstract: We propose a direct test of conformal symmetry using Rydberg-atom arrays. By arranging the atoms on a circle and measuring equal-time two-point correlation functions, the characteristic functional form predicted by conformal field theory can be tested directly. We estimate the number of experimental snapshots required to resolve this behavior with sufficient statistical precision. We further optimize the time-dependent ramp profile of the Rabi frequency~$Ω$ and detuning~$Δ$ used to prepare the target critical state adiabatically. The optimized profile achieves higher fidelity over much shorter evolution times than those employed in previous experiments. Taken together, these results suggest that a direct experimental test of conformal symmetry is feasible with current Rydberg-atom technology.

</details>


### [4] [Fully quantum training of parametric circuits with Quantum Hamiltonian Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33144)
*Marco Burato,Leonardo Banchi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出将参数化量子电路的全流程训练放在量子计算机上完成：用QHD把参数编码为量子比特位置、以代价函数为势能，并通过一个在线步骤让参数寄存器相干地控制第二寄存器上的电路；证明对具光滑周期延拓的代价函数（含参数化电路）仿真误差界与网格分辨率无关、且随参数个数线性增长；并表明理想动力学仅由一条控制绝热性的曲线决定，剩余自由度可用于压缩门数，基准函数与电路仿真中同等代价下显著提高找到全局最小值的概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数化量子电路训练依赖经典局部优化器，每次只能逐点调用量子计算机评估代价函数或其梯度；QHD虽以量子粒子在势能景观中隧穿寻找全局最小，但它假设代价函数可经典计算，其数字化实现的误差界随每参数比特数指数增长，且时间调度沿袭经典优化，缺乏量子层面的理论刻画。

Method: 在量子计算机上端到端实现QHD训练：参数成为量子粒子位置，代价函数作为势能，用两条时间调度分别控制动能与势能；通过在线步骤使编码参数的比特相干地控制第二寄存器上的参数化电路；对具光滑周期延拓的代价函数给出与网格分辨率无关的仿真误差界，并证明对电路情形误差界至多随参数个数线性增长；进一步证明理想动力学对两条调度的依赖可约化为单条控制绝热性的曲线，剩余自由度用于最小化门数。

Result: 理论上得到分辨率无关的仿真误差界及对参数个数线性增长的门数/误差界；揭示调度的单曲线等价性；在基准函数与参数化电路的仿真中，同等资源代价下按此设计的调度显著提升了找到全局最小值的概率。

Conclusion: QHD可作为完全量子化的参数化电路训练范式，兼具可证明的数字化仿真误差控制与调度自由度压缩带来的门数优化，实验仿真显示其在全局最优搜索上优于常规做法。

Abstract: Parametric quantum circuits are usually trained by classical, local optimizers, which use the quantum computer only to evaluate the cost function, or its gradient, one point at a time. Quantum Hamiltonian Descent (QHD) takes a different route: in this quantum algorithm for continuous optimization, the parameters become the position of a quantum particle, with the cost function as its potential, and two time schedules control kinetic and potential energy so that the wavefunction explores the whole landscape and tunnels towards the global minimum. QHD, however, assumes a classically computable cost function, and its digital implementation is poorly understood: standard error bounds grow exponentially with the number of qubits per parameter, and the schedules are borrowed from classical optimization. Here we propose to train parametric circuits with QHD entirely on the quantum computer, through an online step in which the qubits encoding the parameters coherently control the circuit on a second register. We prove that, for cost functions with a smooth periodic extension, including those of parametric circuits, the simulation error admits a bound independent of the grid resolution, and that for circuits the bound grows at most linearly with the number of parameters. We also show that the ideal dynamics depends on the two schedules only through a single curve, which controls the adiabaticity of the search, and that the remaining freedom can be used to minimize the gate count. Simulations on benchmark functions and parametric circuits show that, at equal cost, schedules designed in this way markedly increase the probability of finding the global minimum.

</details>


### [5] [Quantum state revivals, time quasicrystals, and entanglement steering in a Fibonacci-driven central-spin model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35555)
*Anushka Agrawal,Adithya A. Vasista,Tanay Nag,Sayan Choudhury*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文在无 disorder 的 Fibonacci 驱动中心-卫星自旋模型中，通过精细调谐相互作用与横向驱动，实现完美复现与长寿命时间准晶（TQC），并在 Clifford 点动力学生成 Bell-cat/自旋-cat 态用于海森堡极限度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是寻找 Floquet 范式之外的非平衡物相，探索能否在无序缺失条件下仅凭准周期驱动产生稳定复现与时间准晶。

Method: 研究对象为中心自旋与卫星自旋间具 Ising 相互作用的 Fibonacci 驱动中心-自旋模型。利用解析方法分析纠缠生成的抑制条件：当相互作用强度 λ=2mπ 时任意横向驱动与驱动序列下频闪时刻中心-卫星间不产生纠缠；当 θ=π 且初态具确定浴磁化时任意 λ 下无纠缠。并考察参数偏离精细调谐极限时的鲁棒性及特殊 Clifford 点（奇偶依赖的 Fourier 响应），数值/解析追踪磁化动力学的存活时间。

Result: λ=2mπ 时卫星自旋磁化呈现无限长寿的准周期振荡；θ=π 初态具确定浴磁化时在 Fibonacci 时刻出现周期-3 复现。偏离精细极限后仍存在长寿命 π-脉冲近邻 TQC 区间；Clifford 点处卫星磁化呈完美准周期振荡并具奇偶依赖 Fourier 响应，偶数卫星自旋下偏离 Clifford 点仍可形成 Clifford-近邻 TQC。

Conclusion: 模型提供无需无序的 TQC 生成途径，并表明 Clifford 点处系统沿纠缠轨迹演化、动力学生成 Bell-cat 与自旋-cat 态，其集体自旋磁化涨落随卫星自旋数二次标度，可实现海森堡极限度量。

Abstract: Motivated by the search for non-equilibrium phases of matter beyond the Floquet paradigm, we propose a route to realize perfect revivals and time quasicrystals (TQCs) in a Fibonacci-driven central-spin model with Ising interactions between the central and satellite spins in the absence of disorder. We analytically demonstrate that when the interaction strength, $λ$ is tuned to $2 m π$, no entanglement is generated between the central and satellite spins at stroboscopic times, independent of the strength of the transverse driving field and the drive sequence. This leads to indefinitely long-lived quasiperiodic oscillations in satellite spin magnetization. Furthermore, for initial states with a definite bath magnetization, no entanglement is generated when the transverse driving field, $θ$ is tuned to $π$ for any value of $ λ$, and the satellite spin magnetization oscillations exhibit period-3 revivals at Fibonacci times. Examining the robustness of these oscillations away from this fine-tuned limit, we identify a parameter regime where the quasiperiodic response does not decay over the timescales probed, giving rise to a long-lived $π$-pulse-proximate TQC regime. We further show that at special Clifford points, the system exhibits perfect quasiperiodic oscillations of the satellite spin magnetization, with a parity-dependent Fourier response. This response persists over the timescales probed, even away from the Clifford points for an even number of satellite spins, leading to the formation of a Clifford-proximate TQC. Finally, we demonstrate that at this Clifford points, the system is steered along an entangled trajectory, leading to the dynamical generation of Bell-cat and spin-cat states. These states exhibit magnetization of collective spin fluctuates quadratically with the number of satellite spins, thereby enabling Heisenberg-limited metrology.

</details>


### [6] [Genuinely multipartite entangled qudits: one rule to link them all](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33160)
*Gonçalo M. Quinta,Rui André*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文将"环链"作为多体纠缠的分类图像，用超图重构链多项式，给出任意链类的纯qudit态显式构造，证明其纠缠在任意粒子丢失模式下与链完全一致，并证明m-resistant纯qudit态对任意粒子数与m均存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环链的切割与粒子迹掉（丢失）之间的类比可把多体纠缠按"丢失部分粒子后哪些粒子群仍纠缠"进行分类，这对鲁棒量子网络至关重要；但此前仅特殊情形才知道实现给定链类的纯态，一般链类的纯态构造与严格保真性验证存在空白。

Method: 把链多项式重新表述为超图，基于超图结构给出纯qudit态的显式公式化构造；随后在任意粒子丢失模式（部分迹掉）下分析剩余态的纠缠结构，并追踪其真多体纠缠性质。

Result: 证明对每一链类，构造态的纠缠在任意丢失模式下与对应链精确匹配；该构造同时控制真多体纠缠，精确判定哪些保持纠缠的粒子群是真多体纠缠的，且可使全部成为真多体纠缠；由此证明m-resistant纯qudit态猜想：存在在丢失任意m个粒子后仍纠缠、丢失m+1个后完全可分的态，适用于任意粒子数与任意m。

Conclusion: 环链图像可作为多体纠缠分类的严格可实现框架，超图表述提供了构造性、可验证的纯qudit态族，彻底解决链类的实现问题并正向解决m-resistant态存在性猜想。

Abstract: Links of rings offer an intuitive picture of multipartite entanglement. Cutting one ring can free some of the others, just as losing a particle, i.e. tracing it out, can disentangle some of the remaining ones. This analogy classifies multipartite entanglement according to which groups of particles stay entangled when others are lost, a question at the heart of robust quantum networks. However, pure states realizing a given link class were known only in special cases. Here, we close this gap. Recasting link polynomials as hypergraphs, we give an explicit formula for pure qudit states and prove that, for every link class, their entanglement under any pattern of particle loss matches the link exactly. The same construction also controls genuine multipartite entanglement, determining exactly which of the groups that stay entangled are genuinely multipartite entangled, and all of them can be made so. As a consequence, we prove the conjecture that $m$-resistant pure qudit states, which stay entangled after the loss of any $m$ particles but become fully separable after the loss of $m+1$, exist for any number of particles and any $m$.

</details>


### [7] [Post-Quantum Perfect Zero-Knowledge Arguments for NP From Any One-Way Permutation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33185)
*Frédéric Dupuis,Rémi Ligez,Khaireddine Medhioub,Louis Salvail,Louis-Charles Thibodeau*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次证明：后量子单向置换足以构造针对所有NP语言的、可抵抗量子对手的完美零知识经典论证（perfect zero-knowledge classical arguments）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的完美零知识论证方案通常依赖更强的假设（如陷门置换、碰撞抗性哈希等），本文希望将构造基础弱化到最弱的密码学原语之一——后量子单向置换，从而厘清完美零知识论证所需的最小假设。

Method: 分两步：(1) 证明基于任意置换并使用交互哈希（interactive hashing）构造的NOVY比特承诺方案，在后量子单向置换假设下具备对量子多项式时间承诺方的sum-binding与collapse-binding性质；(2) 利用NOVY方案的collapse-binding性质，证明Blum针对HAMCYCLE问题的完美零知识论证在量子对手面前无需修改即保持完美零知识性。

Result: NOVY比特承诺在后量子单向置换下具有sum-binding与collapse-binding；Blum的HAMCYCLE完美零知识论证对量子对手仍保持完美零知识，且安全基于后量子单向置换。

Conclusion: 后量子单向置换足以实现针对全部NP的完美零知识经典论证，且具有抗量子安全性，给出了该目标的最弱假设之一。

Abstract: In this paper, we show for the first time that post-quantum one-way permutations are sufficient for perfect zero-knowledge classical arguments for all of NP with security guaranteed against quantum adversaries. We first establish that Naor, Ostrovsky,Venkatesen, and Yung's bit commitment scheme built from any permutation using interactive hashing, called the NOVY scheme, is sum-binding and collapse-binding against quantum polynomial-time committers provided the permutation is post-quantum one-way. We then show that the collapse-binding property of the NOVY scheme allows for Blum's perfect zero-knowledge argument for HAMCYCLE to remain so exactly as it stands against quantum adversaries.

</details>


### [8] [Quantum Heuristic Separation of Comb Inequalities for the Travelling Salesman Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33188)
*Yohan Finet,Victor Drouin-Touchette*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种用于旅行商问题梳状不等式的量子启发式分离算法，基于可制备所有固定齿数梳的均匀叠加态，并用Grover-Mixer QAOA搜索被违反的不等式，给出显式电路构造与资源分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梳状不等式是旅行商问题的重要割平面，但其分离在计算上极具挑战性，作者希望借助量子计算范式为该分离问题提供新思路。

Method: 利用多项式数量的门和量子比特制备所有固定齿数梳的均匀叠加态；给出将数据寄存器汉明重量以one-hot形式相干编码到辅助寄存器的电路；再以Grover-Mixer Quantum Alternating Operator Ansatz搜索被违反的不等式；并显式构造mixer酉算子、完整问题哈密顿量及资源分析。

Result: 在预设的多项式预算（QAOA轮数、参数优化步数、采样数）下算法运行时间为多项式；但作者未提供证据表明此类预算足以在存在被违反梳时以常数概率找到它。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了梳不等式量子启发式分离的完整电路级方案与资源评估，属于可行性构造与框架性贡献，实际分离效能（常数概率保证）仍是开放问题。

Abstract: Comb inequalities are essential cutting planes for the travelling salesman problem, yet their separation remains computationally challenging. We propose a quantum heuristic separation algorithm for this family of inequalities. The algorithm uses a procedure that prepares a uniform superposition of all combs with a fixed number of teeth using polynomially many gates and qubits. As part of this procedure, we present a circuit that coherently encodes the Hamming weight of a data register in one-hot form in an auxiliary register. The separation algorithm then searches for violated inequalities using the Grover-Mixer Quantum Alternating Operator Ansatz. Abstract: We investigate surprising consequences of the hypothesis that gravity and proper time obey the quantum superposition principle. Realistic device data further show that hardware response can change the preferred states and evolution times before execution. Abstract: Bayes' rule connects forward and reverse processes in classical probability theory, and its quantum analogue has been discussed in terms of the Petz (transpose) map. For quantum dynamics governed by the Lindblad equation, the corresponding Petz map can also be written in Lindblad form. In classical stochastic systems, the analogue of the Lindblad equation is the Fokker-Planck equation, and applying Bayes' rule to it yields the reverse diffusion equation underlying modern diffusion-based generative models. It is known that a semiclassical approximation of the Lindblad equation yields the Fokker-Planck equation for the Wigner function, which is a quasiprobability distribution defined on phase space as the Wigner transform of the density operator. Here we demonstrate that applying the same approximation to the Lindblad equation associated with the Petz map produces an equation that coincides with that obtained from the Fokker-Planck equation via Bayes' rule. This finding establishes a direct correspondence between the Petz map and Bayes' rule, unifying quantum reversibility with classical reverse diffusion.

Abstract: This work proposes a self-attention mechanism based on the fully connected quantum Boltzmann machine (FC-QBM), and further constructs a fully connected quantum Boltzmann machine language model (FC-QBM-LM) using the mechanism as the core engine. By performing unitary evolution on semantic quantum states, the mechanism implicitly implements positional encoding and self-attention computation, eliminating the need to decompose continuous semantic streams into discrete matrices for explicit matrix multiplication, with the measurement results naturally satisfying the normalization condition. Specifically, this work rigorously derives the computational principle of the self-attention mechanism based on FC-QBM, and demonstrates that this mechanism is mathematically equivalent to the classical self-attention mechanism. The mathematical principles governing the training and generation of FC-QBM-LM are thoroughly derived, and the two corresponding algorithms are subsequently developed. An 8-qubit FC-QBM-LM is finally constructed and trained on the corpus of Tang poetry. Under the 8-qubit setting, the quantum layer implements the entire positional encoding and self-attention computation pipeline with only 44 parameters. In the noiseless scenario, the model exhibits favorable convergence. Furthermore, the quantum circuit proposed in this work, which terminates with Y-axis rotation gates, achieves better convergence than the counterpart terminating with X-axis rotation gates. Under the small-sample regime and across eight distinct noise intensity levels, the model demonstrates strong noise robustness, retaining over 99% of its noiseless convergence performance.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三角色码中hook错误会降低电路级距离。Kishony与Fowler近期工作按hook错误为哪一种颜色的任意子提供捷径来分类降距hook，并据此为每种颜色的稳定子选择CNOT顺序以规避错误。本文旨在检验这一分类是否完备。

Conclusion: Kishony–Fowler的任意子类型分类不完备，需补充junction hook error这一类；它说明该色相关调度虽仍有残余降距漏洞但能达d−⌊(d+3)/6⌋的电路级距离，而非空间均匀调度下的d/2。

Abstract: Color-dependent syndrome extraction schedules have been proposed to mitigate hook errors in the triangular color code. Recent work by Kishony and Fowler (arXiv:2603.28852) identifies distance-reducing hook errors by the type of anyon for which they provide a shortcut and chooses the CNOT ordering for stabilizers of each color to avoid these errors. We identify an additional class of distance-reducing hooks, which we call junction hook errors, that is not captured by this classification. The two data qubits of a junction hook error terminate anyon strings of two different colors at the point where the three colored strings of a logical operator meet, which we call the junction. As such, the hook error does not correspond to a single type of anyon, yet it still reduces the distance. For distances $d=7,9,11$, we verify that every non-corner plaquette admits a scheduled hook error that, together with $d-2$ single data qubit faults, produces an undetectable logical error using $d-1$ faults. Each of these is a junction hook error. Because of this, every stabilizer, including those in the bulk, still requires additional protection from hook errors (such as flag qubits) to restore the full circuit-level distance with this schedule. A minimum weight logical operator has at most one junction, however, so junction hook errors cannot accumulate on one minimum weight logical operator in the way that single-color malign hook errors can. This helps explain why the schedule still improves the circuit-level distance to $d-\lfloor (d+3)/6\rfloor$, rather than the $d/2$ distance associated with spatially uniform color code scheduling.

We further demonstrate an independently tunable timescale for multipartite dynamics using quantum-error-correcting codes. TL;DR: 该文研究零噪声外推（ZNE）在变分量子优化中是否"降低估计误差却导致更差决策"，通过QAOA/MaxCut的配对基准发现MSE改善与决策后悔损失可同时出现，但稳健的损失样本少且幅度小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ZNE通常以缓解后观测量的精度（如MSE）来验证，但变分量子优化恰恰用这些观测量来选择参数。因此需要回答：ZNE带来的精度增益是否、以及在多大程度上会与更差的参数决策相重合。

Method: 在4–6量子比特MaxCut的深度一QAOA模拟中，对每个采样参数景观（120×120网格）同时计算MSE与所选网格最小值的理想后悔值；在相同每格点总测量次数预算下比较一至三阶Richardson外推与未缓解估计。先做675个条件的探索性筛选（各点随机种子存在碰撞），用族错误率（Holm）校正检验540个重复条件中同时出现方向性MSE改善与后悔增加者；再以每条件100次全新配对重复进行冻结确认。理论上区分偏差路径（残余偏差重排均值景观）与方差路径（Richardson权重放大有限采样噪声），并在有界控制网格上计算精确一、二阶矩。

Result: Holm分析仅保留540个重复条件中的4个同时满足MSE方向性改善与后悔上升；冻结确认的100次新配对重复复现全部4个，MSE中位改善7.65%–73.09%，对应归一化后悔中位增幅仅0.0031–0.0073。精确矩分析未发现偏差路径导致的反转：缓解后的均值景观保留理想极小值，ZNE可将期望MSE降低一半以上，但有限采样下的选点仍产生更高的平均后悔。

Conclusion: 在严格检验下存活的决策损失数量少且幅度小，但可复现，且无法由聚合MSE反映。作者提出配对基准，并建议在面向优化评估缓解方法时，除估计误差外同时报告决策后悔。结论限于模拟的穷举网格，不涉及硬件或优化器迭代回路。

Abstract: Zero-noise extrapolation (ZNE) is usually validated by the accuracy of mitigated observables, yet variational quantum optimization uses those observables to select parameters. We ask whether, and by how much, an accuracy gain from ZNE coincides with a worse decision. In depth-one simulations of the quantum approximate optimization algorithm (QAOA) for MaxCut on four to six qubits, each sampled $120\times120$ landscape yields both its mean-squared error (MSE) and the ideal regret of its selected grid minimum, comparing Richardson orders one to three with unmitigated estimation at the same total shot budget per grid point. In an exploratory screen of 675 conditions, whose per-point random seeds collide, a familywise (Holm) analysis retains only four of 540 replication conditions as showing both directional MSE improvement and regret increase. A frozen confirmation with 100 fresh paired repetitions per condition reproduces all four: median MSE improvements of 7.65-73.09% accompany median normalized regret increases of only 0.0031-0.0073. We distinguish a bias route, in which residual bias reorders the mean landscape, from a variance route, in which Richardson weights amplify finite-shot noise. Exact first and second moments on a bounded control grid find no bias-route reversal: mitigated mean landscapes preserve the ideal minima, and ZNE can more than halve expected MSE, yet finite-shot selections incur higher mean regret. Decision losses that survive strict testing are few and small in this benchmark, but reproducible and not revealed by aggregate MSE. We present a paired benchmark and recommend reporting decision regret alongside estimation error when mitigation is assessed for optimization. Results concern simulated exhaustive grids, not hardware or optimizer loops.

Abstract: We construct a controllable imaginary-time Mpemba effect in quantum spin systems using spatially modulated local rotations without changing initial bipartite entanglement spectra and correlation magnitudes. This feature unambiguously isolates the redistribution of spectral weights as the sole driver of the anomalous relaxation. Using the compact-boson description, we reveal that these twists act as precise mode selectors: higher-harmonic twists exclusively populate high-energy standing waves, structurally bypassing slow-decaying infrared modes to accelerate convergence. We derive exact analytical crossing times for these reversals and demonstrate that intentionally reintroducing the slow-mode population systematically delays the ITME. This exact mechanism is validated beyond the fixed-point theory via calculations on interacting XXZ chains and ladders. Furthermore, a twelve-qubit simulation of variational quantum imaginary-time evolution reproduces the reversals and their controlled delay, providing a concrete route to realize this protocol in realistic quantum circuits. Ultimately, these results provide a strictly controllable and exact starting point for realizing and investigating the imaginary-time Mpemba effect, establishing a foundational paradigm to harness anomalous non-equilibrium relaxation in quantum ground-state preparations.

*Rathindra Nath Das,Arnab Kundu,Nemai Chandra Sarkar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了Leggett-Garg (LG)不等式违背与时空密度核负本征权重之间的普适定量界限：给出任意测量次数、任意维度/初态/动力学/测量规则下的最小相容核负性精确关系，该最小值非零当且仅当至少有一条LG不等式被违背。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LG不等式检验不同时刻测得的相关性是否可由历史的联合正概率分布描述；违背意味着任何重现这些相关性的联合准概率分布必含负值，但反之负值存在并不必然导致违背。因此需要厘清LG违背与密度核负性之间的定量关系。

A violation requires negative values in any joint quasiprobability distribution reproducing those correlations, but such distributions can have negative values even without a violation. Spacetime density kernels extend the density-matrix description to multiple times and are used to study timelike entanglement. We establish a universal bound that relates LG violations to their negative eigenvalue weight. For LG tests involving any number of measurements, we determine the least compatible kernel negativity through an exact relation valid for any system dimension, initial state, dynamics, or measurement rule. This minimum is nonzero if and only if at least one LG inequality is violated. Abstract: Quantum computing offers promising approaches to combinatorial optimization problems whose search spaces grow exponentially with problem size. The Closest String problem belongs to this category, which seeks a string that minimizes the maximum Hamming distance to any string in a given collection of reference strings. In this work, we present an end-to-end quantum implementation of the Closest String problem using the Non-Variational Quantum Walk Optimization Algorithm (NV-QWOA), evaluating its resource requirements and solution amplification capabilities. Our automated workflow converts arbitrary problem instances into quantum circuits expressed in one- or two-qubit gates that encode candidate solutions, evaluate the objective function, and apply NV-QWOA optimisation to increase the measurement probabilities of high-quality solutions. Analytical resource estimates yield a qubit complexity of $\mathcal{O}(nm\log k)$ and a circuit depth of $\mathcal{O}(pm\log^2 n + pn\log n)$, where $n$ is the string length, $m$ is the number of reference strings, $k$ is the alphabet size, and $p$ is the number of NV-QWOA iterations. We introduce a revised prescription for the phase-separation parameter, $γ$, that depends on problem size but requires no instance-specific optimization. This prescription mitigates excessive phase wrapping modulo $2π$, which can weaken the relationship between objective values and the phases guiding the optimization dynamics. Across all simulated problem sizes, the algorithm consistently amplifies optimal and near-optimal candidates. This work provides an explicit gate-level implementation, an improved phase-scaling method, and a foundation for hardware experiments and benchmarking against classical algorithms.

Abstract: Hermitian polynomials in the quadrature operators $x$ and $p$ are the basic ingredients of continuous-variable quantum information, and increasingly exotic ones are now routinely engineered in superconducting circuits and trapped ions. However, such a polynomial need not define unique quantum dynamics: it may define exactly one, none, or an infinite family of physically distinct ones. Such obstructions are invisible to algebraic arguments and can compromise finite-dimensional truncations, which may fail to converge or silently converge to the wrong dynamics. We develop a method, implemented as an algorithm, which decides which case occurs, and how large the family is, from the coefficients alone, with no functional-analytic input from the user. For Gaussian operators the answer is always one; for non-Gaussian ones, a regime continuous-variable quantum computing cannot do without, the other outcomes occur already for simple and physically natural Hamiltonians. We apply the algorithm to operators drawn from the recent literature.

At each feasible fringe visibility, the Helstrom-optimal distinguishabilities form at most two closed intervals, which can be separated by a gap. The intervals arise from opposite axial marker polarizations with the same fringe-amplitude modulus. Fixing the complex fringe amplitude instead gives a connected interval; for balanced original-path discrimination, a gap requires nonunital marker preparation. For a balanced pure source, relative $π$ rotations, and no subsequent marker noise, we obtain a necessary and sufficient geometric criterion for a gap under some rotation axis perpendicular to the preparation displacement. Explicit channels and a time-independent Lindblad realization show how the gap depends on preparation geometry and evolution, with exact onset and optimization formulas at fixed fractional visibility. For full-state tomography, it likewise has lower mean infidelity throughout the tested local regime. The fixed batch uses no feedback loops and far fewer settings; under the illustrative latency model, its acquisition cost is roughly three to four orders of magnitude lower than that of the adaptive methods.

Abstract: The Summers-Werner theorem shows that the vacuum of a relativistic quantum field theory violates the Bell-CHSH inequality maximally on complementary wedges, but it is an existence statement: it exhibits neither the observables nor an apparatus that measures them. Abstract: Simulation of molecular vibrations is emerging as a promising application of quantum computing in quantum chemistry. In this work, we develop new strategies for block-encoding the second-quantized $n$-mode representation of the vibrational Hamiltonian, whose algebraic structure closely parallels that of the second-quantized electronic Hamiltonian. We derive linear-combination-of-unitaries (LCU) representations of this Hamiltonian based on Pauli decomposition and tensor hypercontraction (THC) and construct efficient circuits for the corresponding block encodings. We also extend the Block-Invariant Symmetry Shift (BLISS) technique to vibrational Hamiltonians, yielding a lower LCU 1-norm and, consequently, a lower overall circuit cost. For the molecules considered here, BLISS-THC reduces the Toffoli cost of quantum phase estimation by more than two orders of magnitude relative to existing block-encoding approaches. In comparison with recent Trotter-based methods, it shows up to a ninefold reduction in the T-gate count, with the relative savings tending to increase with system size. Asymptotically, and up to polylogarithmic factors, both the Pauli and THC LCUs trade additional ancillas for a quadratic improvement in scaling with the number of modes, while THC provides an additional quadratic improvement in scaling with the number of modals. These results highlight the importance of Hamiltonian factorizations and Toffoli-qubit tradeoffs for reducing the computational cost of quantum simulations of molecular vibrations.

Abstract: Compiling classically optimized matrix product states (MPSs) into quantum circuits is a promising route to initial-state preparation for strongly correlated molecules, but the accuracy, gate cost, and classical scalability of existing MPS compilation methods have not been systematically assessed. Here, we benchmark representative analytical, variational, and hybrid MPS compilation methods for strongly correlated systems including hydrogen chains and iron-sulfur clusters. We compare state infidelity, CNOT gate count, classical runtime, and parallel scalability across different system sizes and bond dimensions. We also introduce a divide-and-conquer environment-aware compilation algorithm (DCE), which optimizes each local isometry approximation according to its contribution to the prepared-state fidelity. The results reveal complementary operating regimes: direct variational compilation methods are gate-efficient for some smaller molecular systems with $O(10)$ qubits and $O(10)$ bond dimensions or moderate accuracy targets with infidelity of order $10^{-1}$, whereas divide-and-conquer methods scale more favorably for larger systems and relatively higher bond dimensions of $O(10\text{-}10^2)$, particularly when target infidelities below $10^{-1}$ are required. Within the latter family, environment-aware optimization improves accuracy over unitary-distance-based compilation while retaining task-level parallelism. For the FeMo-cofactor (FeMoco), we find the DCE algorithm together with the MPS obtained in the entanglement-minimized orbitals (EMOs) provides a resource-efficient route to initial-state preparation. Our results provide practical guidance for selecting and developing MPS compilation methods for strongly correlated molecular simulations.

Abstract: Quantum Fisher information (QFI) determines the ultimate precision of a quantum sensor after optimization over all allowed measurements, but it does not quantify how difficult the optimal readout is to implement. Abstract: Quantum processing units (QPUs) require frequent benchmarking to track hardware drift, yet standard protocols such as randomized benchmarking are resource-intensive and compete with application workloads for scarce QPU time. We instantiate the strategy for two applications. For gate-quality verification, we show that optimally designed screening probes outperform randomized benchmarking with optimal experimental design (OED) by more than 4$\times$ in detection rate at equal resource cost. For compiler benchmarking, we formalize compiler comparison as a paired hypothesis test and derive the optimal circuit size for distinguishing compilation strategies. Despite their different observables and test statistics, both applications yield structurally the same closed-form expression for the optimal probe circuit size as a function of gate fidelity, illustrating the generality of the framework. Numerical and hardware experiments on QPUs (Rigetti Ankaa-3, IQM Garnet, IQM Emerald) confirm the theoretical predictions, with observed optimal circuit sizes matching the derived values across all devices.

Abstract: Practical fault-tolerant quantum computation requires a large number of physical qubits, posing a major challenge for scaling up individual quantum processors. Abstract: Learning with a quantum reservoir can be far simpler than simulating its dynamics. Memory orthogonality and factorization of propagated overlaps reduce each kernel evaluation to a scalar recurrence, linear in history length and independent of reservoir size. We establish self-averaging around this limit and connect the kernel coefficients to out-of-time-ordered correlators. Our analysis of finite-dimensional effects shows that the computational simplicity of the Haar kernel does not come at the expense of expressive power. Moreover, we demonstrate how the kernel can be tuned for different learning tasks by adjusting properties such as memory retention and temporal interaction order. On the NARMA10 benchmark, the tuned kernel achieves lower prediction error than the tested Ising and Sachdev--Ye--Kitaev reservoirs.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We construct an exact $n$-bit quantum adder with $O(n)$ gates and $O(\log n)$ depth that requires no ancillary qubits. We then coordinate inter-block propagation via two short-path forest operations to cut the per-block workspace requirement, yielding a nonrecursive implementation with $O(n)$ gates. Abstract: Path integral control is a stochastic optimal control framework that computes optimal policies as weighted averages over state trajectories. Motivation: 分布式量子协议需要"一个节点产生的测量结果控制另一节点的操作"，这种跨节点依赖难以在单一程序中表达并自动协调，因此需要统一的编程与编排抽象。

Result: 在七个协议工作流上评估：模拟器路径的穷尽前馈分支测试（层析完备输入集）输出态失真度 ≤5e-12，无错误经典输出，按随机拓扑序重放计划后末态不变；多执行器进程加延迟消息、集中式态模拟服务下全计划运行均正确，移除必需的跨节点依赖则协议失败；下沉线路与手写实现在双比特门和条件块计数上持平；两台 IBM Heron 处理器上的同芯片（虚拟 QPU 分区）运行跨一周两次会话成功，但会话间差异大、损耗超出标定噪声模型预测，相干性敏感检验未能确立相干 Toffoli 操作或目标重复码叠加态的保持。

Conclusion: 结果支持 QQLink 工作流语义在已测试执行路径上的正确性，同时揭示当前硬件实现的局限；跨物理联网 QPU 的执行留待未来工作。

Abstract: Distributed quantum protocols require measurement outcomes produced at one node to control operations at another. We present QQLink, a programming and orchestration layer that expresses these dependencies in a single program, using workflow nodes, deferred measurement references, and declared entanglement, and derives the cross-node coordination automatically. Its planner constructs a provably acyclic dependency graph in time linear in the total size of the instructions' qubit footprints. The same programs execute through simulator backends or are lowered to dynamic circuits on partitioned quantum hardware. We evaluate seven protocol workflows. Exhaustive feedforward-branch tests on the simulator runtime path, over tomographically complete input sets, yield output-state infidelities of at most $5\times10^{-12}$ and no incorrect classical outputs, and replaying every plan in random topological orders leaves each final state unchanged. Separate executor processes run the plans with delayed messages, with the joint quantum state held by a centralized simulation service. Every run with the full plan is correct, and removing required cross-node dependencies causes protocol failures. The lowered circuits match hand-written implementations of the same construction in two-qubit gate and conditional-block counts. Runs on two IBM Heron processors, each partitioned into virtual QPUs, demonstrate same-chip execution in two sessions a week apart. The runs also show large variation between sessions and losses beyond what calibration-derived noise models predict. Coherence-sensitive checks do not establish coherent Toffoli operation or preservation of the intended repetition-code superposition. These results support QQLink's workflow semantics on the tested execution paths and identify limits of the current hardware realization; execution across physically networked QPUs remains future work.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子中继器控制器需在相邻链路生成时可靠估计存储纠缠。固定指数寿命无法刻画非马尔可夫复兴，而微观族已知时复杂替代模型又属多余。

Result: 匹配的单赝模情形下，正确设定的一赝模拟合最优。在族不确定的支持案例中，QMemTwin相对两赝模模型将布居RMSE和Bell保真度RMSE显著降低（Holm校正后仍显著）。灵活被动模型对未知族平均误差略低但与QMemTwin统计上不可区分，且在匹配情形精度下降。时序留出闸门可检测结构突变并控制虚警，AUROC良好。8链路中混合队列无显著goodput增益，而预先声明的高回流压力队列相对两赝模模型获得显著8链路goodput增益。

Conclusion: 结果支持：族已知时用已知族，否则应考虑模型不确定性，并对不支持的动态弃权。

Abstract: Quantum-repeater controllers need reliable estimates of stored entanglement as adjacent links are generated. Fixed exponential lifetimes miss non-Markovian revivals, while complex surrogates are unnecessary when the microscopic family is known. We present QMemTwin, a physics-constrained digital twin that accounts for model uncertainty. It calibrates a bank of contractive passive state-space models, combines their amplitudes with finite-sample-corrected Akaike weights, and abstains when temporal-holdout data indicate unsupported dynamics. Passivity guarantees a completely positive, trace-preserving amplitude-damping channel for each candidate and their convex amplitude average. We evaluate [N cases] synthetic memories: [N supported] supported passive time-invariant cases and [N structural] nonstationary structural-shift cases. In the matched one-pseudomode regime, the correctly specified one-pseudomode fit performs best. Across [N unknown] supported cases with uncertain families, QMemTwin reduces population RMSE by [population reduction]% and Bell-fidelity RMSE by [Bell reduction]% relative to the two-pseudomode model; both paired effects remain significant after Holm correction. The flexible passive model has a slightly lower mean error for unknown families but is statistically indistinguishable from QMemTwin and loses accuracy in the matched regime. The temporal-holdout gate detects [detection]% of structural shifts, with [false alarm]% false alarms on supported families and AUROC [AUC]. In 2-, 4-, and 8-link repeater chains, the mixed cohort shows no significant 8-link goodput gain. A predeclared high-backflow stress cohort yields an 8-link goodput gain of [goodput gain]% over the two-pseudomode model, with paired p = [p value]. These results favor using a known family when available, accounting for model uncertainty otherwise and abstaining on unsupported dynamics.

</details>


### [57] [Quantum Anomaly Detection in network traffic using Variational Quantum Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34635)
*Mir H M Ilham,Hardique Dasore,Abdulrahman Ababneh,Hafidz A Kabira,Samyam Shrestha,Jeyasri Sundar,Shah Haque,Eric Howard*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究评估四种变分量子分类器（VQC）架构在网络流量异常检测中的表现，发现电路表达力与优化稳定性之间存在权衡，经典模型（SVM、随机森林）在合成数据上仍优于VQC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络威胁态势日益复杂，经典机器学习模型虽有效，但在处理高维数据时面临计算瓶颈，因此探索量子机器学习（QML）用于网络流量异常检测的潜力。

Method: 生成合成网络流量数据集；构建四种VQC架构，系统性地改变特征编码方法、参数化ansatz电路、量子神经网络类型和经典优化器；并与SVM、随机森林等经典模型进行对比分析。

Result: 更深电路（VQC-4）准确率最高达89.0%，但对训练条件敏感；较浅的VQC-1性能不足但优化曲线稳定；VQC-2存在类别偏差，产生大量假阴性；最优模型VQC-3采用ZFeatureMap+RealAmplitudes ansatz并以ADAM优化。经典模型在该合成数据上实现完美分类，优于所有VQC。

Conclusion: 在类别分离较简单的结构化数据上，经典算法仍占主导；但部分VQC配置展现出的决策边界学习能力，预示其在更高维、更高噪声问题中的潜在应用价值。

Abstract: The increasingly complex cybersecurity landscape requires more advanced network anomaly detection systems. Although, classical machine learning models are known to be effective, they reach computational bottlenecks when processing high-dimensional data. This study investigates the potential of Quantum Machine Learning (QML) for network traffic anomaly detection by evaluating four distinct Variational Quantum Classifier (VQC) architectures. A synthetic network traffic dataset is generated for the evaluations. The VQC models systematically varied feature encoding methods, parameterized ansatz circuits, quantum neural network types, and classical optimizers. Experimental results show that a trade-off exists between circuit expressivity and optimization stability. Deeper circuits (VQC-4) achieved higher accuracy of upto 89.0\%, while simultaneously being sensitive to training conditions. A shallower variant (VQC-1) under performed, although it had consistent and stable optimization curves. Class-dependent biases were also observed such as in VQC-2, where the model struggled to find an effective decision boundary, and produced a large number of false negatives. The best performing model in this study (VQC-3) combined ZFeatureMap with RealAmplitudes ansatz, optimized using ADAM. Comparative analysis against classical machine learning models such as Support Vector Machine and Random Forest reveal a performance gap, where classical models are able to achieve perfect classification performance on the synthetic data. It is concluded that classical algorithms remain dominant in their ability to classify structured data with relatively simple class separations. However, due to the observed potential of certain VQC configurations to learn decision boundaries to classify data, their utility for future applications in noisier, higher-dimensional problems is highlighted.

</details>


### [58] [Neutrinos as Quantum Information Carriers? A Gap Analysis Against the DiVincenzo Criteria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34666)
*Ramanuj Awasthi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该短文以DiVincenzo七项判据为框架，对中微子作为量子信息平台进行差距分析，结论是中微子应被视为优异的"飞行型"信息载体，但作为计算平台在多个判据上失败或表现很差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子、离子、中性原子、超导电路已是成熟量子信息平台，而中微子具有独特优势：可在数千公里尺度上展现量子干涉（味振荡），且相互作用极弱，能近乎无阻地穿越地球。作者希望系统评估中微子是否可作为量子信息平台，并明确其能力边界与瓶颈，为后续研究指明方向。

Method: 采用文献综述式的"差距分析"（gap analysis）：从已有文献中提取中微子物理的相关特性，逐条对照DiVincenzo七项判据（五项与计算相关、两项与通信相关），逐项分析其满足程度与差距所在。

Result: 中微子具备高保真度和良好的相干性；但缺乏明确的量子比特定义与初始化方案，缺乏通用量子门、单量子比特读出，以及静止—飞行态转换能力，这些方面或直接失败或表现很差。

Conclusion: 中微子最适合被视为一种卓越的"飞行型"信息载体，但极难操控；不宜作为通用量子计算平台。作者梳理了全部差距，可用于指导未来工作。

Abstract: Photons, trapped ions, neutral atoms, and superconducting circuits are all well-known quantum information platforms. Neutrinos offer a unique possibility, displaying quantum interference (flavour oscillation) over thousands of kilometres, yet interacting so weakly that they can travel through the Earth with minimal interaction. This short paper is a gap analysis. We use information from the existing literature to compare neutrinos to the seven DiVincenzo requirements. Firstly, there are five requirements to do with computation and secondly two requirements to do with communication. We analyse where the gaps are for each requirement. Overall, we find that neutrinos have high fidelity and good coherence; however, they do not have a clear qubit definition and initialisation. They do not have universal gates, individual qubit readout, or stationary-to-flying conversion, meaning they either fail or are very poor. Therefore, we think they are best considered as an exceptional flying information carrier; however, they are very difficult to control. We have outlined all of the gaps which can be used to inform future work.

</details>


### [59] [Rank-one Factored Gradient Descent without Restricted Isometry: Application to Quantum Pure State Tomography on Multi-Qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34696)
*Takahiro Kajisa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 针对量子态层析中低重量Pauli测量下RIP失效的问题，作者将交换观测按setting分组并转化为各setting的概率分布，提出max-sliced全变差损失，证明在最大值唯一的分层上无伪局部极小，且仅需测量映射在一般纯态上单射即可保证全局最优解为真实态（相差相位），并在4量子比特上数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FGD是低秩半正定矩阵恢复的可扩展非凸方法，但现有良性损失景观理论多依赖测量映射的限制等距性质（RIP）。在量子态层析应用中，受探测效率与漂移限制，可稳定统计获取的测量仅限于低重量Pauli可观测量；论文首先指出在此类部分重量Pauli测量下RIP以最坏方式失效，因而原有理论保证不再适用，需要新的损失设计与理论分析。

Method: 按setting将相互对易的可观测量打包，把量子态前推为每个setting下的概率分布，构造max-sliced全变差（total-variation）损失；在达到最大值的setting唯一的分层（stratum）上分析损失景观。

Result: 在该唯一最大值分层上不存在伪局部极小；并且仅要求测量映射在一般（generic）纯态上为单射，全局最小值即为真实态（至多相差一个相位）。在4量子比特系统上进行数值实验，并与使用普通损失的FGD方法进行比较以验证结论。

Conclusion: 低重量Pauli测量使RIP严重失效，基于RIP的既有FGD理论不适用；所提出的max-sliced全变差损失配合FGD框架可在弱得多的单射性假设下避免伪局部极小并恢复真实态，数值实验支持该理论并显示其相对普通损失的优势。

Abstract: Factored gradient descent (FGD) is a scalable nonconvex method for low-rank positive semidefinite matrix recovery, in which the rank and positivity constraints are satisfied automatically by the factorization. Most of the existing theory that certifies benignity of its loss landscape, however, relies on a restricted isometry property (RIP) of the measurement map. In quantum state tomography, one of its applications, the measurements that can be acquired with stable statistics are limited by detection efficiency and drift to Pauli observables of low weight; we first show that under such partial-weight Pauli measurements RIP fails in the worst possible way. We then bundle the mutually commuting observables setting by setting, pushing the state forward to the probability distribution of each setting, and introduce a max-sliced total-variation loss. On the stratum where the setting attaining the maximum is unique there is no spurious local minimum, and provided only that the measurement map is injective on generic pure states, the global minimum is the truth up to a phase. Finally we verify these claims numerically on four qubits and compare against FGD with the ordinary loss.

</details>

*Su-Yong Lee,Yonggi Jo,Jihwan Kim,Zaeill Kim,Yong Sup Ihn,Duk Y. Kim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究热态经分束器产生的双模经典关联特性，厘清经典与量子关联的界限，并证明经典关联态（尤其非对称情形）在目标检测中优于量子关联态。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要明确高斯体系中双模经典关联的本质，并探索其在目标检测等量子增强协议中的作用，特别是区分经典与量子关联的判据及其实际优势。

Method: 分析热态输入分束器所产生双模态的经典关联；显式研究对其中一个模进行测量如何影响另一模，以此区分经典关联与量子关联；在开-关探测下，比较非对称经典关联态（信号模与闲置模平均光子数不同）与量子关联态在目标检测中的性能，并考察局域高斯操作的作用。

Conclusion: 经典关联（尤其是非对称形式）在高斯目标检测中可作为有效资源，并在热噪声环境下超越量子关联态；局域高斯操作具有额外增益。

Abstract: We study the characteristics of two-mode classical correlation in Gaussian regime, which is produced by impinging a thermal state into a beam splitter. It is explicitly investigated how a measurement on one mode affects the other. The result enables the distinction between classical and quantum correlations. Then, we show how classically correlated states are advantageous in target detection, specifically for an asymmetric classical correlation with different mean photon numbers in the signal and idler modes. Under on-off detection, the classically correlated states significantly outperform quantum correlated states as the environmental thermal mean photon number increases. Furthermore, we demonstrate the advantage of local Gaussian operations in the target detection.

</details>


### [61] [Quantum ordinal selection with two comparisons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34762)
*Hongyu Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究至多两次无噪声相干成对比较下、在给定排名阈值内选中目标的量子最优成功概率，建立刻画可达成功的比较相关半定规划模型，并用计算机辅助证明给出精确最优值与显式算法族的近最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅用多项式次数界会高估两次比较所能达到的成功率，因此需要一个能精确刻画两次比较可达成功概率的模型，以确定各排名阈值下的真实最优值及其相较经典算法的量子优势。

Method: 构建比较相关（comparison-specific）的半定规划（SDP）模型，完全刻画两次相干成对比较的可达成功概率；结合计算机辅助证明求精确最优解；对最小选择问题构造显式算法族，并推导所有可行两比较算法的界以评估其渐近最优性。

Result: 对七个对象的所有排名阈值以及八个对象的最小选择情形给出精确最优值；显式算法族的渐近成功概率与无约束最优值相差不超过0.016%，并证明该相对差距的极限严格为正；最小选择的量子-经典最优成功比在两次比较预算下收敛到约2.48。

Conclusion: 半定规划模型可精确刻画两次比较的可达成功概率，显式算法族在渐近上几乎达到无约束最优，且在最小选择中两次比较即可带来约2.48倍的量子优势。

Abstract: We study the optimal probability of selecting an object within a prescribed rank threshold using at most two noiseless coherent pairwise comparisons of an unknown strict order. Polynomial degree bounds alone can overestimate the success attainable with two comparisons. We develop a comparison-specific semidefinite model that completely characterizes attainable success. Computer-assisted proofs establish the exact optimum for every rank threshold at seven objects and for minimum selection at eight. For minimum selection, we construct an explicit algorithm family and determine its exact optimum. Bounds for all admissible two-comparison algorithms show that the family's asymptotic success is within 0.016% of the unrestricted optimum. We also prove that this relative gap has a strictly positive limit. The optimal quantum-to-classical success ratio for minimum selection converges to approximately 2.48 under the same two-comparison budget.

</details>


### [62] [Measure of set coherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34784)
*Jiabo Panel,Yu Guo,Yuqin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立集合相干性（set coherence）的公理化框架：证明自由操作为共同保交换量子信道，提出SCM公理体系，检验并分类基于鲁棒性与对易子的度量，并讨论其在最优近似量子广播中的操作意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集合相干性刻画一组量子态能否在同一基下同时非相干，是单一态相干性的自然推广，但此前缺乏统一的公理化处理：自由操作未明确界定，已有度量（平均/最大鲁棒性）未系统检验其公理满足性，度量间的强弱关系与操作意义也未澄清。

Method: 1) 识别集合相干性的自由操作为共同保交换量子信道，据此建立集合相干性度量（SCM）的公理框架；2) 在共同参考基下，分别取单态鲁棒性的平均、最大与求和构造鲁棒型度量，逐一检验其是否满足SCM公理及统一性、完备性、强超可加性；3) 基于两两对易子构造多类SCM，如Schatten p-范数型度量；4) 检验文献中的普适四阶Bargmann间隙度量；5) 在最优近似量子广播任务中考察SCM的操作意义。

Result: 平均鲁棒性是SCM但不统一、不完备；最大鲁棒性是统一SCM，但不完备且不强超可加；求和鲁棒性是完备SCM。Schatten p-范数型SCM完备且强超可加；普适四阶Bargmann间隙度量也是完备且强超可加的集合相干性量化器。此外，集合相干性量能紧密反映最优近似量子广播的性能。

Conclusion: 集合相干性可在共同保交换信道的自由操作下获得一致的公理化刻画；不同鲁棒型与对易子型度量在统一性、完备性与强超可加性上各有取舍，求和鲁棒性与Schatten p-范数型、Bargmann间隙度量等具有更强性质；SCM可定量刻画近似量子广播任务的表现。

Abstract: Set coherence is a quantum resource for a set of states that characterizes whether a collection of quantum states contains coherence, namely whether these states fail to be simultaneously incoherent in a common basis. In this work, we show that the free operations of set coherence are common commutativity-preserving quantum channels, from which we then establish an axiomatic framework for set-coherence measure (SCM). Within this framework, we investigate the robustness based measures deduced by taking, respectively, the average, maximum, and sum of the single-state robustnesses over a common reference basis, where the first two were previously introduced in the literature and we check here whether they satisfy our SCM axioms. Consequently, we show that the averaged robustness is an SCM but it is neither unified nor complete, the maximal robustness is a unified SCM but is neither complete nor strongly superadditive, whereas the sum robustness is a complete SCM. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画 stote（state over time，配对态 $\rho$ 与信道 $\mathcal{E}$ 得到的 Hermitian 算子 $(\rho\otimes\mathbf{1})\star J_\mathcal{E}$）所生成的凸锥 $\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}$ 是文献 [HoogstederCalsamigliaWinter2025] 提出的公开问题；理解其几何结构与计算复杂性对量子信息中的联合可表示性判据至关重要。

Conclusion: $\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}$ 的复杂性与其 separable cone 子结构直接相关：其成员性问题本质上不弱于量子可分性判定，因而计算上不可行；并且在上述维数条件下它超出有限 SDP 可描述的范围，说明仅靠 SDP 层次无法精确刻画该锥。

Abstract: A state over time (stote) pairs a state $ρ$ with a channel $\mathcal{E}$ as the Hermitian, generally non-positive operator $(ρ\otimes\mathbf{1})\star J_\mathcal{E}$. Characterizing the convex cone $\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}$ these operators generate was posed as an open problem in~\cite{HoogstederCalsamigliaWinter2025}. We exhibit a linear copy of the bipartite separable cone as an exposed face of $\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}$: for output systems of the form $B = A'R$ with $A'$ a copy of the input, there is an explicit witness $W_0\in\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}^*$ and an explicit injective linear map $L$ with $σ\in \mathrm{SEP}_{+}(A{:}R)\iff L(σ)\in\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}(A{:}A'R)$. Composing with the NP-hardness of separability shows that exact membership, weak membership, and weak optimization are NP-hard for $\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}$ and its dual. A quantitative stability bound makes the weak-membership reduction valid at inverse-polynomial accuracy. Thus polynomial-time membership tests do not exist unless $\mathrm{P}=\mathrm{NP}$. Independently of this complexity assumption, Fawzi's theorem implies that $\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}(A{:}A'R)$ admits no finite semidefinite representation when $d=\dim A\ge2$, $r=\dim R\ge2$, and $d+r>5$.

</details>

Abstract: Characterizing nitrogen-vacancy (NV) ensembles requires connecting their collective spectra to individual centers that experience different local environments. We develop a symbolic-regression pipeline that carries single-center spin structure into a calibrated ensemble model by generating individual spectral responses and combining them with explicit weights. A constrained search selects Hermitian operator terms from single-center optically detected magnetic resonance spectra, fits their coefficients, and retains the selected expressions as source models for the ensemble. Ensemble calibration then accounts for resonance shifts, local disorder, orientation, and optical response while keeping the individual spectral contributions accessible. On public single-center and ensemble data, the zero-field ensemble held-frequency root-mean-square error decreases from 0.008835 to 0.003872 in normalized photoluminescence, a 56.2% improvement over matched uncorrected composition. In controlled QuTiP ensembles of 128 sites spanning four NV orientations, models calibrated at 0 and 3 mT predict an independently held-out 5.17 mT spectrum with errors of 0.000207-0.000228 across three random seeds, comparable to the 0.000200 noise standard deviation. Resolving the simulated response into its individual-center contributions shows how local spectral diversity combines into the ensemble line shape. By retaining the source Hamiltonians while calibrating sample-dependent spectral differences, the pipeline connects local spin physics to collective optical readout and makes the contributions to an ensemble line shape available for physical interpretation.

</details>


### [69] [Self-Correcting 3D Surface Code with Growing Local Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35099)
*Jinkang Guo*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自纠错量子存储通常需要在几何局域性、平移不变性与热动力学简单性之间做妥协；本文旨在探索"增长局域Hilbert空间维数"这一新资源，在不改变标准三维表面码简单局域几何和局域热动力学的前提下获得随系统尺寸增长的纠错存储时间。

Method: 在$\mathbb Z_q$上构造三维表面码，$q$随系统尺寸增长；保留普通三维表面码的局域几何、稳定子支撑与体内平移不变性，仅改变稳定子的Pauli幂次。采用Lee度量刻画误差权重，并考虑单qudit $X^{\pm1}$与$Z^{\pm1}$更新的局域Metropolis动力学，每次更新能量变化至多为一与$q$和系统尺寸$N$无关的常数。

Result: 证明在临界温度以下、局域维数按$q=\Theta(N)$标度时，两个误差扇区的存储时间均至少以$\exp[\Omega(N^{1/3})]$增长；数值模拟结果进一步支持上述解析结论。

Conclusion: 增长的局域Hilbert空间维数可作为设计自纠错量子存储的新资源，且无需牺牲简单的局域几何与局域热动力学。

Abstract: We construct self-correcting 3D surface codes over $\mathbb Z_q$ with local dimension $q$ growing with system size. The model preserves the local geometry and stabilizer support of the ordinary 3D surface code and remains translation invariant in the bulk, while modifying only the stabilizer Pauli powers. We use the Lee metric to characterize error weight and consider local Metropolis dynamics with single-qudit $X^{\pm1}$ and $Z^{\pm1}$ updates, each changing the energy by at most a constant independent of $q$ and system size $N$. We prove that, below the critical temperature, when the local dimension scales as $q=Θ(N)$, the memory times in both error sectors grow at least as $\exp\left[Ω(N^{1/3})\right]$. Our numerical results further support these analytical results. Together, these results establish growing local Hilbert-space dimension as a new resource for designing self-correcting quantum memories while retaining simple local geometry and local thermal dynamics.

</details>


### [70] [Multinomial shot sampling for quantum circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35119)
*Ijaz Ahamed Mohammad,Daniel Nagaj,Martin Plesch*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出用多项分布+条件二项（BTPE）抽样替代逐次shot生成，使量子线路模拟中的测量计数抽样代价与shot数S无关，在最坏测试配置下提速超10^3倍且统计上不可区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Qiskit、PennyLane等经典模拟器中，即使已获得完整态矢量，从末态采样shot仍逐次生成和处理，代价随shot数S线性增长；当只需测量计数（counts）而非逐次结果时，这种逐shot开销是不必要的。

Method: 将counts建模为多项分布抽样，并分解为一串条件二项抽样，每次用BTPE算法在期望常数时间内完成；据此实现抽样流程，并对概率集中于少数结果的态进一步缩短计算。

Conclusion: 当模拟只需测量计数时，采用多项分布/条件二项抽样可消除对S的线性依赖，在保持统计正确性的同时带来数量级加速，可作为现有模拟器采样原语的直接替代。

Abstract: In classical simulations of quantum circuits, sampling shots (measurement outcomes) from the final state is a routine but costly step. In widely used software, including Qiskit and PennyLane, its cost grows linearly with the number of shots $S$, even when the simulation has already produced the full state vector, because every shot is generated and processed individually. If only the measurement counts are needed, this is unnecessary. The counts can be drawn directly from a multinomial distribution, decomposed into a sequence of conditional binomial draws, each performed in expected constant time by the BTPE algorithm. The sampling cost is then independent of $S$. We describe an implementation of this approach, which also shortens the computation for states whose probability is concentrated on a few outcomes, and compare it with Qiskit's sampling primitives for up to 20 qubits and $10^6$ shots. Our implementation is faster in every configuration tested, by a factor that grows with the number of shots and exceeds $10^3$ in most configurations at $S = 10^6$, and its samples are statistically indistinguishable from those of the reference implementations.

</details>


### [71] [Comparing classical and quantum QUBO solvers: optimality, trajectory diagnostics, and limits of solver selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35126)

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QUBO求解器比较中，“找到好解”与“证明最优”常被混为一谈；同时张量网络与变分量子方法的表现缺乏可解释的诊断指标，也缺少对量子方法是否优于经典的严格检验。

Method: 主基准上运行通用分支割求解器（600 s上限）作参照；将已知最优解随机置换成随机比特串以改变搜索难度（n=48时对比并行回火PT，60 s预算）；引入结构与轨迹诊断（树width上界预测轨迹键维需求，Spearman ρ）；通过受控实验定位暖启动DMRG扫描中的激发态滞留并用重启缓解；另设Wishart随机实例的规模扩展研究。

Result: 分支割在主基准上匹配全部参考能量并在600 s内认证176个实例；但最优解随机化后n=48仅找到4/10个最优且无一认证，而PT在60 s内每次运行均命中全部最优。树width界对键维需求的样本内/外Spearman为0.78/0.73，但仅能粗略估计运行时间。LR-QAOA相对经典基线无解质量提升；Wishart更难设置下，n≥72经典方法在给定预算内均未达最优。

Conclusion: 诊断工具可解释部分性能差异但未能导出经校验的求解器选择规则；测试的LR-QAOA未显示优势，Wishart结果不构成量子优势证据，但界定了值得进一步研究的区间。

Abstract: Comparing solvers for quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) requires distinguishing solution quality from optimality certification. We compare classical, tensor-network, and fixed-schedule quantum approaches on 180 benchmark instances from ten families with 14-48 variables, supplemented by harder synthetic instances with known optimal solutions, extending to 128 variables. On the main benchmark, a general-purpose branch-and-cut solver matches all reference energies and certifies 176 instances within a 600,s cap. When the known optima are moved to randomly chosen bitstrings, the search comparison changes: at n = 48 the branch-and-cut solver finds only four of ten optima and certifies none, whereas parallel tempering reaches every optimum in every tested run at a 60,s budget. Structural and trajectory diagnostics explain aspects of tensor-network performance. A treewidth bound ranks trajectory bond-dimension demand at Spearman $ρ= 0.78$ in sample and $0.73$ out of sample, but provides only a coarse runtime estimate. Controlled experiments identify excited-state trapping during warm-started density-matrix renormalization group (DMRG) sweeps and demonstrate mitigation by restarting the variational search. These diagnostics do not yield a validated solver-selection rule. The tested linear-ramp quantum approximate optimization algorithm (LR-QAOA) runs offer no solution-quality improvement over the classical comparators. In the separate Wishart scaling study, on the harder setting, none of the classical methods tested reaches the optimum at the sampled sizes from n = 72 onward within the tested budgets. While this does not provide evidence for quantum advantage, it identifies a domain for further investigation.

</details>


### [72] [Complete characterization of internal-external entanglement trade-offs in two-mode Gaussian states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35183)
*Yasusada Nambu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 刻画了固定局部辛本征值下非对称双模高斯态中"内部纠缠（模间negativity）"与"外部纠缠（跨高斯纯化的negativity）"之间的精确权衡关系，给出解析边界及可达性构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内部与外部纠缠在量子信息中此消彼长，但此前缺乏对非对称双模高斯态在固定局部辛本征值条件下二者精确权衡边界的解析刻画。

Method: 以negativity N_{A:B} 度量内部纠缠、以跨高斯纯化的 N_{AB:CD} 度量外部纠缠；先通过数值采样推测 (N_{AB:CD}, N_{A:B}) 平面可达区域边界结构，再对全局辛谱参数化解析求解；在固定 N_{AB:CD} 下给出剩余谱参数的闭式容许区间，并构造性证明区间内每点对应物理协方差矩阵。

Result: 得到可达区域的解析锐利上下界，证明两端之间所有中间 N_{A:B} 值均可实现；上界由等幅反号关联取到，下界正支在一个全局辛本征值等于1时出现；另识别两个外部纠缠阈值，导出饱和的monogamy型不等式，并退化回对称态极限。

Conclusion: 非对称双模高斯态中内部与外部纠缠存在可精确解析刻画的连续可达权衡区间，边界可饱和且与monogamy约束一致，统一涵盖对称态情形。

Abstract: We characterize the exact trade-off between internal and external entanglement in asymmetric two-mode Gaussian states at fixed local symplectic eigenvalues. Internal entanglement is quantified by the negativity $\mathcal{N}_{A:B}$ between the two modes, while external entanglement is quantified by the negativity $\mathcal{N}_{AB:CD}$ across a Gaussian purification. Numerical sampling suggests the boundary structure of the attainable region in the $(\mathcal{N}_{AB:CD},\mathcal{N}_{A:B})$ plane, which we then determine analytically. Parametrizing the global symplectic spectrum yields a closed admissible interval for the remaining spectral parameter at fixed $\mathcal{N}_{AB:CD}$. We prove constructively that every point in this interval corresponds to a physical covariance matrix, implying that all intermediate values of $\mathcal{N}_{A:B}$ between the resulting sharp bounds are achievable. The upper bound is attained for opposite-sign correlations of equal magnitude, while the positive branch of the lower bound occurs when one global symplectic eigenvalue equals unity. We also identify two external-entanglement thresholds, derive a saturated monogamy-type inequality, and recover the symmetric-state limit.

</details>

*Lucas Weitzel*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模cQED中量子发射体可与多个共振场模耦合，实验上已可观测其独特特征；但损耗腔中常用的有效离散模方法在超过两个模式时的准确性鲜有基准检验，本文旨在检验其必要性与可靠性。

Method: 采用Weitzel等（2026, arXiv:2512.18478）提出的共振量子化方案，模拟一维损耗谐振腔中单个原子的动力学，并系统研究其对光-物质耦合强度与原子位置两个参数的依赖。

Result: 捕获原子动力学关键特征所需的最小模式数随耦合强度增大而增加；多模区制下原子动力学对位置表现出强依赖性。文中对各结果给出物理解释，并讨论了该形式体系用于提取物理相关量的方式。

Conclusion: 有效离散多模方法在多模区制的损耗腔中不可或缺，两模近似不足以描述；模式截断数需依据耦合强度选取，且原子位置是多模效应的关键控制参数。

Abstract: In the multimode regime of cavity quantum electrodynamics (cQED), light-matter coupling can be strong enough such as to allows a quantum emitter to couple with multiple resonant field modes. This regime leaves distinctive signatures in the behavior of both the field and the emitter, which are already accessible by modern experimental techniques. To model such effects in lossy cavities, effective discrete-mode approaches have been widely employed, despite their accuracy beyond two modes remaining rarely benchmarked. In this work, we demonstrate the necessity of employing multiple modes to describe the multimode signatures by analyzing the dynamics of an atom in a one-dimensional lossy resonator. The atom's dynamics is simulated using the formalism developed in Weitzel et al. (2026) (arXiv:2512.18478), where a resonance quantization prescription is provided. We investigate its dependency with respect to two different parameters: the light-matter coupling strength and the atom's position. In the former case, we find that the minimal number of modes necessary to capture the essential features of the atom's dynamics increases with the coupling strength. In the latter, we further observe that the multimode regime leads to a strong position-dependency of the atom's dynamics. In all cases, we discuss the physical interpretation of our results and further discuss about how the employed formalism can be used to recover physically relevant quantities.

</details>


### [74] [Basis-set error bounds for first-quantised quantum simulation of chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35205)
*Dominic W. Berry,Arkin Tikku,Sophia Simon,Ryan Babbush*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文严格证明了量子模拟电子结构时，基组离散化误差在地基能量、时间演化可观测量及动能等情形下按 𝒪(N⁻¹) 缩放（动能带对数因子），涵盖平面波与空间网格（周期及开放边界）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子模拟中普遍预期地基能量收敛率为 𝒪(N⁻¹)，但此前仅有解析与数值观察，缺乏对量子算法所关注量的严格误差处理，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 对平面波基与空间网格基进行严格误差分析：网格情形分别处理周期体系与开放边界非周期体系；时间演化方面分别界定态误差与可观测量误差，引入平滑位置可观测量，并对非周期网格施加"真实演化无明显边界泄漏"的条件。

Result: 地基能量误差为 𝒪(N⁻¹)（平面波与网格基）；态误差弱于 𝒪(N⁻¹)，但可观测量误差为 Õ(N⁻¹)；平滑位置型可观测量的误差为 𝒪(N⁻⁵ᐟ³+tN⁻¹)；动能误差为 𝒪((t+log N)/N)。

Conclusion: 𝒪(N⁻¹) 缩放对量子算法众多相关情形成立，动能仅多一平凡对数因子；结果为量子化学模拟的基组选择与资源估计提供严格理论依据。

Abstract: In quantum simulation of electronic structure it is often expected that the estimate of the ground energy converges to the continuum basis limit at a rate of $\mathcal O(N^{-1})$ for a basis of size $N$. This has been found in analytical and numerical studies, but without a rigorous treatment of the error in the quantities considered in quantum algorithms. Here we show the $\mathcal O(N^{-1})$ scaling of the error for a wide range of cases needed for quantum simulation of chemistry. We show the error in the ground energy scales as $\mathcal O(N^{-1})$ for plane-wave as well as spatial grid bases. For the spatial grid we consider both a periodic system and a non-periodic system with open boundaries. For time evolution, our bound on the error in the state itself is weaker than $\mathcal O(N^{-1})$, but we show that the error in observables scales as $\widetilde{\mathcal O}(N^{-1})$ in important cases. In particular, we may consider a smoothed observable that describes the position of particles. We show that this error scales as $\mathcal O(N^{-5/3}+tN^{-1})$ for time $t$, again both for plane-wave and spatial grid bases. For the non-periodic grid case we use a condition that the true evolution would not result in significant leakage past the boundaries, which introduces basis error unrelated to the resolution of the discretisation. We also show the error in the kinetic energy of a particle scales as $\mathcal O((t+\log N)/N)$ for the plane-wave and grid bases. Together, these results show that $\mathcal O(N^{-1})$ scaling of the error holds for many of the relevant cases for quantum algorithms (with a trivial log factor for kinetic energy).

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向容错通用超导量子计算需数百万物理比特，传统"专用控制线"范式在布线密度与稀释制冷机制冷功率上不可扩展；尽管微波频分复用（FDM）已在经典通信中成功，但其在超导量子控制中的移植尚缺关键环节——两比特门仍主要依赖单极性脉冲驱动，是全微波架构中缺失的一环。

Method: 构建全微波、可周期性扩展的混合复用架构：采用多色（polychromatic）微波脉冲结合带通滤波器，对全部门操作实施频分复用；与片上DC源配合完成完整复用控制模块；以1:4复用比例开展实验，测量并行FDM CZ门与并行FDM XY(π/2)门的平均误差，并统计检验复用引起的性能退化。

Result: 1:4演示中，并行FDM CZ门平均误差为(6.4±0.2)×10⁻³，并行FDM XY(π/2)门平均误差为(10.6±0.2)×10⁻⁴；统计上未观察到显著的复用诱导性能退化；控制线数量可减少1–2个数量级。

Conclusion: 该FDM门控制方案补齐了可扩展复用控制架构中最后一个缺失模块，与片上DC源共同构成向通用容错量子计算迈进的全微波复用控制路径。

Abstract: Universal fault-tolerant superconducting quantum computers are expected to require millions of physical qubits; yet extending the prevailing dedicated-control-line paradigm to this scale would exceed the wiring density and cooling-power budget of dilution refrigerators. Microwave frequency-division multiplexing (FDM) revolutionized classical communications by enabling many independent channels to share common transmission infrastructure. Translating this principle to superconducting quantum control, however, requires an all-microwave architecture and experimental validation of frequency-multiplexed gate control. Recent work in quantum error correction has demonstrated microwave-based readout, single-qubit gates and leakage suppression, whereas two-qubit gates remain predominantly driven by unipolar pulses and thus constitute the missing element of an all-microwave architecture. Here, we propose a periodically extensible hybrid multiplexing framework in which all gate operations are frequency-division multiplexed using polychromatic microwave pulses and band-pass filters, thereby reducing the required control-line count by one to two orders of magnitude. In a 1:4 demonstration, we achieve average errors of $(6.4\pm0.2)\times10^{-3}$ for parallel FDM CZ gates and $(10.6\pm0.2)\times10^{-4}$ for parallel FDM $XY(π/2)$ gates, without statistically significant multiplexing-induced degradation. Together with on-chip DC sources, this FDM-based gate-control scheme completes the last missing module in a scalable multiplexed-control architecture toward universal fault-tolerant quantum computing.

</details>

Method: 构建混合量子-经典Benders分解框架：引入基于逻辑的冲突割（conflict cuts），几乎不需辅助量子比特即可替代编码可行性割；同时提出稀疏递归SoC建模，使约束矩阵密度随调度周期T降低约T/3倍。

Conclusion: 逻辑冲突割与稀疏递归SoC公式有效缓解了量子资源开销与模型稠密化问题，使量子-经典混合Benders框架在V2G车队优化中具备可行性与扩展潜力，但硬件级验证目前仍限于小规模概念验证。

Abstract: The integration of bidirectional (V2G) charging allows electric vehicle (EV) fleets to operate as distributed energy assets. However, the joint optimization of charging schedules and trip assignments is NP-hard. Moreover, bidirectional power flows create non-monotonic state-of-charge (SoC) dynamics that weaken standard dual-based Benders cuts. We propose a hybrid quantum-classical Benders decomposition framework. To overcome the qubit overhead associated with encoding feasibility cuts, we introduce logic-based conflict cuts, which require near-zero auxiliary qubits. Furthermore, we develop a sparse recursive SoC formulation that reduces the constraint-matrix density by a factor of T/3 in the horizon length T. We demonstrate that our framework recovers MILP-optimal solutions on benchmark instances with a low auxiliary-qubit budget, and, as a minimal proof of concept, we execute a small instance end-to-end on IBM quantum hardware.

</details>

Abstract: Rydberg-atom sensors have emerged as a promising quantum technology for broadband radio-frequency detection owing to their intrinsic calibration, wide frequency coverage, and high sensitivity. However, their potential for intelligent identification of real-world wireless signals remains largely unexplored. Here, we demonstrate a quantum-enabled framework for mobile signal classification based on Rydberg electromagnetically induced transparency spectroscopy and deep learning. Three types of wireless emissions, including cellular voice call, Wi-Fi, and 5G signals from three commercial smartphone brands, are measured to construct a spectral-fingerprint dataset. A convolutional neural network trained on this dataset achieves an initial baseline classification accuracy of 93.44\%. To compensate for spectral drifts induced by temporal and environmental variations, a progressive fine-tuning strategy is employed to continuously adapt the pretrained model, enabling robust classification across diverse deployment scenarios. Furthermore, continuous real-time monitoring is demonstrated under dynamically varying wireless conditions, including cellular voice call, Wi-Fi, and their coexistence. The proposed system achieves an overall frame-level recognition accuracy of 87.67\% over a 60-s measurement. These results establish a pathway toward intelligent quantum radio-frequency sensing and highlight the potential of Rydberg-atom platforms for real-time wireless signal awareness and spectrum monitoring.

Abstract: Dissipative algorithms for preparing thermal and ground states typically require a total Hamiltonian simulation time that grows at least linearly with system size $N$, even when their mixing time grows only polylogarithmically. We achieve sublinear simulation time for free fermions by engineering local interactions with a bath whose size grows in proportion to the system size. Starting from arbitrary initial states, our thermal state preparation algorithm achieves trace-norm error $ε$ in total simulation time $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{N}/ε)$ at fixed inverse temperature and bounded single-particle bandwidth, while ground state preparation of gapped systems requires only $\mathrm{polylog}(N/ε)$ time. We further establish lower bounds for thermal state preparation within the bath protocols considered, identifying two obstacles to faster preparation, namely fourth-order deviations from the target thermal state and fluctuations induced by a random frequency shared by all bath qubits. Our results demonstrate that the linear simulation time barrier in dissipative state preparation can be overcome and clarify the bath and filter-design constraints that govern further complexity improvements.

</details>

*Yiming Bian,Sarah Bastiaens,Saeed Hakimi,Huy Quang Nguyen,Aboobackkar Sidhique,Cédric Bruynsteen,Govind J. Wanjalkar,Ali Al Hadi Amhaz,Jasper Jans,Axl Bomhals,Ulrik L. Andersen,Tobias Gehring,Xin Yin,Adnan A. E. Hajomer*

TL;DR: 提出CMOS兼容的光电集成多模连续变量QKD架构，以偏振复用为双模示例，通过硅光收发芯片与双通道28-nm CMOS TIA共集成、导频辅助的幺正数字恢复及计及模式串扰的多模安全性分析，在10 km现网光纤上实现平均总有限码长成密钥率估计6.69 Mbit/s。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复用与光子-电子集成可通向可规模化、高吞吐的QKD网络，但需要多通道收发机以及能计入模式耦合的安全性分析。

Method: CMOS兼容的硅光电子多模CV-QKD架构（偏振复用双模）：发射与接收芯片并行制备/探测两个偏振模；接收端共集成双通道28-nm CMOS跨阻放大器；采用导频辅助的幺正数字恢复替代主动光学偏振控制；建立计入模式串扰的多模安全性分析。

Conclusion: 结果确立了基于可量产技术构建可规模化多模量子保密网络的可行路径。

Abstract: Multiplexing and photonic-electronic integration offer a route to scalable and high-throughput quantum key distribution (QKD) networks through parallel transmission and compact hardware suited to volume manufacturing. Realizing this route requires multichannel transmitters and receivers, and a security analysis accounting for mode coupling. Here we present a CMOS-compatible photonic-electronic multimode continuous-variable QKD architecture, using polarization multiplexing as a two-mode example. Silicon-photonic transmitter and receiver chips prepare and detect two polarization modes in parallel. The receiver is co-integrated with a dual-channel 28-nm CMOS transimpedance amplifier, providing shot-noise clearances exceeding 20 dB in both channels. Pilot-assisted unitary digital recovery removes the need for active optical polarization control, while a multimode security analysis accounts for the mode crosstalk. At 2 percent residual crosstalk, the multimode analysis extends the simulated secure distance from 39 to 84 km, near the crosstalk-free limit of 89 km. Over 10 km of deployed fibre, the mean total finite-size secret-key rate estimate is 6.69 Mbit/s, 12 percent above the single-mode analysis from the same digitally recovered data. These results establish a route to scalable multimode quantum-secured networks built on technology suited to mass production.

</details>

Result: 容量界给出完整可达性约束；任意嵌套容量链可被可达态同时饱和，从而得到任意给定可观测量（含系统 Hamiltonian）的最大期望值；固定比较权重下，所构造态最小化所有加权凸可分残差代价，并与目标中心平滑区域取得共同首次接触；最优迹距离误差等于目标相对初始态的最大容量优势，且在该目标前缀处取到，等于同一组二元探针中最大正优化储能亏空除以能隙。

Conclusion: 布居容量几何统一刻画了热操作下准经典态的可达性、可观测量优化、精确与近似转换证书以及通用近似误差，提供了完整且可计算的约束与最优性框架。

Abstract: We characterize quasiclassical state conversion under thermal operations through population capacities: the maximum population that thermal operations can concentrate on each set of energy levels. These capacities form a normalized, nondecreasing submodular rank function whose base polytope is exactly the thermally reachable population region. Their bounds therefore provide a complete system of accessibility constraints. Every nested chain of capacities can be simultaneously saturated by a reachable state, yielding the maximum expectation value of any prescribed observable, including the system Hamiltonian. For a prescribed target, its thermomajorization prefixes suffice to certify exact conversion through at most $n-1$ binary work-extraction probes. For approximate conversion, we construct a distinguished reachable state from active capacity constraints using a weighted principal sequence in $O(n^2 \log n)$ operations. At fixed comparison weights, this state minimizes every weighted convex separable residual cost and realizes a common first contact with the corresponding target-centered smoothing regions. Finally, the optimal trace-distance error equals the largest capacity advantage of the target over the initial state. This gap is attained at a target prefix and equals the largest positive optimized energy-storage deficit among the same binary probes, divided by their energy gap. Thus a common population capacity geometry determines accessibility, observable optimization, conversion certificates, and universal approximation.

</details>
