Abstract: We study the nonequilibrium steady states of an open anisotropic quantum Rabi trimer. Combining semiclassical and quantum mechanical treatments, we map out the system's phase diagram and elucidate the critical phenomena induced by anisotropic light-matter coupling, cavity dissipation, and complex photon hopping, which give rise to abundant multistable phases. Counterintuitively, the frustrated superradiant phase (FSP) can be stabilized by both positive and negative photon hopping, a feature absent in closed Rabi and Dicke trimers. We further uncover two independent dissipation thresholds for the normal superradiant phase and the FSP. Unlike isotropic open quantum Rabi trimers, both phases are suppressed once the dissipation exceeds their respective critical values. The rotating and counter-rotating interaction terms exhibit mutually reciprocal effects on the quantum phase transitions. By analyzing universal scaling behaviors with quantum fluctuations incorporated, we reveal an unprecedented finite-fluctuation critical exponent for the FSP. Moreover, the critical exponents of individual phases are coupling-strength dependent. The multistability and FSP can be tuned by anisotropy and dissipation. Our results broaden the scope of nonequilibrium quantum phase physics in few-body systems and hold promising applications for quantum state engineering and cavity/circuit quantum electrodynamics.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究电子与其散射光子之间的纠缠如何在真实空间和时间中被分辨，并理解这一纠缠在驱动与散射过程中的时空结构与局域特征。

Method: 从Fermi振子表象的非相对论QED出发，在长度规范下写出光子构型空间中固定光子数分量的Fock耦合方程（光子通过非局域核在原子周围产生）；截断至单光子，不作旋转波近似，对波导中单横模、增强耦合的一维模型原子进行数值求解；原子由短的非共振激光脉冲驱动，电子与光子均在实空间网格上表示；通过在两个光子数分量上采样的walker计算局域纠缠。

Result: 全局电子-光子纠缠大体是瞬态的，在脉冲期间达到峰值，随极化电子云返回原子实而减小，最终态接近电子态与“场真空叠加单个散射光子”之积。局域纠缠（由walker采样得到）对两种粒子呈现相反的空间图样：电子熵在原子核处最小，映射偶极耦合的局域强度；光子熵在原子附近最大（光子产生与吸收处），在出射波包中较小。

Conclusion: 电子与其散射光子之间的纠缠在时间上主要是瞬态的，在空间上表现为电子与光子互补（相反）的局域纠缠分布，反映出偶极耦合强度与光子产生/吸收位置的作用。

Abstract: We study the entanglement between an electron and the photon it scatters, resolved in real space and time. Starting from nonrelativistic QED in Fermi's oscillator representation, we write Fock's coupled equations for components with fixed photon number in the photon configuration space, in the length gauge, where the photon is created around the atom through a nonlocal kernel. Truncated at one photon, the equations are solved numerically without the rotating-wave approximation for a one-dimensional model atom in a waveguide with a single transverse mode and enhanced coupling. The atom is driven by a short, off-resonant laser pulse, with the electron and photon represented on real-space grids. The global electron-photon entanglement is largely transient. It peaks during the pulse and decreases as the polarized electron cloud returns to the core, leaving a state close to a product of the electron state and a superposition of the field vacuum with a single scattered photon. Local entanglement, obtained from walkers sampled across both photon-number components, exhibits opposite spatial patterns for the two particles. The electron entropy is smallest at the nucleus and maps the local strength of the dipole coupling, whereas the photon entropy is largest near the atom, where photons are created and absorbed, and smaller in the outgoing wave packets.

</details>


### [83] [Benchmarking quantum-annealing-based approaches for factory layout planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35478)
*Xiangqian Wu,Philipp Schworm,Marcel Wagner,Jan C. Aurich*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文对四种量子退火(QA)方法（IBQM、HDQM、HCQM、HNLM）在工厂布局规划问题上进行基准测试，并与遗传算法、粒子群优化及禁忌搜索-模拟退火混合方法在14个布局实例上比较解质量、计算时间与可扩展性，发现QA方法在保持竞争性解质量的同时计算时间最多降低94.51%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工厂布局规划早期阶段直接影响制造系统的运营成本、效率与生产率，而传统自动化规划方法常在解质量与计算开销之间面临权衡；已有量子退火研究多局限于单一QA建模形式，未充分利用当前支持QA与经典方法混合的多模型类型混合求解器，因此需要系统评估QA在布局规划中的潜力。

Method: 实现并基准测试四种基于QA的方法及其对应求解器：迭代二次模型(IBQM)、混合离散二次模型(HDQM)、混合约束二次模型(HCQM)、混合非线性模型(HNLM)；为提升可扩展性，为IBQM设计迭代分解策略，为HDQM引入候选选择流程；在14个布局实例（布局规模、几何复杂度、空间约束、运输路径配置各异）上与遗传算法、粒子群优化、禁忌搜索-模拟退火混合方法对比，评估解质量、计算时间与可扩展性。

Result: QA类方法解质量具竞争力且计算优势显著，相对经典参考方法计算时间最多减少94.51%；其中IBQM在78.6%的实例中取得最优解质量，最大改进达17.41%，同时计算时间降低81.34%。

Conclusion: 混合与迭代QA建模式方法在工厂布局规划中兼顾解质量与计算效率，为其进一步应用于工业布局规划提供了依据。

Abstract: The earlystage of factory layout planning has a direct effect on the operating costs, efficiency, and productivity of manufacturing systems. Conventional automated planning approaches often face a trade-off between solution quality and computational effort. Recent studies have investigated the potential of quantum annealing (QA) for solving factory layout planning problems. However, existing studies have mainly focused on individual QA-based formulations, whereas current hybrid QA solvers support multiple model types that combine QA and classical methods. To explore the potential of QA for factory layout planning, this paper benchmarks four QA based approaches implemented with the corresponding solvers, including an iterative binary quadratic model (IBQM), a hybrid discrete quadratic model (HDQM), a hybrid constrained quadratic model (HCQM), and a hybrid nonlinear model (HNLM). To improve scalability, an iterative decomposition strategy is developed for IBQM, while a candidate selection procedure is introduced for HDQM. The approaches are benchmarked against genetic algorithm, particle swarm optimization, and a hybrid tabu search and simulated annealing approach on 14 layout instances with different layout sizes, geometric complexity, spatial restrictions, and transport route configurations. The comparative analysis considers solution quality, computation time, and scalability. The results show that QA-based approaches achieve competitive solution quality and substantial computational advantages, reducing computation time by up to 94.51% relative to the classical reference methods. Among them, IBQM obtains the best solution quality in 78.6% of the instances, with a maximum improvement of 17.41% associated with an 81.34% reduction in computation time. These findings provide a basis for their further application in industrial layout planning.

</details>

*Junichi Haruna,Kotaro Murakami*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种融合局部平衡单比特翻转与簇交换的MCMC核，解决splitting方法中单比特翻转核被"困住"导致逻辑错误率估计偏差的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: splitting方法可在稀有事件区高效估计量子纠错码逻辑错误率，但依赖以单比特翻转为提议的MCMC采样器；已有报道指出单比特翻转在部分码上会被困住，从而使估计产生偏差。

Method: 设计新核：将局部平衡（locally balanced）的单比特翻转提议与簇交换（cluster-exchange）移动相结合。在码容量比特翻转噪声下，对12种码，以两种方式对比新核、单比特翻转核与直接Monte Carlo：(1)与MCMC无关参考量比较平均故障权重；(2)测量达到固定精度所需计算时间。

Result: 新核在所有码上与参考一致，包括单比特翻转被困的码；在单比特翻转被困之处新核更快，部分码上快若干数量级；在最低错误率下，新核在所有码上都快于直接Monte Carlo，部分码上快四个数量级以上。

Conclusion: 簇交换与局部平衡提议的组合有效克服了单比特翻转的遍历性缺陷，兼顾无偏性与效率，使splitting方法在稀有事件区更具实用性。

Abstract: The splitting method estimates logical error rates of quantum error-correcting codes deep in the rare-event regime, where direct Monte Carlo sampling is prohibitively expensive. It relies on a Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) sampler of faulty configurations, usually driven by one-bit-flip proposals. However, one-bit flips have been reported to be trapped on some codes, which biases the estimate. To address this issue, we propose a new kernel that combines locally balanced one-bit-flip proposals with a cluster-exchange move. We benchmark both kernels and direct Monte Carlo on twelve codes under code-capacity bit-flip noise in two ways, by comparing the mean fault weight with an MCMC-independent reference and by measuring the computation time to reach a fixed accuracy. In the first, our kernel agrees with the reference on every code, including those where one-bit flips are trapped. In the second, it is faster than one-bit flips wherever they are trapped, by orders of magnitude on some codes. Furthermore, at the lowest error rate, it is faster than direct Monte Carlo on every code, by more than four orders of magnitude on some.

</details>


### [85] [Geometric aspects of fault tolerance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35513)
*Jonathan Conrad*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从几何视角研究容错：给出容错的纤维丛框架的算符代数表述，证明Knill-Laflamme条件的推广蕴含平坦联络；为GKP码发展该框架，证明各极化类型的模空间光滑嵌入正则化分布空间的有限维Grassmannian；并说明与Jacobian相关的GKP码可经高斯态的码空间投影产生magic态，识别出一类CCZ型magic态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错与量子纠错码的结构通常以代数条件（如Knill-Laflamme条件）刻画，缺乏统一的几何语言；同时GKP码作为连续变量编码，其模空间与可实现的非Clifford资源（magic态）的几何来源尚不清晰。作者希望用纤维丛与模空间的几何工具统一描述容错，并将该框架应用于GKP码以理解其参数空间结构与magic态的产生机制。

Method: (1) 构造容错纤维丛框架的算符代数表述，将Knill-Laflamme条件推广，并证明其蕴含该框架上的平坦联络；(2) 将该框架用于GKP码，对每一极化类型研究其模空间，证明其光滑嵌入正则化分布空间的有限维Grassmannian；(3) 对与Jacobian相伴的GKP码，构造由高斯态经码空间投影得到的magic态，并识别出CCZ型magic态。

Result: Knill-Laflamme条件的推广等价于（蕴含）纤维丛框架上的平坦联络，从而以联络平坦性刻画容错；GKP码各极化类型的模空间可光滑嵌入正则化分布空间的有限维Grassmannian，赋予其 Grassmann 几何结构；与Jacobian相伴的GKP码可通过高斯态的码空间投影生成magic态，并具体识别出一类CCZ型magic态。

Conclusion: 容错具有可由算符代数与纤维丛联络统一刻画的几何本质；GKP码的模空间自然位于Grassmannian中，且Jacobian型GKP码为magic态（含CCZ型）提供了系统的几何构造途径，将纠错码几何与非Clifford资源理论联系起来。

Abstract: We highlight three geometric aspects of fault tolerance. First, we construct an operator-algebraic formulation of the fiber bundle framework for fault tolerance, and show that a generalization of the Knill-Laflamme conditions implies a flat connection for this framework. Next, we develop this framework for Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill (GKP) codes. We show that, for each polarization type, their moduli space embeds smoothly into a Grassmannian of finite-dimensional subspaces of a regularized distribution space. Finally, we explain how GKP codes associated with Jacobians give rise to magic states that can be obtained by code space projections of Gaussian states and identify a $\rm CCZ$-type magic state arising this way.

</details>


### [86] [Equilibrium propagation in non-equilibrium quantum systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35585)
*Aditya Gandotra,David D. Noachtar,Aashish A. Clerk*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: I need to analyze this abstract about equilibrium propagation extended to driven-dissipative quantum systems. Let me structure my analysis.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The paper addresses a key challenge in equilibrium propagation (EP): the need to repeatedly relax the many-body quantum system to its ground state after each parameter update, which is computationally/experimentally costly. The motivation is to circumvent this ground-state relaxation requirement.

Method: The authors extend EP to many-body driven-dissipative quantum systems whose dynamics naturally relax to a parameter-dependent non-equilibrium steady state. They show that despite the absence of a free energy, a quantum detailed balance symmetry in a class of open systems enables an efficient EP strategy, crucially relying on newly derived non-equilibrium static susceptibility relations. They analyze two experimentally relevant models: a driven-dissipative transverse field Ising model and a parametrically-driven bosonic lattice.

Result: The authors derive a new class of non-equilibrium static susceptibility relations and demonstrate that a quantum detailed balance symmetry permits an efficient EP strategy for a class of open systems, validated on the transverse field Ising model and a parametrically-driven bosonic lattice.

Conclusion: By leveraging natural relaxation to a non-equilibrium steady state together with quantum detailed balance, EP can be extended to driven-dissipative quantum systems, avoiding repeated ground-state preparation while retaining efficient training.

Abstract: Equilibrium propagation (EP) is a powerful training algorithm for self-learning physical systems that is widely used for a variety of machine learning inspired tasks. It relies on the existence of an energy function that depends on tunable training parameters, and can be extended to apply to many-body quantum systems in equilibrium. A key challenge however is the need to repeatedly relax to the system ground state after each parameter update. We show here a route for circumventing this problem by extending EP to many-body driven-dissipative quantum systems, where the dynamics naturally relaxes to a parameter-dependent non-equilibrium steady state. Despite the lack of a free energy, we find that for a class of open systems, a quantum detailed balance symmetry still allows for an efficient EP strategy. This relies crucially on a new class of non-equilibrium static susceptibility relations that we derive in detail. We analyze examples of our new non-equilibrium training paradigm for two experimentally-relevant models: a driven-dissipative transverse field Ising model, and a parametrically-driven bosonic lattice.

</details>


### [87] [Did the Gran Sasso Experiment Rule Out the Diósi-Penrose Theory? Musing on the 2021 Result and the Present Experimental Status](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35608)
*Catalina Curceanu,Nicola Bortolotti,Lajos Diósi,Simone Manti,Kristian Piscicchia*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: Diósi-Penrose（DP）自发辐射实验对短程截断参数 $R_0$ 的约束不断收紧（XENONnT 已达 $R_0>4.5\times10^{-10}\,\mathrm{m}$@95%CL），但这只是对马尔可夫型（白噪声）DP 动力学的参数约束，并不独立否定 Penrose 的寿命判据，也不排除彩色噪声、耗散型等修正的自发坍缩理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清 2021 年 Gran Sasso 低本底实验对 DP 理论排除结论的逻辑范围：后续实验（Majorana Demonstrator、XENONnT）强化了参数界限，但常被误读为对整个 Penrose–Diósi 框架或所有引力相关自发坍缩理论的否定，需重建其"已排除/未排除"的边界并置于当前实验语境中。

Method: 比较无参数prescription（以核的空间量子涨落标定 $R_0\simeq5\times10^{-12}\,\mathrm{m}$，对应锗在实验温度）与实验下限；梳理 2021 年 Gran Sasso 结果、2022 年 Majorana Demonstrator 改进及 XENONnT 的世界领先界限（$R_0>4.9\times10^{-10}\,\mathrm{m}$@90%CL、$>4.5\times10^{-10}\,\mathrm{m}$@95%CL），并分析这些界限所依赖的理论假设（马尔可夫近似、有效白噪声、质量密度分辨）。

Result: 无参数 DP 预言值 $R_0\simeq5\times10^{-12}\,\mathrm{m}$ 已被 2021 年 Gran Sasso 的 $R_0>5.4\times10^{-11}\,\mathrm{m}$@95% 排除，即 Diósi 随机动力学与 Penrose 式质量密度分辨的特定组合被否证；XENONnT 界限进一步将 $R_0$ 下界提高约一个数量级，强化该排除。

Conclusion: 这些实验仅"参数化约束"马尔可夫、有效白噪声型 DP 动力学，不独立检验 Penrose（及 Diósi）的寿命判据本身，也不排除彩色噪声、耗散型或其他修正的引力相关自发坍缩理论；应在明确理论假设的前提下解读界限。

Abstract: In 2021, a low-background experiment at the Gran Sasso National Laboratory searched for spontaneous radiation predicted by the standard Markovian Diósi-Penrose (DP) dynamics and obtained the new bound $R_0 > 5.4 \times 10^{-11}\,\mathrm{m}$ at $95\%$ probability for the short-length cutoff parameter. For germanium at the experimental temperature, the parameter-free prescription identifying $R_0$ with the spatial quantum spread of a nucleus gives $R_0 \simeq 5 \times 10^{-12}\,\mathrm{m}$. That particular combination, Diósi's stochastic dynamics and the Penrose-motivated mass-density resolution, was therefore excluded. Subsequent searches strengthened the cutoff bound. The Majorana Demonstrator improved it in 2022, and XENONnT has now obtained the world-leading limits $R_0 > 4.9 \times 10^{-10}\,\mathrm{m}$ at $90\%$ confidence level and $R_0 > 4.5 \times 10^{-10}\,\mathrm{m}$ at $95\%$ confidence level. These advances reinforce the 2021 exclusion but do not alter its logical scope: they \emph{parametrically constrain} the Markovian, effectively white-noise DP dynamics, not Penrose's (and Diósi's) lifetime criterion independently of a dynamics, and not all colored-noise, dissipative, or otherwise modified gravity-related spontaneous collapse theories. We reconstruct what Gran Sasso did and did not rule out and place that conclusion in the present experimental context.

</details>


### [88] [Quantum-enhanced metrology with classical shadows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35632)
*Luca Ion,Manuel Gessner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出结合随机局域测量估计的Pauli矩与优化矩估计器的量子计量协议，实现无需预先选定可观测量的高效线路计量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计量依赖能最大化参数信息量的可观测两，但这类可观测两往往难以实际测量获取。

Method: 从固定集合的随机化局域测量中估计Pauli矩，并组合成优化的矩方法估计器；对局域Pauli经典阴影推导出决定估计量方差与样本复杂度保证的"阴影信息"。

Result: 确定了可分态灵敏度极限，证明纠缠可带来常数因子的灵敏度提升，并用one-axis-twisted探针进行说明。

Conclusion: 该协议实现了无需选择可观测量的高效、基于线路的量子计量。

Abstract: Quantum metrology relies on observables that maximize information about the parameter of interest, but are often hard to access. We develop a protocol that combines Pauli moments estimated from a fixed ensemble of randomized local measurements into an optimized method-of-moments estimator. For local Pauli classical shadows, we derive the shadow information which determines estimator variance and sample-complexity guarantees. Identifying the separable-state sensitivity limit, we show that entanglement can lead to a constant-factor sensitivity enhancement, as illustrated with one-axis-twisted probes. This protocol allows for efficient circuit-based metrology without choosing an observable.

</details>


### [89] [Quantum-Inspired and Optimization-Based CT Reconstruction Through Spin-Error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35657)
*Hyunju Lee,Jeonghwa Lee,Yushuang Sam Yang,Clement Chu,Anthony B. Murphy,Kyungtaek Jun*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文面向量子优化CT重建的可扩展性问题，在不改变CT-QUBO目标的前提下，采用模拟退火（SA）加自旋误差缓解优化（SEMO）处理54视图ROI与含探测器行损坏的九视图重建，显著降低错误像素数，达到与部分未缓解混合求解器相当或更优的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子优化CT重建在稀疏视图与含噪正弦图条件下精度优于经典方法，但问题规模扩大时QUBO模型的二值变量与交互项急剧增长（如60×60 ROI、3个衰减系数即需10,800个二值变量），难以直接扩展，故需探索可扩展的求解路径。

Method: 保持CT-QUBO目标函数不变，先使用模拟退火（SA）求解，再施加自旋误差缓解优化（SEMO）；在三个54视图ROI算例及含20%探测器行损坏的九视图算例上评估，并与选定的混合量子-经典求解器结果对比。

Result: 低预算SA输出的错误重像素数经SEMO后由13降至1、38降至0、54降至15；20%探测器行损坏下四组SA/SEMO配对中有三组实现精确重建；对某混合求解器输出施加SEMO使ROI误差由78降至1像素。改进呈实例依赖性：某损坏正弦图案例中QUBO能量下降但图像域误差略增；某ROI案例精化后仍保留15个错误像素且QUBO能量低于真值。

Conclusion: 在所评估的问题规模下，SA与SEMO的重建精度与选定未缓解混合求解器相当或更优，为未来更大规模、并行化的量子CT图像重建提供了初始解质量基准；但QUBO能量与图像域误差并非始终一致，需谨慎使用能量作为唯一判据。

Abstract: Quantum-optimization-based computed tomography (CT) reconstruction has demonstrated quantum supremacy in reconstruction accuracy over conventional classical methods under sparse-view and corrupted-sinogram conditions. However, scaling this approach remains challenging because larger images require increasingly many binary variables and interactions in the underlying quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) model. Even with iterative region-of-interest (ROI) refinement, a 60 by 60 ROI with three X-ray mass attenuation coefficients requires 10,800 binary variables under our encoding. As an initial step toward larger quantum CT images, we investigate simulated annealing (SA) followed by spin-error mitigation for optimisation (SEMO), without altering the CT-QUBO objective. We evaluate three 54-view ROI reconstruction instances and nine-view reconstruction with detector-row corruption, comparing SA and SEMO with selected hybrid quantum-classical solver results. For the ROI cases, SEMO reduced the numbers of incorrectly reconstructed pixels in low-budget SA outputs from 13 to 1, 38 to 0, and 54 to 15. With 20 percent detector-row corruption, three of four selected SA and SEMO pairs achieved exact reconstruction. Applying SEMO to one hybrid-solver output reduced its ROI error from 78 to 1 pixel. Improvements were instance-dependent: in one corrupted-sinogram case, QUBO energy decreased while image-domain error increased slightly; in one ROI case, the refined image retained 15 incorrect pixels despite having lower QUBO energy than ground truth. At the evaluated problem sizes, SA and SEMO attained reconstruction accuracy comparable to or better than selected unmitigated hybrid-solver results, providing an initial solution-quality benchmark for future larger-scale, parallelized quantum CT image reconstruction.

</details>


### [90] [Quantum computation of energy level degeneracy and thermodynamical properties of spin systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35660)
*V. M. Tkachuk,M. I. Samar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出量子-经典混合方法，用单个辅助量子比特测量虚逆温下的配分函数，通过逆离散傅里叶变换从等间距能谱网格精确重构简并度，并在IBM量子处理器上演示了36自旋Kagome阻挫Ising反铁磁体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接从量子硬件获取自旋系统的能级简并度与热力学性质（如热容）存在困难，需在含噪中等规模量子设备上找到可行且可扩展的方案。

Method: 单个辅助量子比特在虚逆温下测量配分函数Z(β)；对位于已知等间距能量网格上的能谱，采用逆离散傅里叶变换在无噪声情形下精确重构简并度且无谱泄漏；量子线路通过对易相互作用与欧拉序（Eulerian ordering）进行优化。

Result: 在ibm_kingston处理器上对周期Kagome图上最多36个自旋的阻挫Ising反铁磁体进行了验证；即使线路深度大且存在硬件噪声，重构能谱仍保留主要特征，计算得到的热容复现了精确结果的主要温度依赖关系。

Conclusion: 该方法为在含噪量子硬件上提取自旋系统的能谱简并度与热力学量提供了可行途径，对大规模阻挫体系具有良好的适用性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: We propose a hybrid quantum-classical method for reconstructing energy-level degeneracies and thermodynamic properties of spin systems. A single ancilla qubit is used to measure the partition function at imaginary inverse temperatures. For spectra lying on a known equally spaced energy grid, the inverse discrete Fourier transform allows exact reconstruction of degeneracies in the absence of noise, without spectral leakage. The quantum circuits are optimized using commuting interactions and Eulerian ordering. We demonstrate the method for frustrated Ising antiferromagnets on periodic Kagome graphs with up to 36 spins, using the ibm_kingston quantum processor. Despite substantial circuit depth and hardware noise, the reconstructed spectra retain their dominant features, and the calculated heat capacities reproduce the main temperature dependence of the exact results.
  Keywords: partition function, imaginary temperature, Ising model, heat capacity, ancilla qubit, quantum computing.

</details>


### [91] [$κ$-Parametrized Boltzmann Distribution Sampling on Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35665)
*João Marcos Cavalcanti de Albuquerque Neto,Gustavo Castro do Amaral,Guilherme Penello Temporão*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文用 Qiskit Aer 模拟评估参数化退火调度 QAOA（PAQ-QAOA）辅助受限玻尔兹曼机（RBM）的玻尔兹曼分布采样，通过分析分布的有效逆温度 κ，为可处理规模实例提供数值证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RBM 是生成模型的核心，但其训练依赖对未知玻尔兹曼分布采样，计算上难以处理；QAOA 有望辅助 RBM 训练并优于纯经典策略，但存在关键问题：对任意问题规模如何确定能给出预期采样结果的量子线路深度。

Method: 在 Qiskit Aer 模拟器上评估 QAOA 辅助的玻尔兹曼分布采样；针对可处理问题规模，分析分布的有效逆温度 κ；采用参数化退火调度形式的 QAOA（PAQ-QAOA）。

Result: 基于 Qiskit Aer 的数值实验表明，PAQ-QAOA 能够近似所考察的可处理规模实例上的 RBM 玻尔兹曼采样。

Conclusion: PAQ-QAOA 在可处理规模下具备近似 RBM 玻尔兹曼采样的潜力，但真实硬件性能与规模扩展性仍是待解问题。

Abstract: Generative models use training data to produce new samples that resemble the original, with applications in classification and recommendation systems. Restricted Boltzmann Machines (RBMs) are at the core of generative models but suffer from the computational intractability of training, which relies on sampling an unknown Boltzmann Distribution (BD). The Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA) can assist RBM training, potentially providing benefits over strictly classical strategies. One particular problem is determining the quantum circuit depth for an arbitrary problem size that yields the expected sampling results. In this study, we evaluate QAOA-assisted sampling of BDs using Qiskit's Aer Simulator by analysing the inverse effective temperature of the distribution, $κ$, for tractable problem sizes. The results provide numerical evidence, based on Qiskit Aer simulations, that parameterized annealing-scheduled QAOA (PAQ-QAOA) can approximate RBM Boltzmann sampling for the tractable instances considered, while hardware performance and scaling remain open questions.

</details>


### [92] [Bell-pair distillation under imperfect operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35699)
*András Kinyó,Tamás Kiss,Orsolya Kálmán*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于局域操作与经典通信（LOCC）的概率性普适纠缠蒸馏协议在实现误差下的鲁棒性，考察非相干与相干门误差及测量读出误差，确定蒸馏仍能提升目标Bell态保真度的参数区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作提出了可从未知两量子比特态的多个副本中概率性、普适地蒸馏出最大纠缠Bell对的LOCC协议，但实际实现中的不完美会削弱其性能，因此需要评估该协议的抗误差能力及其适用范围。

Method: 构建多种非相干与相干门误差模型以及测量读出误差模型，对协议进行含噪建模，量化蒸馏后相对目标Bell态保真度的变化；并分析双方非对称误差及互补后选择结果对应的蒸馏动力学。

Result: 协议在较大的误差范围内仍保持有效性，但鲁棒性强烈依赖于误差类型；在特定非对称误差条件下，互补的后选择结果给出相同的蒸馏动力学，从而可提升成功概率。

Conclusion: 该普适纠缠蒸馏协议对实现误差具有可观的容忍度，但需按误差类型评估其收益；非对称误差分析为进一步优化成功概率提供了途径。

Abstract: We recently proposed an entanglement distillation protocol based on local operations and classical communication that probabilistically and universally distills a maximally entangled Bell pair from multiple copies of an unknown two-qubit state [Phys. Rev. Lett. 135, 260202 (2025)]. Here, we investigate the robustness of this protocol against imperfections in its implementation. We consider several incoherent and coherent gate-error models, as well as measurement readout errors, and determine the regimes in which distillation enhances the fidelity with the target Bell state. We find that the protocol remains effective over substantial error ranges, although its robustness depends strongly on the type of error. We further analyze asymmetric errors between the two parties and identify conditions under which complementary postselection outcomes yield identical distillation dynamics, allowing the success probability to be enhanced.

</details>


### [93] [Distillable entanglement is continuous](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35722)
*Lukas Schmitt,David Sutter*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了可提纯纠缠（distillable entanglement）的连续性，给出了适用于任意两体态、随系统维度对数增长的显式渐近连续性上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可提纯纠缠是LOCC下渐近纠缠转换的最优速率，但其连续性此前缺乏显式、普适的定量刻画；而已知的条件熵、纠缠代价、相对熵纠缠等量均具有结构相似的连续性界，故希望为可提纯纠缠建立同一形式的界。

Method: 通过构造显式的LOCC协议，使两个任意两体态可相互转换，且转换所需的ebit代价随二者迹距离趋于零而消失，由此导出渐近连续性上界。

Result: 得到对任意两体态成立、关于系统维度呈对数依赖的显式渐近连续性界；该界与条件熵、纠缠代价和相对熵纠缠的已知连续性界具有相同结构。

Conclusion: 可提纯纠缠在LOCC（误差消失）意义下是连续的，其连续性界形式与若干经典纠缠度量一致，深化了对纠缠度量稳定性的理解。

Abstract: We prove continuity of distillable entanglement, the optimal asymptotic rate at which copies of a bipartite state can be converted into maximally entangled pairs by local operations and classical communication (LOCC) with vanishing error. We establish an explicit asymptotic continuity bound that holds for arbitrary bipartite states and depends logarithmically on the system dimension. This bound has the same structure as known continuity bounds for conditional entropy, entanglement cost, and relative entropy of entanglement. Our proof constructs an explicit LOCC protocol that converts either of two arbitrary bipartite states into the other at an ebit cost that vanishes as their trace distance tends to zero.

</details>


### [94] [Decoding with alternating weighted graph sparsification for quantum low-density parity-check codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35735)
*Josu Etxezarreta Martinez,Francisco Garcia-Herrero,Ruben M. Otxoa,Reza Dastbasteh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出DAWG解码器，将Tanner图稀疏化与BP解码器集成（ensembling）相结合，配合LSD-0后处理，在双变量双曲码与超密色码电路上达到与Relay BP、Vibe相当的性能，同时后处理调用次数降低一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建容错量子计算机需要实时解码。现有基于BP的先进解码器（如Relay BP、Vibe）性能虽好，但后处理调用频繁、延迟尾部大，且后处理需在全尺寸探测器错误模型图上运行，对专用硬件资源要求高，难以满足实时性约束。

Method: DAWG由一组BP解码器构成：每个成员先在全探测器错误模型Tanner图上运行若干轮BP，再将后验信息通过各自的转移矩阵映射到稀疏化图上继续运行BP；采用最小和BP的串行调度，并借鉴Vibe思想对每个成员随机化调度。最后可用LSD-0后处理器，该后处理在稀疏化的小图上运行。

Result: 在双变量双曲码与超密色码电路上与现有先进解码方法进行数值对比：DAWG+LSD-0的性能与Relay BP、Vibe（同样搭配LSD-0）相近；在相同集成规模下，LSD-0被调用次数比Vibe少一个数量级，因此延迟尾部极小；且LSD-0作用于远小于完整探测器错误模型的稀疏图，降低了专用硬件的资源需求。

Conclusion: DAWG+LSD-0是一种可行、低成本的基于BP的量子纠错解码算法，在保持竞争力的纠错性能的同时显著降低后处理频率与硬件复杂度，有利于实时解码实现。

Abstract: Real-time decoding is a fundamental necessity for building fault-tolerant quantum computers. In this work, we introduce decoding with alternating weighted graph sparsification (DAWG), an ensemble based approach that combines Tanner graph sparsification with ensembling belief-propagation (BP) decoders. The DAWG decoder runs BP decoders over the full detector error model Tanner graph for certain iterations to then transfer the posteriors to a sparsified graph in which additional rounds of BP are run. Each of the decoders in the ensemble uses a different transfer matrix to map the posterior information to the sparsified graph. We use the serial schedule for the minimum-sum BP decoders and, thus, employ Vibe decoding ideas by randomizing the schedules of each of them. The performance of the DAWG decoder is then numerically studied for bivariate bicycle codes and superdense color code circuits to be compared with other state-of-the-art decoding approaches. Importantly, the DAWG decoder shows a similar performance to Relay belief-propagation and Vibe decoding when combined with an order-$0$ Localized Statistics Decoding (LSD-$0$) post-processor. Crucially, the post-processor is called an order of magnitude less times than for Vibe with the same ensemble size, indicating that the introduced latency tail will be minimal. Furthermore, LSD-$0$ operates on a sparsified decoding graph of a fraction of the size of the full detector error model, which simplifies the resources required by specialized hardware to support it. Overall, the DAWG+LSD-$0$ decoder represents a viable low-cost BP-based decoding algorithm for quantum error correction codes.

</details>


### [95] [Competing coherence geometries in positive maps: positivity hierarchies, atomicity, and optimal entanglement witnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35758)
*Massimiliano F. Sacchi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了一族qudit正映射，其Choi算子由独立交换耦合与集体对角耦合两种相干模式叠加而成，并给出混合族中每个成员的k-正性、可分解性与原子性的精确相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在任意局域维度下比较k-正性、可分解性、原子性、PPT edge state几何与见证最优性，需要一个可精确求解的模型族。

Method: 引入独立交换耦合与集体对角耦合两种竞争相干模式构成的混合正映射族；解析推导正性、完全正性及所有中间k-正性阈值；结合"正+共正"显式分解与匹配的PPT态族证明可分解边界；用嵌入qutrit证书判定原子性；在最大相干端点通过显式正减法构造原子最优纠缠见证。

Result: k-正性阈值依赖信道重数，而可分解边界在无标度坐标下具有普适性；PPT edge states的探测区域为椭圆（集体重标度后为圆盘）；混合族内不可分性与原子性完全重合；纯集体端点在其整个正性区间内可分解；最大相干处的原子最优见证具有spanning性质且PPT探测集严格更大。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了任意局域维度下可精确求解的统一框架，用于对比k-正性、可分解性、原子性、PPT-edge-state几何与见证最优性。

Abstract: We introduce a family of positive maps on qudits whose Choi operators combine two competing coherence patterns: independent exchange couplings and a collective set of diagonal couplings. For every mixed member of the family, we derive exact conditions for positivity, complete positivity, and all intermediate levels of k-positivity, and we determine the complete phase structure of decomposability and atomicity. The higher-positivity thresholds depend on the channel multiplicities, whereas the exact decomposability boundary is universal in scale-free coordinates. Its proof combines an explicit positive-plus-copositive decomposition with a matching family of positive-partial-transpose (PPT) states. The latter form PPT edge states whose detection region is an ellipse, or a disk after collective rescaling. An embedded-qutrit certificate shows that indecomposability and atomicity coincide throughout the mixed family. In contrast, the purely collective endpoint is decomposable over its entire positive interval. Finally, at maximal coherence, an explicit positive subtraction produces an atomic optimal entanglement witness with the spanning property and a strictly larger PPT- detection set. The study thus provides an exactly solvable setting in which k-positivity, decomposability, atomicity, PPT-edge-state geometry, and witness optimality can be compared in arbitrary local dimension.

</details>


### [96] [The $\varepsilon$-smoothed Entanglement of Formation and its relation to the Moreau-Yosida approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35797)
*M. E. Shirokov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文将 Mora-Piani-Briegel 的 ε-测度构造应用于纠缠形成熵（EoF），构造出一族凸连续泛函 E_{F,ε}，并系统分析其变分表示、弱次可加性与连续性，给出在有界秩/能量集合上的一致收敛上界，同时证明无穷维双分体量子系统任意态存在（且可能不唯一）最近可分态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EoF 等纠缠度量在无穷维情形下往往只有不连续、非凸或难以计算的版本，缺乏既贴近原度量又具备良好解析性质（凸性、连续性）的近似族，且需要可量化的收敛速率。

Method: 以迹范数距离为基本度量，对 EoF 施加 Mora-Piani-Briegel 的 ε-测度构造，得到 E_{F,ε}（ε∈(0,1]）；利用其与 Moreau-Yosida 近似族 E^λ_F 的关系进行性质分析；借助已有 EoF 半连续性界导出收敛速率估计；并讨论向相对熵纠缠等度量的推广。

Result: E_{F,ε} 关于 ε 单调不增且当 ε→0⁺ 逐点收敛到 EoF；给出其变分表达式（ε=0 时退化为 EoF 的标准变分式）；刻画弱次可加性与连续性；在有界秩/能量约束的态集上得到可计算的一致收敛上界；证明无穷维双分体系统任意态存在最近可分态且不唯一。

Conclusion: E_{F,ε} 构成 EoF 的性质良好的凸正则化序列，兼具可计算收敛速率与结构化变分形式，并可推广至其他纠缠度量。

Abstract: We describe a family of convex continuous functions $E_{F,\varepsilon}$, $\varepsilon\in(0,1]$, on the set of states of a bipartite quantum system (consisting of finite-dimensional or infinite-dimensional subsystems) obtained by applying the Mora-Piani-Briegel construction of $\varepsilon$-measure (proposed in [Phys. Rev. A 109, 052413]) to the Entanglement of Formation (EoF) with the use of the trace norm distance in the role of basic metric. These functions are non-increasing in $\varepsilon$ and converge pointwise to the EoF as $\varepsilon\to0^+$. We conducted a detailed analysis of properties of the functions $E_{F,\varepsilon}$, using (among others) the relationship between the family $\{E_{F,\varepsilon}\}_{\varepsilon\in[0,1]}$ and the family $\{E^λ_F(ρ)\}_{λ>0}$ of Moreau-Yosida approximations of the EoF explored in [arXiv:2609.30246].
  We obtain a variational expression for the functions $E_{F,\varepsilon}$ (which for $\varepsilon=0$ turns into the standard variational expression for the EoF). We also describe the weak subadditivity property of the functions $E_{F,\varepsilon}$ and analyse their continuity properties.
  The semicontinuity bounds for the EoF (presented in [Lob.J.Math., 46, 2632-2658],[arXiv:2404.07978]) allow us to obtain easily computable upper bounds on the rate of uniform convergence of the function $E_{F,\varepsilon}$ to the EoF as $\varepsilon\to0^+$ on the sets of states with bounded rank/energy of one of the marginal states.
  Applicability of the obtained results to other entanglement measures, in particular, to the relative entropy of entanglement, is discussed.
  As an auxiliary result, it is shown that for any state of an infinite-dimensional bipartite quantum system, there exists a nearest separable state w.r.t. the trace norm distance. The non-uniqueness of such a state is also shown.

</details>


### [97] [Sparse Carleman Embeddings for Nonlinear Discrete Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35824)
*Hsuan-Cheng Wu,Xiantao Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究受迫二次离散系统的Carleman嵌入及量子态制备应用：证明在显式稳定性与初态假设下截断误差随嵌入度几何衰减且对所有迭代一致；给出稀疏保持的精确辅助线性系统表示，并用其构造线性于迭代步数、对数于精度的量子算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维离散非线性动力系统（种群模型、迭代优化、数值模拟）需大量迭代，计算代价高；希望用Carleman嵌入将非线性迭代线性化，并借助量子算法加速态制备。

Method: 对受迫二次离散系统建立Carleman嵌入；针对离散时间下提升更新矩阵变稠密的问题，构造逐张量因子施加系数映射的精确辅助线性系统表示，使行/列稀疏度与截断度无关；对历史系统重缩放并分析条件数；据此设计制备第n次迭代归一化近似的量子算法，并用一维与三维数值实验验证截断嵌入的收敛性。

Result: 截断误差随嵌入度几何下降并在所有迭代上一致；辅助表示的稀疏度有界；重缩放历史系统的条件数随迭代次数线性增长、对截断度一致；在相干输入访问、固定稀疏度与稳定性参数、已知末态范数正下界条件下，预言机查询复杂度对n线性、对精度倒数多对数；数值实验显示收敛性。

Conclusion: Carleman嵌入可为受迫二次离散系统提供稀疏保持的精确线性化，并在明确假设下实现高效量子态制备，兼顾理论保证与实验可行性。

Abstract: Discrete nonlinear dynamical systems arise in population models, iterative optimization, and numerical simulations, where computing many iterations can be costly in high dimensions. We study Carleman embedding for forced quadratic discrete systems and its application to quantum state preparation. Under explicit stability and initial-state assumptions, we prove that the truncation error decreases geometrically with the embedding degree, uniformly over all iterations. A central obstacle in discrete time is that the lifted update can become dense even when the original coefficient maps are sparse. We overcome this obstacle by constructing an exact auxiliary linear-system representation that applies the coefficient maps one tensor factor at a time, with row and column sparsities bounded independently of the truncation degree. After rescaling, the associated history system has a condition number that grows linearly with the iteration count, uniformly in the truncation degree. These results yield a quantum algorithm for preparing an approximation to the normalized $n$th iterate with constant success probability. Under coherent input-access assumptions, and for fixed sparsity, stability parameters, and a known positive lower bound on the final-state norm, the oracle query complexity is linear in $n$ and polylogarithmic in the inverse accuracy. Numerical experiments in one and three dimensions illustrate the convergence of the truncated embedding.

</details>


### [98] [Interference-induced dynamical crossovers in quantum walks on disordered square lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35828)
*Ramnarayan Bera,Arijit Roy,Soumyadip Ghosh,Soumen Roy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在存在部分透射边的无序方格上研究经典与量子行走，发现量子输运对硬币对称性与干涉高度敏感，二维中扩散指数单调衰减，不存在量子扩散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨无序（缺边）方格上的输运行为，并区分"缺边"与"弱透射边"两种情形，以厘清量子行走输运是否可仅由边透射统计刻画。

Method: 在二维方格上分别以Fourier、Grover、Hadamard硬币实现离散时间量子行走，并与经典随机行走对比；边以概率p缺失，广义模型中将缺失边替换为透射系数f的部分透射边；在(f,p)平面绘制输运相图，标定β=1轮廓，并计算有效扩散系数、逆参与比及边透射前两阶矩；仅分析有限尺寸边界反射前的体输运。

Result: 纯缺边情形下经典与量子输运退化基本一致；引入部分透射边后，经典行走的异常扩散指数β(t)呈渐近稳定，而量子行走在初始瞬态后β(t)单调衰减，二维中量子扩散消失；β=1轮廓将亚扩散与超扩散区分开。

Conclusion: 量子输运显著依赖硬币相关的干涉效应，无法仅由透射统计完整描述；无序与实际输运性质之间的联系须显式考虑量子相干性。

Abstract: We study discrete-time quantum walks with Fourier, Grover, and Hadamard coin operators on disordered square lattices, alongside classical random walks. Edges in the lattice are absent with a probability, $p$. In this case, both classical and quantum walks exhibit an essentially identical degradation of transport. We then study a general version of this model, in which the existing edges are retained. However, the absent edges are no longer absent, but now become partially transmitting, with a transmission coefficient $f$. We find that the response is now quite sensitive to the walk protocol and coin symmetry. The classical walk exhibits asymptotic stability of the anomalous diffusion exponent, $β(t)$. In comparison, quantum walks exhibit a monotonically decaying $β(t)$ beyond the initial transient regime, leading to the absence of quantum diffusion in two dimensions. We map the transport regimes in the $(f,p)$ plane and identify the contour for $β=1$, which separates the subdiffusive and superdiffusive regimes. We also examine the effective diffusion coefficient, the inverse participation ratio, and the first two moments of edge transmission. We restrict ourselves to analyzing bulk propagation prior to finite-size boundary reflections. The results indicate that quantum transport depends significantly on coin-specific interference and cannot be fully described by transmission statistics alone.

</details>


### [99] [Network nonlocality with tripartite entangled sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35838)
*Sudip Chakrabarty,Amit Kundu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究多体纠缠源在量子网络中的非局域性与单源Mermin/Svetlichny非局域性的关系，发现该关系取决于源在网络中的"接线"方式：当两个无输入中心节点共享全部源时，Svetlichny违背（乃至所有纠缠GGHZ态）可转化为网络非局域性违背；当仅共享一个中心节点时关系反转，网络违背需要源满足Mermin违背；源数目更多时最大违背取决于奇偶性并给出紧界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往网络非局域性研究多基于双体纠缠源，而三比特纠缠源的非局域性可由Mermin或更严格的Svetlichny不等式检验。本文旨在厘清多体源所携带的这两种单源非局域概念与网络非局域性之间的对应关系，并揭示网络拓扑（接线方式）在其中的决定作用。

Method: 构造由多个独立三比特纠缠源组成的网络，考虑两种典型接线：两个无输入中心节点共享全部源，以及仅共享一个中心节点。通过Bell态测量将单源不等式（Mermin、Svetlichny）与网络不等式联系起来，并用广义GHZ（GGHZ）态检验违背范围；对多源情形按源数目奇偶分情况推导最大违背的紧界。

Result: 两个中心节点共享全部源时，Bell态测量把任意Svetlichny违背转化为网络违背，且所有纠缠GGHZ态均违背网络不等式；仅共享一个中心节点时关系反转，网络违背要求源存在Mermin违背；源数更多时最大违背依赖源数奇偶，并给出两种情形的紧界。

Conclusion: 多体源的接线方式决定了网络继承何种非局域性：全共享接线使网络非局域性等价于（甚至弱于）Svetlichny非局域性，单节点共享接线则使其受限于Mermin非局域性。该结果为网络非局域性的资源化设计与判定提供了拓扑层面的准则。

Abstract: Quantum networks are built from independent sources, and their nonlocality has mostly been studied with sources that emit bipartite entangled states. A source of three entangled qubits is richer, since its nonlocality can be tested by the Mermin or the more demanding Svetlichny inequality. We ask how network nonlocality relates to these two single-source notions and find that the answer depends on the wiring. When two central nodes without inputs share every source, Bell-state measurements turn every Svetlichny violation into a network violation, and even all entangled generalised Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger states violate the network inequality. When a single central node is shared, the relation reverses, and a network violation requires a Mermin violation of the source. With more sources, the maximal violation depends on whether their number is even or odd, and we give tight bounds for both cases. These results show that the way multipartite sources are wired decides which kind of nonlocality a network inherits.

</details>


### [100] [Complete entropy contraction from conditional hyperboundedness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35863)
*Li Gao,Lijun Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明：对与忠实条件期望相容的有限维量子信道，条件超有界性与严格GNS压缩共同蕴含相对熵压缩；对KMS对称量子Markov半群，时刻t0的条件2→q超有界性配合GNS间隙λ可给出修正对数Sobolev常数α=λ(1-2/q)/[12(λt0+log M+1-2/q)]，并在辅助系统上完全超有界时得到同速率的完全熵收缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子Markov半群的熵收缩（modified logarithmic Sobolev inequality）通常由超有界性、谱隙、GNS隙等条件推出，但已有结果多缺少"条件化"处理与"完全"（对辅助系统）版本，且对非交换设定下超有界性到熵压缩的定量关系不够精细。

Method: 在与忠实条件期望相容的有限维量子信道框架下，联合使用条件超有界性与严格GNS压缩证明相对熵收缩；对KMS对称量子Markov半群，把t0时刻的条件2→q超有界性（常数M）与GNS间隙λ结合，通过插值与超压缩技巧导出修正对数Sobolev常数α的显式估计；再借助辅助系统上的完全有界超有界性提升为完全熵收缩。

Result: 得到显式常数α=λ(1-2/q)/[12(λt0+log M+1-2/q)]，其分母同时含GNS隙、超有界时间与超有界常数M的对数，具有可跟踪的速率依赖；完全有界超有界性给出与普通版本同速率的完全熵收缩版本。

Conclusion: 条件超有界性加严格GNS压缩足以导出相对熵收缩，据此为KMS对称量子Markov半群提供可计算的修正对数Sobolev常数，并把结果完全化到辅助系统，为量子信道与半群的熵收缩研究提供统一的定量框架。

Abstract: For finite-dimensional quantum channels compatible with a faithful conditional expectation, we show that conditional hyperboundedness and strict GNS contraction together yield a relative entropy contraction. For KMS-symmetric quantum Markov semigroups, a conditional $2\to q$ hyperboundedness at time $t_0>0$ combined with a GNS gap $λ>0$ implies a modified logarithmic Sobolev constant \[ α=\frac{λ(1-2/q)}
  {12(λt_0+\log M+1-2/q)},\] where $M$ is the hyperboundedness constant. As a consequence, completely bounded hyperboundedness over auxiliary systems gives complete versions of entropy contraction with the same rates.

</details>
