### [101] [The Ising lattice gas and the surface diffusion of CO on Cu(001) at finite coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33147)
*S. Miret-Artés*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 将晶格气体相干中间散射函数（ISF）的记忆方程应用于Kawasaki动力学下的Ising晶格气体，推导静态结构因子与有效跳跃率的闭式表达，并以一维通道精确解检验热力学量对退相位速率的可读性，最终用于解释CO/Cu(001)的实测退相位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相干ISF是表征吸附粒子密度波衰减的核心量，但其弛豫行为包含记忆效应，尚不清楚仅由平衡（静态）量能否决定可测的退相位速率，以及强相互作用下记忆如何改变相关时间。需要在一可精确求解的模型上厘清"静力学—动力学"关系。

Method: 采用先前针对晶格气体导出的ISF记忆方程，施加于Kawasaki动力学的Ising晶格气体；用求和规则固定初始衰减速率，以闭式给出静态结构因子和有效跳跃率，并沿一维通道做精确解析处理；将单指数下界（仅由平衡量构成）与精确弛豫谱对比；以近似恒定时间窗拟合退相位速率，并与最慢弛豫速率比较。

Result: 沿一维通道，求和规则加闭式静力学量给出ISF的单指数下界；精确谱显示该单指数仅在弱耦合下复现相关时间，此时退相位速率可由静力学读出。强吸引下团簇缓慢溶解使相关时间延长至最多九倍；排斥层在结构峰附近近似单模弛豫。拟合的退相位速率趋近最慢弛豫速率而非初始速率；求和规则仅通过归一化衰减曲线下面积（即相关时间）体现。对CO/Cu(001)，模型复现两个晶向的实测退相位速率，无论跳跃率如何均要求最近邻排斥至少为35–50 meV，此时实测速率接近相关时间倒数，并预测记忆随覆盖度增长。

Conclusion: 静力学量仅在弱耦合极限下可直接给出退相位速率；强相互作用下记忆效应显著、相关时间由慢团簇溶解主导，求和规则经由相关时间间接约束实验。该框架为CO/Cu(001)等体系提供了可检验的定量解释，并预测记忆强度随覆盖度增强。

Abstract: The memory equation of the coherent intermediate scattering function (ISF), derived previously for lattice gases, is applied to the Ising lattice gas under Kawasaki dynamics. The ISF follows the decay of a density wave of adparticles, a superposition of exponential relaxation modes: the decay starts at a rate fixed by a sum rule and is then slowed by a memory. The static structure factor and the effective hop rate are obtained in closed form, exactly along a one-dimensional channel; with the sum rule they give a single exponential, a lower bound on the ISF. Exact relaxation spectra show that this single exponential, built from equilibrium quantities alone, reproduces the correlation time only at weak coupling; only there can a measured dephasing rate be read through the statics. For strong attraction the slow dissolution of clusters lengthens the correlation time up to ninefold, whereas a repulsive layer relaxes nearly as a single mode at its structure peak. The dephasing rate, fitted over a time window where it is nearly constant, approaches the slowest relaxation rate, not the initial one; the sum rule reaches the data only through the area beneath the normalized decay, which defines the correlation time. For CO/Cu(001), the lattice gas reproduces the dephasing rates measured along two crystal directions and requires a nearest-neighbour repulsion of at least $35$--$50$~meV, whatever the jump rates; the measured rate is then close to the inverse correlation time, and the memory is predicted to grow with coverage.

</details>


### [102] [Optimal Search Strategies under Finite Resource Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33316)
*Hugues Meyer*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究有限资源预算下的目标搜索问题，推导出一维扩散搜索者在力、半反射旗标与自趋化线索三类资源下的精确最优分配方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究通常把搜索资源建模为有代价但数量无限，而现实中资源往往只能有限产生，需要明确如何在搜索全过程中分配有限预算。

Method: 给一维扩散搜索者配置三种有限资源（外部力、部分反射旗标、自趋化线索），分别针对可获取自身过去与当前位置信息的"有信息搜索"和"盲搜索"，推导精确的最优资源分配协议。

Result: 有信息搜索的最优协议总要求资源在空间上集中投放；盲搜索则要求资源使用更均匀分布。产生环境记忆的非马尔可夫线索在有位置信息时能显著提升性能，即使在有限资源约束下亦然；但对盲搜索收益有限，尤其当线索不提供方向信息时。

Conclusion: 有限资源约束下，最优资源分配策略取决于搜索者是否掌握位置信息；环境记忆型线索的价值高度依赖信息可得性。

Abstract: Improving target search is a challenge faced by many natural and artificial agents, often requiring the use of energetic or material resources. These inputs may be supplied by an external controller or generated by the searcher itself, for example, through the deposition of cues along its trajectory. Although such resources are commonly modeled as costly but available without an explicit limit, in practice, they can often only be produced in finite amounts. Here, we go beyond the assumption of an unlimited supply of resources by investigating searches in which a finite resource budget must be allocated throughout the search process. To investigate this problem, we equip a one-dimensional diffusive searcher with three types of resources: an external force, a supply of partially reflecting flags, and auto-chemotactic cues. We derive exact optimal resource-allocation protocols for informed search processes in which the agent has continuous access to its past and current positions, which we find to always require resource allocation to be spatially concentrated. We then determine the corresponding protocols for blind searchers and show that resource usage must be more evenly distributed. Non-Markovian cues that generate environmental memory are found to substantially improve search performance when the agent has access to positional information, even under finite-resource constraints. In contrast, their benefits are considerably more limited for blind search, particularly when the cues provide no directional information.

</details>


### [103] [Distributional statistics of nonequilibrium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33383)
*Harrison Hartle,Aanjaneya Kumar,David H. Wolpert*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文将Crooks的hyperensemble思想推广，在单纯形上以最大不确定性（Jaynes）原则构造带约束的随机概率分布参考模型，并以平均能量和Shannon熵为约束研究非平衡统计性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要一个通用的、与Jaynes最大熵逻辑和贝叶斯推断兼容的参考建模框架，用于刻画受约束的随机概率分布及可调控的非平衡情形，而不仅限于Boltzmann-Gibbs平衡。

Method: 推广hyperensemble方法，在类别分布的单位单纯形上定义最大不确定性Jaynes先验；对平均能量约束得到等能超平面上的均匀密度hyperensemble；引入Shannon熵约束获得可调非平衡的参数化族；并用解析方法与精确采样算法计算均值、众数微状态分布及随机分布的期望自由能。

Result: 平均能量约束唯一确定Boltzmann-Gibbs平衡并定义超平面子集，其hyperensemble在各分布上均匀（与到平衡的距离无关）；给出非平衡性质（均值、众数分布、期望自由能）的解析与采样结果；熵约束实现可调非平衡；最大化Shannon熵时退化为Boltzmann-Gibbs统计。

Conclusion: 该框架遵循标准Jaynes建模逻辑、兼容贝叶斯推断，并与其他非平衡建模方法广泛兼容；通过超统计比较、随机非平衡稳态生成和超先验下贝叶斯推断三个场景加以说明，并指出理论推广与实证应用的前景。

Abstract: We establish a reference modeling framework for random probability distributions subject to constraints. Generalizing the hyperensemble approach of Crooks, we formulate Jaynesian priors of maximal uncertainty over the unit simplex of categorical distributions. This framework is entirely general, but we primarily investigate models with constraints on physically relevant quantities, namely, average energy and Shannon entropy. A constraint on the exact value of average energy uniquely determines a Boltzmann-Gibbs equilibrium, but also defines a hyperplanar subset of the probability simplex consisting of all distributions with that value of average energy. The associated hyperensemble has a uniform probability density across all such distributions, regardless of distance to equilibrium. Such reference models make predictions of generic nonequilibrium properties such as the mean and modal microstate distributions and the expected free energy of a random distribution, which we examine analytically and via exact sampling algorithms. Moreover, introducing constraints on Shannon entropy permits parametric access to tunably nonequilibrium regimes. The approach follows conventional Jaynesian modeling logic and is compatible with standard Bayesian inference, and reduces to Boltzmann-Gibbs statistics when Shannon entropy is maximized. We examine the physical predictions of this scheme, and demonstrate its wide compatibility with other approaches to nonequilibrium modeling. We illustrate the framework in three scenarios: (i) comparison to superstatistics quasi-equilibrium modeling, (ii) generation of random nonequilibrium steady states, and (iii) Bayesian inference under a prior hyperensemble. We highlight numerous prospects for theoretical extensions and empirical application.

</details>


### [104] [Large spin large deviations for interacting particle systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33523)
*Cristian Giardinà,Tomohiro Sasamoto*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出在格点上通过大自旋极限（s→∞）分析相互作用粒子系统样本路径大偏差的新框架，显式给出路径空间速率函数，并与经典可积自旋链及宏观涨落理论（MFT）对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MFT在空间连续极限下存在紫外奇异性与密度爆破等问题，需要一个自然正则化的格点类比框架，同时将微观粒子系统的罕见轨迹代价显式量化。

Method: 对变量做适当缩放使过程集中到由格点扩散方程支配的确定性轨迹；基于Feng–Kurtz理论，从缩放后的微观生成元显式计算路径空间大偏差速率函数，以格点上经典自旋变量的哈密顿量表示；辅以Poisson噪声MSR计算直接从微观动力学导出非线性格点哈密顿量，用相对熵论证获得密度–流作用量，并建立格点涨落定理及其对称性起源。

Result: 对称调和过程对应的哈密顿量被识别为经典可积非紧致Ishimori自旋链；大自旋大偏差理论与传统MFT完全一致，在空间连续极限下平滑恢复标准MFT哈密顿量；并将方案应用于部分对称排斥过程、对称包含过程、Brownian能量过程与可积热传导模型，其大偏差哈密顿量分别对应经典紧致与非紧致自旋链。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了MFT的自然正则化格点类比，规避了空间紫外奇异性与可能的连续密度爆破，并把多种相互作用粒子系统的大偏差哈密顿量与经典（紧致/非紧致）自旋链统一联系起来。

Abstract: We propose a novel framework for analyzing the sample-path large deviations of interacting particle systems directly on a lattice by considering a large-spin limit ($s \to \infty$). Through an appropriate scaling of variables, the process concentrates around its deterministic trajectory, governed by the lattice diffusion equation. We quantify the exponential cost of observing atypical trajectories by explicitly computing the path-space large deviation rate function from the scaled microscopic generator, based on a theory due to Feng and Kurtz. The rate function is described in terms of a Hamiltonian on a lattice in terms of classical spin variables. Notably, for our main model, the symmetric harmonic process, the corresponding Hamiltonian is identified with the classical integrable non-compact Ishimori spin chain.
  Furthermore, we show that our large spin large deviation theory is fully consistent with traditional Macroscopic Fluctuation Theory (MFT), smoothly recovering the standard MFT Hamiltonian in the spatial continuous limit. We also perform a Poisson-noise Martin--Siggia--Rose (MSR) computation to derive the non-linear lattice Hamiltonian directly from microscopic dynamics, use a relative entropy argument to obtain the density-current action, and establish the lattice fluctuation theorem alongside its symmetry origin. Overall, our framework provides a naturally regularized, lattice analogue of MFT that circumvents spatial ultraviolet singularities and possible continuous density blow-ups.
  We also apply our new scheme to partial symmetric exclusion process, symmetric inclusion processes, Brownian energy process and integrable heat conduction model, showing how their large-deviation Hamiltonians relate to classical compact and non-compact spin chains.

</details>


### [105] [Jensen-Mercer two-sided bounds for entropy production rate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34112)
*Xiao-Kan Guo,Zhiqiang Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 论文在Jensen下界（凸性结构）之外给出互补的熵产生率上界，其源自Mercer反射不等式，与Bhatia-Davis方差界及最小仿射majorant的凸对偶等价，从而得到仅由平均漂移与局域流极值控制的双边夹逼及最优界层级，并以热力学不确定性关系提供独立的涨落下限；进一步处理位置依赖扩散、欠阻尼动力学与倾斜周期势（弱势垒闭式解、强势垒渐近解），并在布朗马达上数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 熵产界能将有限观测转化为对隐藏耗散的约束，但既有Jensen型结果只给出连续随机系统总与子系统熵产率的下界，缺少与之匹配的上界，导致观测信息只能单向约束耗散。

Method: 利用与Jensen界相同的凸性结构，借助Mercer反射不等式构造互补上界；对过阻尼Langevin动力学的二次熵产泛函，证明Mercer反射不等式与sharp Bhatia-Davis方差界一致，并等于该泛函在局域流取值范围内最小仿射majorant的凸对偶；将上下界组合为由平均漂移与流极值控制的"三明治"式双边界，并组织成随观测增多而收紧的最优界层级；以热力学不确定性关系作为独立涨落下限；推广至位置依赖扩散、欠阻尼动力学及倾斜周期势（弱势垒级数闭式、强势垒渐近），最后在布朗马达上数值检验。

Result: 获得与Jensen下界互补的上界及双边夹逼结构；证明Mercer反射不等式等于sharp Bhatia-Davis方差界与最小仿射majorant的凸对偶；构建每个新增观测都收紧上限的最优界层级；倾斜周期势的弱势垒级数以闭式给出、强势垒区渐近求解；布朗马达数值结果与理论界一致。

Conclusion: 熵产率的凸性结构可同时产生下界与上界，二者仅依赖平均漂移与电流极值即可给出紧致的双边约束，并可与热力学不确定性关系互补；该框架适用于多种动力学设定与势垒区，为从有限观测量化隐藏耗散提供了系统且可加细的工具。

Abstract: Bounds on entropy production convert limited observations into constraints on hidden dissipation. The Jensen bound expresses a lower bound on the total and subsystem entropy production rates of continuous stochastic systems in terms of mean coordinate rates alone. We show that the same convexity structure yields a complementary upper bound, inspired by Mercer's reflection inequality. For the quadratic entropy-production functional of overdamped Langevin dynamics, Mercer's reflection inequality is identical to the sharp Bhatia-Davis variance bound and coincides with the convex dual of the smallest affine majorant of the functional over the range of the local current. The two bounds yield a two-sided sandwich controlled solely by the mean drift and the extrema of the current, organized into a hierarchy of optimal bounds in which each further observation tightens the ceiling. The thermodynamic uncertainty relation supplies an independent fluctuation floor. We also treat position-dependent diffusion, underdamped dynamics and the tilted periodic potential, for which the weak-barrier series is obtained in closed form and the strong-barrier regime is solved asymptotically. We check the bounds numerically on the Brownian motor.

</details>


### [106] [Extremal and global-time statistics of sluggish random walks with stochastic resetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34568)
*Theophilus Dwamena Frimpong*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究带泊松重置的1维sluggish随机游走的极值与全局时间统计：通过变量替换转化为signed Bessel过程，得到有限窗口最大值的精确Laplace变换、Gumbel极限、二阶动力学转变及最后通过时间的精确密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Brownian重置过程已知存在“单次异常长区间”与“占据整个窗口区间”两类极值来源之间的二阶动力学转变，但扩散系数随距原点幂律衰减的sluggish随机游走在重置下的极值统计尚不清楚，本文旨在将其纳入统一框架并检验该转变是否普遍成立。

Method: 对sluggish随机游走作变量替换，映射为signed Bessel过程，从而可应用更新理论（renewal theory）；推导有限窗口最大值分布的精确Laplace变换；分析长时极限、尾部大偏差率函数；并研究最大值时刻分布、最后通过原点时间的精确密度及自上次通过以来的时间分布。

Result: 长时下变换过程的中心化最大值服从Gumbel律，物理空间典型最大值随窗口长度对数呈幂律增长；远尾率函数与Bessel变量中的α无关，故Brownian重置的二阶动力学转变对任意α成立；最大值时刻在窗口内部均匀分布，边界层使均值高于中点并对数偏移；最后通过时间有精确密度，其后经过时间收敛到Gamma律；反射对称使正半轴占据时间与最后通过时间不相关；结果在Brownian极限下退化一致并与模拟吻合。

Conclusion: sluggish随机游走在Poisson重置下的极值与全局时间统计可由signed Bessel过程与更新理论精确刻画，且其定性结构（Gumbel极值、二阶动力学转变、Gamma型等待时间）对幂律指数α具有普适性，推广了Brownian重置的已知结果。

Abstract: We study the extremal and global-time statistics of a one-dimensional sluggish random walk, whose diffusivity decays as a power law of the distance from the origin, under Poissonian resetting to the origin. A change of variable maps the process onto a signed Bessel process, so that renewal theory applies. We obtain the exact Laplace transform of the distribution of the maximum over a finite window. At long times the centered maximum of the transformed process follows the Gumbel law, and in physical space the typical maximum grows as a power of the logarithm of the window length. The far-tail rate function does not depend on $α$ in the Bessel variable, so the second-order dynamical transition known for Brownian resetting, between maxima produced by a single anomalously long inter-reset interval and maxima produced by an interval occupying essentially the whole window, holds for every $α$. The time of the maximum is uniformly distributed in the bulk of the window, with boundary layers that shift its mean above the midpoint logarithmically in the window length. The last-passage time through the origin has an exact density, and the time elapsed since it converges to a Gamma law. Reflection symmetry makes the occupation time of the positive half-line and the last-passage time uncorrelated. The results reduce to the Brownian case and agree with simulations.

</details>


### [107] [Reference-state dependence and interface scaling of superfluid response in a two-dimensional crystal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34664)
*Néstor Sepúlveda*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 在二维软核GP晶体中，用张量响应、独立制备双晶与实测界面长度分离晶界与晶内对超流刚度的贡献，并建立可量化的比较协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶界同时改变输运路径与周围晶体结构，使晶界对超流刚度的贡献难以单独分离；需要区分"边界增强"与"晶内变化"。

Method: 静止二维软核Gross–Pitaevskii晶体的张量响应计算；独立制备的双晶样品；直接测量界面长度；以不同体相参考（bulk reference）做扣除，并按晶粒应变与化学势匹配比较基准；十二态家族中做288次体相替换检验；约化链接网络（reduced link network）分析方向性响应。

Result: 晶格常数1%变化使体相刚度改变约5–9%，与边界贡献量级相当；同一缺陷态下两种体相参考给出的相对超出量分别为0.027%与4.15%；匹配晶粒应变与化学势后，三个相互作用强度下的成对样品均呈正的对角超出；十二态家族中超出量对288次体相替换稳健，近似服从"长度-面积"关系；独立定面积样品对的线响应系数比三尺寸估计高3.1%；约化链接网络可关联方向响应与边界连接组织，但无法精确解析所有小的扣除后超出量。

Conclusion: 结果表明晶界增强可与周围晶体变化相区分，所建立的定量比较协议适用于缺陷晶体中的超流输运研究；长度-面积关系受尺寸与方向依赖限制，仅在特定晶体条件下成立。

Abstract: Grain boundaries modify both transport paths and the surrounding crystal, making their contribution to superfluid stiffness difficult to isolate. We quantify these contributions in stationary two-dimensional soft-core Gross--Pitaevskii (GP) crystals using tensor response, independently prepared bicrystals, and measured interface lengths. A one-percent change of lattice spacing alters the bulk stiffness by approximately 5--9\%, comparable to the boundary contribution. For the same defective state, two bulk references give relative excesses of 0.027\% and 4.15\%. Matching the comparator to grain strain and chemical potential yields positive diagonal excesses in paired preparations at three interaction strengths. Within a twelve-state family with comparable grain interiors, the excess is robust to 288 bulk substitutions and follows an approximate length-per-area relation. Independent fixed-area pairs yield a line-response coefficient 3.1\% higher than the three-size estimate. Size and directional dependence restrict this relation to the specified crystal conditions. Reduced link networks relate the directional response to the organization of boundary connections but do not accurately resolve every small bulk-subtracted excess. The results distinguish boundary enhancement from changes in the surrounding crystal and establish a quantitative comparison protocol for superfluid transport in defective crystals.

</details>


### [108] [Simplified Parisi Ansatz III: Constructive Cavity Method and Ultrametricity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34710)
*Simone Franchini*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文为Sherrington–Kirkpatrick模型的构造性腔方法（CCM）给出物理解释：将腔变量分层分解与Aizenman–Sims–Starr的随机重叠结构相对应，通过稀释缺陷气体机制导出GREM结构与重叠的超度量性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CCM此前缺乏清晰的物理图像，且早期表述存在模糊之处；作者希望为腔变量的层次分解找到物理对应，并阐明低温激发、GREM结构与超度量性之间的内在联系。

Method: 以基态为参照，用稀释的缺陷气体描述低温激发；先处理有限数目缺陷，再推广到指数级多的态；考察缺陷在后代间的共享以及沿新分支的涨落更新；在固定核下分析新鲜场的高斯协方差与腔变量（含共同分量）及决定Gibbs质量的层次之间的关系；并修正早期表述的模糊点。

Result: 缺陷在子孙态间的共享与沿新分支的涨落更新产生了GREM结构及重叠的超度量性；固定核下新鲜场的高斯协方差把完整的腔变量（含共同分量）与决定Gibbs质量的同一层次结构联系起来。

Conclusion: CCM的腔变量分层分解具有明确物理含义，可统一解释GREM结构与超度量性，并澄清了早期表述中的模糊之处。

Abstract: We present a physical explaination of the Constructive Cavity Method (CCM) for the Sherrington--Kirkpatrick model, which identifies the decomposition of the cavity variables into layers with the random overlap structure of Aizenman--Sims--Starr. We describe the low-temperature excitations through a dilute gas of defects relative to the ground state. The sharing of these defects among the descendants and the renewal of the fluctuations along new branches produce the GREM structure and the ultrametricity of the overlaps. This mechanism is developed first with a finite number of defects and then extended to exponential populations of states. At fixed kernel, the Gaussian covariances of the fresh fields connect the complete cavity variables, including their common components, to the same hierarchy that determines the Gibbs masses. We also correct some ambiguities of earlier formulations.

</details>


### [109] [Learning by Confusion Locates a Parametrization-Dependent Fisher Information Maximum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34769)
*George Issa,Richard Scalettar*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文推导出 LBC（learning by confusion）混淆峰的精确定量平衡条件，证明在单参数平衡系统中峰值紧贴 Fisher 信息极大值，从而给出其有限尺寸标度，并揭示该峰位置依赖于数据采样参数化（T、ln T、β）这一非物理因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LBC 是一种无需预设序参量即可定位相变的机器学习方法，其准确率曲线的中心极大值被解读为相变点，但该极大值的位置与有限尺寸标度由什么决定此前并不清楚，缺乏统计层面的理论解释。

Method: 解析推导 Bayes 最优混淆峰的精确平衡条件；对具有单一扫描参数的平衡系统，将峰值位置与 Fisher 信息极大值对应；分析数据采样方式（在 T、ln T、β=1/T 上均匀的网格）对有限尺寸伪临界轨迹的影响；并在 Ising、Blume-Capel、Potts、Baxter-Wu 模型上做数值验证，覆盖连续、三临界与一阶相变。

Result: 混淆峰紧跟随 Fisher 信息极大值，据此得到有限尺寸标度；同一批构型在 T、ln T、β 网格上的不同排布会产生不同的有限尺寸伪临界轨迹；四个模型的数值实验在连续、三临界和一阶相变下均验证该预测。

Conclusion: 本文明确了 LBC 所度量的统计量（本质对应 Fisher 信息），并指出有限尺寸下的机器学习相变估计不仅取决于底层物理，还依赖训练数据的参数化方式。

Abstract: Learning by confusion (LBC) is a machine-learning method for locating phase transitions without assuming a known order parameter. Its characteristic accuracy curve contains a central maximum that is interpreted as the transition point, but what determines the location and finite-size scaling of this maximum has remained unclear. We derive an exact balance condition for the Bayes-optimal confusion peak and show that, for equilibrium systems with a single swept parameter, the peak closely follows the maximum of the corresponding Fisher information. This immediately gives its finite-size scaling and reveals an unexpected dependence on how the data are sampled: the same configurations arranged on grids uniform in $T$, $\ln T$, or $β=1/T$ can yield different finite-size pseudocritical trajectories. Tests on the Ising, Blume-Capel, Potts, and Baxter-Wu models confirm the prediction across continuous, tricritical, and first-order transitions. Our results identify the statistical quantity measured by LBC and show that finite-size machine-learning estimates can depend on the parametrization of the training data as well as on the underlying physics.

</details>


### [110] [Interaction dependence of the critical correlation length amplitude ratio in anisotropic scalar lattice $φ^4$ theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35081)
*Leonie Müller,Stefan Wessel*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 在弱各向异性二维标量格点φ⁴理论中，蒙特卡洛模拟表明关联长度振幅比并非与局域相互作用强度无关，而是随耦合显著变化，并在连续极限与Ising极限之间插值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作报道关联长度振幅比对局域相互作用强度无关，该结论若成立将简化有限耦合下各向异性效应的理论描述；本文检验该结论并厘清格点效应与局域相互作用的相互关系。

Method: 针对弱各向异性二维标量格点φ⁴理论开展蒙特卡洛模拟，系统改变耦合强度，测量关联长度振幅比的耦合依赖性，并与连续极限、Ising极限的理论值对比。

Result: 模拟显示振幅比对相互作用强度存在明显依赖，随耦合增大在连续极限值与Ising极限值之间连续插值，说明此前的"无关性"结论不成立。

Conclusion: 有限耦合下各向异性效应需修正的理论描述；格点效应与局域相互作用存在非平凡耦合，该原型格点场论中振幅比的普适性假设应被放弃。

Abstract: We investigate critical correlations in the weakly anisotropic two-dimensional scalar lattice $φ^4$ theory and demonstrate that a previously reported independence of the correlation length amplitude ratio from the local interaction strength does not hold. Monte Carlo simulations instead reveal a pronounced dependence of this ratio on the interaction strength. As the coupling is increased, the amplitude ratio interpolates between the continuum and Ising limits, highlighting a nontrivial interplay between lattice effects and local interactions. These findings call for a revised theoretical description of anisotropy effects at finite coupling in this prototypical lattice field theory.

</details>


### [111] [Anomalous Mobility Enhancement in Restricted Geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35324)
*Yuval Zippin,Stanislav Burov*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 在淬火陷阱模型框架下，研究几何限域（有限横截面通道）对反常介质中受驱输运的影响，发现弱力长时极限下平均纵向位移满足 ⟨x∥(t)⟩ ∝ F∥^α t^α Ω^{-(1-α)}，即通道越窄反常迁移率越强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 淬火陷阱模型已能刻画玻璃态反常输运，但几何限域（如窄通道）如何改变受驱输运的反常迁移率尚缺乏解析认识，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在淬火陷阱模型下，粒子在格点上做偏置随机游走，淬火无序给出幂律分布陷阱时间；考虑沿一方向无限、其余 d-1 方向横截面有限的通道，在玻璃态区 α=T/T_g<1 下，于长时与弱力极限推导平均纵向位移的解析表达式，并用数值模拟在多种格点几何与边界条件下验证。

Result: 得到解析标度 ⟨x∥(t)⟩ ∝ F∥^α t^α Ω^{-(1-α)}（Ω 为横向截面格点数），表明反常迁移率随通道变窄而增强；结果适用于 d 维一般 Bravais 格点及周期性横向边界条件，数值模拟在不同格点几何下定量吻合，且在反射边界条件下亦定量一致。

Conclusion: 几何限域通过横向截面尺寸显著调控玻璃态中的反常受驱输运：狭窄通道增强反常迁移率，该标度律具有对格点结构与横向边界条件的普适性。

Abstract: We investigate the effect of geometrical confinement on driven transport in anomalous media within the framework of the quenched trap model. The particle performs a biased random walk on a lattice, where quenched disorder introduces power-law distributed trapping times. We consider channels that are infinite along one direction and have a finite cross-section in the remaining $d-1$ directions. In the glassy regime, where $α=T/T_g<1$, we derive an analytical expression for the mean longitudinal displacement in the long-time and weak-force limits. We find the scaling $\langle x_\parallel(t)\rangle \propto F_\parallel^αt^αΩ^{-(1-α)}$, where $F_\parallel>0$ is the effective longitudinal force and $Ω$ is the number of sites in a transverse cross-section. Thus, the anomalous mobility is enhanced for narrower channels. Our analytical result applies to general Bravais lattices in $d$ dimensions with periodic transverse boundary conditions. Numerical simulations confirm the analytical predictions for different lattice geometries and show quantitative agreement even for channels with reflecting boundary conditions.

</details>


### [112] [Microscopic Derivation of Stochastic Thin-Film Equation and Extensions to Two-Layer and Evaporating Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35422)
*Toby Kay,Serafim Kalliadasis*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文从底层N粒子哈密顿动力学出发，通过广义涨落动态密度泛函理论进行系统粗粒化，直接推导出随机薄膜方程，为纳米尺度界面动力学提供第一性原理建模框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典薄膜流体力学模型在宏观尺度适用，但无法描述支配纳米尺度界面动力学的分子级热涨落；现有随机扩展依赖连续涨落Navier-Stokes方程的长波近似，属于自上而下范式，缺乏与微观粒子轨迹的形式联系，且难以处理复杂的非平衡边界机制。

Method: 采用基于广义涨落动态密度泛函理论（fluctuating dynamical density functional theory）的系统性自下而上粗粒化框架，从精确的N粒子哈密顿动力学直接推导随机薄膜方程。

Result: 该第一性原理表述具有高度灵活性，可无缝整合多层界面与非守恒动力学（如蒸发）；针对此类复杂构型，严格导出全新的随机薄膜方程，其中包含经典自上而下模型所缺失的、刻画微观涨落的涌现噪声项。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了随机薄膜方程与微观粒子动力学的形式联系，突破了传统长波近似的限制，为含复杂界面与非平衡过程的纳米尺度薄膜动力学提供了严格的粗粒化建模途径。

Abstract: While classical thin-film hydrodynamics models widely govern macroscopic fluid layers, they fail to capture the molecular-level thermal fluctuations that dictate nanoscale interfacial dynamics. Standard stochastic extensions rely on the long-wave approximation of the continuum fluctuating Navier-Stokes equations; however, this top-down paradigm lacks a formal connection to microscopic particle trajectories and cannot readily accommodate complex, non-equilibrium boundary mechanisms. We resolve this limitation by deriving the stochastic thin-film equation directly from the exact underlying N-particle Hamiltonian dynamics. This is achieved via a systematic, bottom-up coarse-graining framework based on a generalized fluctuating dynamic density-functional theory. Crucially, this first principles formulation offers unparalleled flexibility, enabling the seamless integration of multi-layered interfaces and non-conserved dynamics such as evaporation. For these complex configurations, our framework rigorously yields entirely novel stochastic thin-film equations featuring emergent noise terms that encapsulate microscopic fluctuations missing from classical top-down models.

</details>


### [113] [Information-thermodynamic principles unveil the response of complex networks to perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35667)
*Andrea Filippo Beretta,Manlio De Domenico*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 标准扩散在最大熵扰动先验下无法增大响应熵；作者提出基于密度矩阵的信息热力学框架，用最小耗散原理与广义自由能刻画沿任意动力学轨迹的扰动响应多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示复杂网络对扰动的响应机制是理解其功能组织的关键，而标准扩散模型在最大熵扰动先验下不能提升响应熵（系统响应多样性），构成理论局限。

Method: 首先证明在最大熵扰动先验下标准扩散不会增大响应熵；随后构建基于密度矩阵的扰动响应信息热力学，适用于任意动力学轨迹，其基础为信息论意义下的最小耗散原理，并引入量化相对瞬时扩散参考偏离的广义自由能。

Result: 形式化框架在非线性复杂网络上得到验证，识别出标准扩散描述无法捕捉的响应多样性增强区间。

Conclusion: 密度矩阵信息热力学为超越扩散极限的扰动响应分析提供了统一工具，可刻画非线性复杂网络中的响应多样性增强机制。

Abstract: Understanding how complex networks respond to perturbations is key to uncovering their functional organization. We show that, for a maximum-entropy perturbation prior, standard diffusion cannot increase response entropy, a measure of the diversity of the system's response. To overcome this limitation, we develop a density-matrix-based information thermodynamics of perturbation responses along arbitrary dynamical trajectories. We show that this framework is grounded in an information-theoretic principle of least dissipation and introduces a generalized free energy that quantifies deviations from an instantaneous diffusive reference. We then illustrate the formalism using nonlinear complex networks, revealing regimes of enhanced response diversity that cannot be captured by standard diffusive descriptions.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.str-el'></div>

# cond-mat.str-el [[Back]](#toc)

### [114] [Cooperation of itinerant and localized physics in the magnetic phase diagram of 214 Ruddlesden-Popper iridates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33196)
*Francesco Cassol,Michele Casula,Benjamin Lenz*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在动力学平均场框架下计算含自旋轨道耦合的三轨道$t_{2g}$ Hubbard模型的相互作用-温度相图，发现Sr$_2$IrO$_4$与Ba$_2$IrO$_4$处于巡游与局域电子协同稳定反铁磁序的中间区间，且光学导率双峰结构是该协同区的可靠指纹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $5d^5$ Ruddlesden-Popper铱氧化物Sr$_2$IrO$_4$和Ba$_2$IrO$_4$被视为铜氧化物的自旋轨道纠缠类比物，但其反铁磁绝缘态的起源究竟是Slater型还是Mott-Heisenberg型长期存在争议，实验与理论报道相互矛盾。

Method: 在动力学平均场理论（DMFT）中构建包含自旋轨道耦合的realistic三轨道$t_{2g}$ Hubbard模型，计算相互作用-温度相图，并系统考察能隙在Néel温度附近的行为、谱函数中的自旋极化子特征以及光学导率，用以判别不同机制区。

Result: 两种材料均位于巡游与局域电子协同稳定反铁磁序的中间区域，Ba$_2$IrO$_4$的关联强度略高于Sr$_2$IrO$_4$；能隙跨Néel温度的行为与谱函数中的自旋极化子特征均无法唯一判别协同区，而光学导率的双峰结构仅在协同区出现，可作为可靠指纹。

Conclusion: 将两种化合物置于统一相图中，协调了看似矛盾的实验与理论结果，为214型铱氧化物及其他自旋轨道耦合关联材料提供了统一研究框架。

Abstract: The $5d^5$ Ruddlesden-Popper iridates Sr$_2$IrO$_4$ and Ba$_2$IrO$_4$ have attracted considerable attention as spin-orbit entangled analogues of the cuprates, yet the origin of their antiferromagnetic insulating state, of Slater or of Mott-Heisenberg type, remains debated. We find that both materials sit in an intermediate regime in which itinerant and localized electrons cooperate in stabilizing the antiferromagnetic order, with Ba$_2$IrO$_4$ slightly more correlated than Sr$_2$IrO$_4$. We obtain this by computing, within dynamical mean-field theory, the interaction-temperature phase diagram of a realistic three-orbital $t_{2g}$ Hubbard model with spin-orbit coupling. Our study shows that neither the behavior of the gap across the Néel temperature nor the spin-polaron features of the spectral function unambiguously identify the cooperative regime, whereas the two-peak structure of the optical conductivity is reproduced only there and therefore constitutes a reliable fingerprint. By placing both compounds in a common phase diagram, our results reconcile seemingly contradictory experimental and theoretical reports, and provide a unified framework for future studies of the 214 iridates and of other spin-orbit coupled correlated materials.

</details>


### [115] [Exact Magnetic Confinement of a Doped Hole by Kinetic Frustration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33585)
*Nikolai Peshcherenko,Yixin Zhang,Cristian D. Batista,Yang Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在一维有限开边界锯齿轮U=∞ Hubbard链中，单空穴掺杂下由动力学阻挫（kinetic frustration）驱动了精确的磁约束机制：磁场选定极化三重态二聚体数目M，从而决定singlet区长度ℓ_s=L−M与空穴的约束长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统磁约束（如禁闭、弦张力）通常依赖无序或交换作用产生的弦张力。作者希望说明约束可以不依赖这些因素，而仅由动能项的阻挫与干涉效应产生，并给出一个可精确求解的机制。

Method: 研究有限开放锯齿轮U=∞ Hubbard链（L个单胞、掺入一个空穴），在磁化M下的基态分析。解析刻画基态结构：M个极化的三重态二聚体包围长度ℓ_s=L−M的连通singlet区；空穴在singlet区内形成驻波并在三重态背景中带指数尾巴。通过直接跃迁与顶点(apex)介导跃迁的相消干涉使三重态键成为反射边界；在特定t_2/t_1条件下实现完全反射。

Result: 当t_2/t_1=[2+4cos(π/(ℓ_s+2))]^{−1/2}时，三重态键完全反射，形成位置简并的紧致局域态（compact localized state）。对每个固定场0<h<h_sat，singlet区在L→∞时保持有限长度，并随磁场增大阶梯式收缩；而在h=0时singlet区延展至整个系统。

Conclusion: 磁约束可由纯动能阻挫精确实现：磁场作为调控参数选择约束长度，三重态背景充当可调的反射壁，无需无序或交换弦张力；在临界跳迁比下出现完全反射与紧致局域化。

Abstract: We establish an exact mechanism of magnetic confinement driven purely by kinetic frustration. In a finite open sawtooth $U=\infty$ Hubbard chain of $L$ unit cells doped with one hole, the ground state at magnetization $M$ contains $M$ polarized triplet dimers surrounding a connected singlet region of length $\ell_{\rm s}=L-M$. A magnetic field selects $M$ and hence the confinement length. The hole forms a standing wave in the singlet region with exponential tails in the triplet background. Confinement requires neither disorder nor an exchange-generated string tension: destructive interference between direct and apex-mediated hopping makes the triplet bonds reflecting. At $t_2/t_1=[2+4\cos(π/(\ell_{\rm s}+2))]^{-1/2}$, they become perfectly reflecting, producing a position-degenerate compact localized state. At every fixed field $0<h<h_{\rm sat}$, the singlet region remains finite as $L\to\infty$ and shrinks stepwise with increasing field, whereas at $h=0$ it spans the system.

</details>


### [116] [Fermionic superfluid transition and pairing scale separation in the kagome flat band](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34342)
*Xiang Li,Yu Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 利用FFT加速与延迟更新的大规模行列式量子蒙特卡罗，研究kagome吸引Hubbard模型平带填充ρ=5/3处的超流相变，给出有限温度相图，发现Tc线性标度Tc=0.0314|U|，并揭示预配对形成的宽中间超可压缩玻色流体区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平带系统因动能淬灭、相互作用效应增强，是探索奇异关联相的理想平台；但平带填充下超流相变的有限温度相图、配对与相位相干温标的分离机制尚不清楚。

Method: 采用快速傅里叶变换与延迟更新加速的大规模行列式量子蒙特卡罗（DQMC），系统计算转变温度、自旋磁化率与密度压缩率，构建平带填充ρ=5/3下的有限温度相图，并与van Hove填充对比。

Result: 超流转变温度随相互作用强度线性标度：Tc=0.0314|U|；局域配对形成与全局相位相干之间存在宽温标分离，稳定出连接正常费米气体与平带超流的广阔中间“超可压缩”预配对玻色流体区；平带中的Tc与密度压缩率均显著高于van Hove填充。

Conclusion: 平带增强的配对密度涨落是分离配对温标与相位相干温标的关键机制，为冷原子及kagome材料平台探测平带超流/超导提供明确实验判据。

Abstract: Flat-band systems provide a promising platform for exploring exotic correlated phases owing to quenched kinetic energy and enhanced interaction effects. In this work, we investigate the flat-band superfluid transition in the attractive kagome Hubbard model using large-scale determinant quantum Monte Carlo simulations accelerated by fast Fourier transforms and delay updates. Through systematic calculations of the transition temperature, spin susceptibility, and density compressibility, we establish the finite-temperature phase diagram at the flat-band filling $ρ=5/3$ and find a clear linear scaling of the superfluid transition temperature with the interaction strength, $T_{\mathrm{c}}=0.0314|U|$. Our results reveal a wide separation of temperature scales between local pair formation and global phase coherence, which stabilizes a broad intermediate supercompressible bosonic fluid region of preformed pairs that bridges the normal Fermi gas and the flat-band superfluid. We further demonstrate that both the transition temperature and density compressibility are significantly enhanced in the flat band compared to van Hove fillings. Our findings highlight the essential role of flat-band-enhanced pair-density fluctuations in separating the pairing and phase-coherence scales, offering clear experimental signatures for probing flat-band superfluidity and superconductivity in cold-atom and kagome-material platforms.

</details>


### [117] [Tuning from intrinsic to disorder-dominated quantum states in a kagome antiferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34230)
*Y. Tanaka,R. Kumakura,T. Yamaguchi,M. Kato,K. Totsuka,B. Li,Y. Zhao,Y. Li,R. Grasset,O. Cavani,A. Alessi,V. Sharma,Y. Kohama,S. Suetsugu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过在YCOB中引入约0.05%的电子辐照点缺陷，发现1/9与1/3磁化平台被强烈抑制，并观察到缺陷诱导关联磁矩及磁比热标度行为的渐进涌现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋1/2 kagome海森堡反铁磁体是研究量子自旋液体与磁化平台的理想平台，但结构无序阻碍实验进展，且无序对这些量子态的影响尚不清楚。需要借助可控无序连接本征区与无序主导区，并识别与无序相关的新兴自由度。

Method: 对自旋1/2 kagome反铁磁体YCu3(OH)6.5Br2.5（YCOB）施加电子辐照以引入可控点缺陷，并通过高场磁转矩测量与磁比热测量追踪其低能磁态的演化。

Result: 仅约0.05%的辐照缺陷即强烈抑制1/9和1/3磁化平台，表明这些精细量子多体态对稀磁无序极其脆弱；辐照仅在低温低场下引起磁转矩符号反转，指示存在关联的缺陷诱导磁矩而非孤立自由自旋；随无序增加，原本在纯净YCOB中缺失的磁比热特征标度逐渐出现。

Conclusion: 稀磁无序可作为有效调控参量，驱动kagome反铁磁体从本征量子态进入无序主导区；对比响应同时表明纯净YCOB是研究本征量子态的极干净kagome反铁磁体。

Abstract: The spin-1/2 kagome Heisenberg antiferromagnet (AFM) provides a paradigmatic platform for realizing quantum spin-liquid (QSL) and magnetization-plateau states. Experimental progress, however, has been hindered by structural disorder, whose effects on these quantum states remain elusive. A key experimental challenge is therefore to connect intrinsic and disorder-dominated regimes through controlled disorder and identify emergent degrees of freedom associated with disorder. Here we introduce controlled point defects through electron irradiation into the spin-1/2 kagome AFM YCu3(OH)6.5Br2.5 (YCOB) and track the resulting evolution of its low-energy magnetic states. High-field magnetic torque measurements reveal that the 1/9 and 1/3 magnetization plateaus are strongly suppressed by only ~0.05% irradiation-induced defects, demonstrating that these delicate quantum many-body states are extraordinarily fragile against dilute disorder. Moreover, the irradiation induces a sign reversal of the magnetic torque only at low temperatures and low fields, indicating the emergence of correlated defect-induced moments rather than isolated free spins. Remarkably, the characteristic scaling of the magnetic specific heat, absent in pristine YCOB, progressively emerges with increasing disorder. These findings establish dilute disorder as an effective tuning parameter that drives the kagome AFM from intrinsic quantum states toward a disorder-dominated regime. The contrasting responses further identify pristine YCOB as an exceptionally clean kagome AFM for accessing intrinsic quantum states.

</details>


### [118] [Controllable charge-spin conversion in sliding multiferroic metal Fe3GaTe2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34295)
*Tongrui Li,Lidong Zhang,Weiyi Pan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文用第一性原理、对称性分析与Wannier线性响应，研究滑动铁电双层Fe3GaTe2的Edelstein与自旋霍尔响应，揭示磁化方向与铁电极化共同决定电荷-自旋转换的分量选择性与符号反转，并给出高自旋霍尔电导与高阶角向谐波。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在单一磁性材料内实现电荷-自旋转换的可控调控，对紧凑型自旋电子器件至关重要；滑动铁电双层Fe3GaTe2同时具备铁电与磁性，具备作为可重构平台的潜力，但其Edelstein与自旋霍尔响应的对称性与微观机制尚不清晰。

Method: 第一性原理计算 + 对称性分析 + 基于Wannier函数的线性响应方法；计算滑动铁电双层的双稳态、切换势垒与偶极矩；分析磁化取向与铁电极化对允许的自旋分量及符号反转的约束（涉及M_z T与M_z对称性）；进行动量分辨分析，区分TR-even与TR-odd的Edelstein响应及其对应的自旋Berry曲率/费米面特征；考察xy与xz面内磁化旋转下的角向谐波。

Result: 两个具有相反层间滑移矢量与面外偶极矩的简并基态，切换势垒约16.3 meV/单胞；磁化方向决定允许的电流诱导自旋分量，铁电切换诱导分量选择性符号反转（面内磁化由M_z T支配，面外磁化由M_z支配）；Edelstein响应强能量依赖，m||+y时在1 V/Å参考场下自旋积累接近2ħ；TR-even响应关联Γ心等能轮廓，TR-odd关联局域带间特征；自旋电导贡献的动量空间特征相反；横向自旋霍尔电导约10^3 (ħ/e)(S/cm)，强烈依赖铁电态与磁化方向；xy/xz面磁化旋转呈现对称性允许的高阶角向谐波。

Conclusion: 滑动铁电双层Fe3GaTe2是一个可实现可重构自旋积累与自旋流产生的可调平台，铁电极化与磁化取向为电荷-自旋转换提供多重调控自由度。

Abstract: Controlling charge--spin conversion within a single magnetic material is important for compact spintronic devices. Using first-principles calculations, symmetry analysis, and Wannier-based linear response, we study the Edelstein and spin Hall responses of sliding-ferroelectric bilayer \ce{Fe3GaTe2}. Two degenerate ground states with opposite interlayer sliding vectors and out-of-plane dipole moments are separated by a calculated switching barrier of about $16.3$~meV per unit cell. Magnetization orientation determines the allowed current-induced spin components, while ferroelectric switching induces component-selective sign reversals governed by $M_z\mathcal T$ for in-plane magnetization and by $M_z$ for out-of-plane magnetization. The Edelstein response is strongly energy dependent, with spin accumulation approaching $2~\hbar$ for $\mathbf m\parallel+y$ under a reference field of $1$~V/Å. Momentum-resolved analysis links the TR-even and TR-odd Edelstein responses to $Γ$-centered constant-energy contours and localized interband features, respectively. Spin-conductivity contributions show the opposite momentum-space character, with broad sign-competing spin Berry curvature for TR-even and narrow Fermi-surface contours for TR-odd. The transverse spin Hall conductivity reaches $\sim10^{3}\,(\hbar/e)(\mathrm{S/cm})$ and depends strongly on ferroelectric state and magnetization direction. Magnetization rotations in the $xy$ and $xz$ planes reveal symmetry-allowed higher angular harmonics in both spin accumulation and spin-current responses. These results establish sliding-ferroelectric bilayer \ce{Fe3GaTe2} as a tunable platform for reconfigurable spin accumulation and spin-current generation.

</details>


### [119] [Theory of the $1/9$ Magnetization Plateau of the Spin-$1/2$ Kagome Heisenberg Antiferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34439)
*Pratyay Ghosh,Frédéric Mila*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 利用iPEPS研究自旋1/2笼目海森堡反铁磁体的1/9磁化平台，发现具有27格点单胞的自旋密度波态，其变分能量低于以往方案；该平台仅保持三重旋转对称，由三类9格点团簇（±2π/3旋转相关联）镶嵌而成，与高磁化平台六重对称的局域磁振子图像不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高磁化平台已由非重叠六边形上的局域磁振子良好描述并保持六重旋转对称，但1/9平台的结构与对称性尚不清楚，需要确定其基态磁序与单胞，并检验对称性是否自发破缺。

Method: 采用无限投影纠缠对态（iPEPS）计算1/9平台变分基态，获得27格点单胞的自旋密度波解；并用团簇平均场理论作为互补手段，检验高平台的对称性保持与1/9平台的自发对称破缺及九种局域磁化模式。

Result: iPEPS给出27格点单胞的自旋密度波态，变分能量低于先前方法；1/9平台仅保留三重旋转对称，呈由三类9格点团簇（经±2π/3旋转关联）构成的镶嵌结构，并具有九种不同的局域磁化强度。团簇平均场正确保持高平台对称性，但对1/9平台给出相同的自发对称破缺和定性相似的磁化图样。

Conclusion: 1/9磁化平台并非高平台局域磁振子图像的直接延伸，而是一个三重对称、27格点单胞的自旋密度波态，伴随自发六重→三重对称破缺；iPEPS与团簇平均场的一致结果增强了该结论的可靠性。

Abstract: We investigate the $1/9$ magnetization plateau of the spin-$1/2$ kagome Heisenberg antiferromagnet using infinite projected entangled-pair states and identify a spin-density-wave state with a $27$-site unit cell and a lower variational energy than previous approaches. In contrast to higher-magnetization plateaus, which are well described by localized magnons on nonoverlapping hexagons and preserve a sixfold rotational symmetry, the $1/9$ plateau only keeps a threefold rotational symmmetry and consists of a tiling by three types of $9$-site clusters related by $\pm2π/3$ rotations. These conclusions are supported by a complementary cluster mean-field theory which correctly preserves the symmetry of the higher plateaus but leads to the same spontaneous symmetry breaking for the $1/9$ plateau with a qualitatively similar pattern of nine different local magnetizations.

</details>


### [120] [Symmetry-resolved quasilocal entanglement as a key to Mott and pseudogap physics in plaquette CDMFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34470)
*Gabriele Bellomia,Carlos Mejuto-Zaera,Massimo Capone,Adriano Amaricci*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文提出两格点纠缠（费米子negativity与相对熵纠缠）的对称性分辨方案，用于识别强关联费米子系统中的内禀量子资源，并应用于Hubbard模型的研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在强关联费米子体系中，需识别真正的内禀量子资源，而传统纠缠度量难以区分不同对称性扇区的贡献，因此需要对称性分辨的纠缠度量。

Method: 引入宇称超选择（parity-superselected）两格点纠缠的对称性分辨，基于费米子negativity与相对熵纠缠；将方案推广至纯态与混合两格点态的全纠缠；应用于Hubbard模型，先研究二聚体，再用团簇动力学平均场（CDMFT）研究plaquette团簇。

Result: 在零温下追踪相互作用驱动与密度驱动的Mott转变，展示两格点纠缠的三个对称性分辨分量分别探测Mott物理与赝能隙物理的不同关键方面。

Conclusion: 对称性分辨的两格点纠缠可作为区分Mott与赝能隙物理中不同量子资源来源的精细诊断工具。

Abstract: In order to identify intrinsic quantum resources in strongly interacting fermionic systems, we introduce a symmetry resolution of the parity-superselected two-site entanglement, as measured by both the fermionic negativity and the relative entropy of entanglement. We discuss how the symmetry resolution can be extended also to the full entanglement in pure and mixed two-site states. We apply our recipe to the Hubbard model, first for a dimer, and then for a plaquette cluster dynamical mean-field theory, an established method tailored to accurately capture short-range correlations. Following the evolution of the model through the interaction- and density-driven Mott transitions at zero temperature, we highlight how the three symmetry-resolved components of the two-site entanglement probe different key aspects of Mott and pseudogap physics.

</details>


### [121] [Quasiparticle Interactions in the $S = 1/2$ Heisenberg Antiferromagnetic Chain CuPzN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34536)
*Leshan Zhao,Timothy Reeder,Matthew Stone,Daniel G. Mazzone,Jakob Lass,Christof Niedermayer,Daniel H. Reich,Anna Keselman,Oleg A. Starykh,Collin Broholm*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 利用非弹性中子散射研究准一维S=1/2海森堡反铁磁体CuPzN中自旋子-自旋子相互作用；磁场下在q→0处出现能隙2Δ，直接标定自旋子背散射相互作用强度，并与MPS计算及解析理论定量吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 各向同性量子自旋体系中准粒子相互作用通常难以实验量化；一维海森堡链是可积模型，其自旋子相互作用的实验标定缺乏直接范例。

Method: 对Cu(C4H4N2)(NO3)2进行非弹性中子散射测量：零场下观测多自旋子连续谱，加场后测量均匀磁化进动模与移动的自旋子连续谱之间在q→0方向的能隙2Δ；与数值矩阵乘积态（MPS）计算及近期解析理论对比。

Result: 零场谱呈现典型多自旋子连续谱；随磁场增大，q→0处出现能隙2Δ，其大小直接量度自旋子背散射相互作用；实验与MPS数值及解析理论高度一致。

Conclusion: 以一维S=1/2海森堡链为例，证明各向同性量子自旋体系中准粒子相互作用可通过中子散射实验定量刻画，2Δ为自旋子背散射相互作用的直接实验指标。

Abstract: We investigate spinon--spinon interactions in the quasi-one-dimensional $S=1/2$ Heisenberg antiferromagnet Cu(C$_4$H$_4$N$_2$)(NO$_3$)$_2$ (CuPzN) using inelastic neutron scattering. At zero field, the spectrum exhibits the canonical multi-spinon continuum of the Heisenberg chain. With increasing field we demonstrate the appearance of an energy gap $2Δ$ towards momentum $q\rightarrow 0$ between the uniform magnetization precession mode and the shifted spinon continuum. $2Δ$ is a direct measure of spinon backscattering interactions. Comparison with numerical matrix-product-state calculations and a recent analytical theory shows excellent agreement. By examining the integrable spin-1/2 chain, we provide an example of how quasiparticle interactions can be quantified experimentally in isotropic quantum spin systems.

</details>


### [122] [Spontaneous magnetic diode effect in Co$_{1/3}$TaS$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34586)
*Yining Hu,Taikang Chen,Jan Priessnitz,Zili Feng,Takashi Kurumaji,Linda Ye,Libor Šmejkal,Chunyu Mark Guo,Philip J. W. Moll*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在晶格与磁序手性可独立反转的三角格子反铁磁体Co₁/₃TaS₂中，观测到由非共面自旋织构（净磁化为零）与自旋轨道耦合共同产生的自发磁二极管效应；反转磁手性即可反转非互易电阻的符号，实现磁控二极管极性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属中传导电子与磁序通过电荷和自旋耦合，但其输运机制几乎总是互易的，电阻不依赖电流方向。常规电学磁手性各向异性需外磁场驱动，作者希望探索能否由材料内部磁序自身产生非互易（二极管）输运，并实现非结构性的、可由磁序调控的方向性。

Method: 选取手性晶格承载自身手性磁序的三角格子反铁磁体Co₁/₃TaS₂，利用晶格手性与磁手性可独立反转的特点，测量非互易电阻；结合对称性分析与模型计算，考察非共面自旋织构与自旋轨道耦合的协同作用，并通过反转磁手性检验电阻符号变化。

Result: 观察到显著的非互易电阻；与由外磁场驱动的常规电学磁手性各向异性不同，该效应源自净磁化消失的内部非共面自旋织构。对称性分析与模型计算表明其源于非共面自旋序与自旋轨道耦合的相互作用，产生手性、破坏时间反演对称的电子响应。反转磁手性会使非互易电阻符号反转。

Conclusion: 手性晶格与可独立反转的手性磁序协同，可产生正向由磁性（而非结构）决定的自发磁二极管效应，实现磁手性对二极管极性的调控，为自旋电子学中非互易输运与磁控功能提供了新途径。

Abstract: When electrons move through non-trivial spin textures in magnetic metals, conduction electrons couple to magnetic order through both their charge and their spin, acquiring non-trivial equations of motion and a dynamic spin polarization providing the foundation of spintronics. These mechanisms are, however, almost invariably reciprocal and the resistance a current encounters does not depend on its flow direction. Here we show that a chiral crystal lattice and an independently reversible chiral magnetic order together produce a diode whose forward direction is set by the magnetism rather than by the structure. In the triangular-lattice antiferromagnet Co$_{1/3}$TaS$_2$, whose chiral lattice hosts a chiral magnetic order of its own, independent handedness, we observe a pronounced nonreciprocal resistance. Unlike conventional electrical magnetochiral anisotropy, which is driven by an external magnetic field, our spontaneous magnetic diode effect arises from an internal noncoplanar spin texture with vanishing net magnetization. Symmetry analysis and model calculations show that the effect originates from the interplay of noncoplanar spin order and spin-orbit coupling, which generates a chiral, time-reversal-symmetry-breaking electronic response. Crucially, reversing the magnetic handedness reverses the sign of the nonreciprocal resistance, demonstrating magnetic control of the diode polarity.

</details>


### [123] [Non-magnetic impurities as a quantum microscope of finite size effects in spin chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34596)
*Quentin Faure,Shintaro Takayoshi,Virginie Simonet,Béatrice Grenier,Stéphane Raymond,Eric Ressouche,Xavier Fabrèges,Pascal Lejay,Thierry Giamarchi,Sylvain Petit*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过Mg掺杂将准一维Ising型反铁磁体Ba(Co₁₋ₓMgₓ)₂V₂O₈的自旋链割裂为有限链段，结合非弹性中子散射与数值计算，揭示了边界限域自旋子与无经典对应的短链"团簇模"两类低能激发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限尺寸效应在量子物质中普遍存在，但在热力学极限下通常不可见；仅当体系尺寸受限时，边界与限域才会显现出被改变的能谱、关联及涌现现象。随着对可控少自旋体系实验能力的提升，有限尺寸效应已可直接观测，从而为探测普适物理和实现量子信息应用提供途径。

Method: 选取准一维Ising型量子反铁磁体Ba(Co₁₋ₓMgₓ)₂V₂O₈（x = 2% 与 5%），利用非磁性Mg²⁺离子取代Co²⁺，将自旋链割裂为长度有限的链段；将非弹性中子散射实验与数值计算相结合，对低能激发谱进行表征与比对。

Result: 识别出低能量子激发性质根本不同的两类区域：其一是被限制在链边界内的自旋子（spinon）激发；其二是出现在较短链段中的团簇模，对应Néel有序链段的集体反转，无经典对应物。

Conclusion: 化学取代可作为一种"量子显微镜"，为强关联自旋体系中被边界塑造的激发提供直接观测手段，并展示有限尺寸物理在可控少自旋平台上的可研究性与潜在量子信息应用价值。

Abstract: Finite-size effects are ubiquitous in quantum matter, yet typically invisible in the thermodynamic limit; when system size is restricted, boundaries and confinement reveal modified spectra, correlations, and emergent phenomena. With growing access to well-controlled few-spin systems, these effects are now directly observable and offer a route to probing universal physics and enabling quantum information applications. Here, we study finite-size physics in the quasi-one-dimensional Ising-like quantum antiferromagnet Ba(Co$_{1-x}$Mg$_x$)$_2$V$_2$O$_8$ with $x = 2\%$ and $x = 5\%$, where non-magnetic Mg$^{2+}$ ions fragment the Co$^{2+}$ spin chains into finite segments. Combining inelastic neutron scattering and numerical calculations, we identify distinct regimes with fundamentally different low-energy quantum excitations: spinons confined to chain boundaries, and a cluster mode in shorter chains corresponding to a collective reversal of the Néel-ordered segment without classical analogue. Our results demonstrate how chemical substitution acts as a quantum microscope providing direct access to boundary-shaped excitations in strongly correlated spin systems.

</details>


### [124] [Electronic Responses in Solid Oxygen with Coexisting Structural and Magnetic Orders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34794)
*Naomichi Sato,Makoto Naka,Joji Nasu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文用含Peierls型电子-晶格耦合的多轨道Hubbard模型研究固态氧中结构序与磁序的耦合，通过Hartree-Fock近似得到基态相图，发现三态键序与反铁磁序的共存相，并预测其中存在线性磁电响应、交变磁自旋劈裂及压磁响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固态氧中O₂分子具S=1自旋、分子间为弱范德华相互作用，结构柔性强，同时分子间跃迁积分小，属强关联多轨道体系，是研究结构自由度与磁自由度相互作用的独特平台，但缺乏能同时描述二者的最小模型与相图。

Method: 构建含Peierls型电子-晶格耦合的多轨道Hubbard模型作为最小描述，在Hartree-Fock近似下求解基态相图，计算电子态以及磁响应与介电响应。

Result: 强库仑相互作用下出现反铁磁序；弱相互作用下出现三种非磁性键序态；中等相互作用下反铁磁序与三种键序分别共存。三个共存相均破坏时间反演对称性，其中一个保持空间反演对称性，另两个破坏反演对称性。两个破坏反演对称性的相呈线性磁电响应；保持反演对称性的相呈交变磁自旋劈裂与压磁响应。

Conclusion: 所预测的磁电、交变磁自旋劈裂与压磁响应可作为高压固态氧中磁序与结构序共存的实验判别特征。

Abstract: Solid oxygen provides a unique platform for studying the interplay between structural and magnetic degrees of freedom, as each O$_2$ molecule possesses an $S=1$ spin and weak intermolecular van der Waals interactions allow substantial structural flexibility. In addition, because of its small intermolecular hopping integrals, this system is known to be a strongly correlated electron system with a multiorbital nature. In this study, we examine electronic states and magnetic and dielectric responses using a multiorbital Hubbard model with Peierls-type electron-lattice coupling. This model provides a minimal description of the interplay between structural and magnetic degrees of freedom in solid oxygen. We apply the Hartree-Fock approximation to obtain the ground-state phase diagram. Antiferromagnetic order appears when the onsite Coulomb interaction is strong, whereas three nonmagnetic bond-ordered states emerge when it is weak. At intermediate interaction strengths, antiferromagnetic order coexists with each of the three bond orders. These three coexistence phases all break time-reversal symmetry; one preserves spatial inversion symmetry, whereas the other two also break it. The two inversion-breaking phases exhibit linear magnetoelectric responses, whereas the inversion-symmetric phase exhibits altermagnetic spin splitting and a piezomagnetic response. These responses may provide experimental signatures of coexisting magnetic and structural orders in high-pressure solid oxygen suggested by recent experiments.

</details>


### [125] [Orbital-selective correlations in kagome flat bands of Co$_3$Sn$_2$S$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35255)
*Rongxiang Zhu,Huaiqiang Wang,Haijun Zhang,Jianzhou Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文指出CoSnS中费米能级附近平带源于轨道/自旋相关自能驱动的几何kagome平带位移，而非重费米子式动力学重整化，并由此产生强能量依赖的反常霍尔电导与巨反常能斯特效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoSnS虽被视为中等关联铁磁Weyl半金属，但光谱实验在费米能级附近观测到DFT无法解释的平带特征，其物理起源不清。

Method: 采用DFT+动力学平均场理论（DMFT）结合最小kagome模型，分析轨道与自旋分辨自能对平带位置的影响，并计算贝里曲率与热电输运。

Result: 该平带特征来自以d_{x²-y²}为主的几何kagome平带在自能作用下向E_F移动；平带迁移增强E_F附近局域贝里曲率，产生强能量依赖的反常霍尔电导与巨反常能斯特效应。

Conclusion: 确立了轨道选择性关联机制对几何平带定位于费米能级的作用，并提出反常热电输运可作为平带诱导拓扑的灵敏探针。

Abstract: Geometrical flat bands provide an ideal platform for studying electronic correlations and quantum-geometric effects. Although the ferromagnetic Weyl semimetal \CoSnS is generally considered a moderately correlated system, recent spectroscopy experiments have revealed a flat-band feature near the Fermi level ($\EF$) that is absent in conventional density functional theory (DFT) calculations. Using DFT plus dynamical mean-field theory and a minimal kagome model, we show that this feature arises not from heavy-fermion-like dynamical band renormalization, but from the shifting of a $d_{x^2-y^2}$ dominated geometrical kagome flat band toward $\EF$ driven by orbital- and spin-dependent self-energies. The relocated flat band enhances the local Berry curvature near $\EF$, resulting in a strongly energy-dependent anomalous Hall conductivity and a giant anomalous Nernst effect. Our findings establish an orbital-selective correlation mechanism for positioning geometrical flat bands at $\EF$, and propose anomalous thermoelectric transport as a sensitive probe of flat band induced topology.

</details>


### [126] [Anisotropic magneto-elastic coupling in the quasi-2D Ising magnet Li$_2$FeSiO$_4$ and thermodynamic signature of low-energy orbital excitations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35341)
*Waldemar Hergett,Kevin Ackermann,Sven Spachmann,Martin Jonak,Mahmoud Abdel-Hafiez,Hend Shahed,Michael Merz,Maurits W. Haverkort,Rüdiger Klingeler*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过热膨胀与比热测量并结合数值计算，分离Li$_2$FeSiO$_4$中的轨道、晶格与自旋自由度信号，定量给出能隙$\Delta\sim300$–$350$ K及其压力依赖$\partial\ln\Delta/\partial p_c\approx-4.6$ %/GPa，与DFT预测吻合；并由磁有序相关的各向异性膨胀异常确定$T_{\rm N}$的各向异性单轴应变依赖，指出短程磁序不由$T_{\rm N}$同一能量尺度驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准二维类Ising磁体Li$_2$FeSiO$_4$中自旋、晶格与轨道自由度相互耦合，各自由度的能量尺度及其压力/应变响应难以从单一实验量中区分，需要一种能够在热力学层面分离各信号并定量标定其能量尺度的手段。

Method: 测量热膨胀与比热，结合Grüneisen分析进行热力学解析，分离轨道贡献并提取能隙及其压力依赖；辅以DFT数值研究作对照；利用长程磁有序起始处的热膨胀异常确定$T_{\rm N}$的各向异性单轴应变依赖，并检验Grüneisen标度、Grüneisen参数符号变化及各向异性面内畸变。

Result: 由实验数据得$\Delta\sim300$–$350$ K、$\partial\ln\Delta/\partial p_c\approx-4.6$ %/GPa，与DFT预测高度一致。磁有序起始的热膨胀异常显示显著磁弹耦合；$T_{\rm N}$的应变依赖以面内($c$轴)响应为主，$(\partial\ln T_{\rm N})/\partial p_c\simeq15$ %/GPa，而$a$轴(负)与$b$轴(正)压力依赖明显更小。短程磁序在$T_{\rm N}$以上大范围存在，但表现为非单调面内畸变、Grüneisen参数变号及Grüneisen标度失效，说明其不由$T_{\rm N}$同一能量尺度驱动。

Conclusion: 热力学与Grüneisen分析可有效分离Li$_2$FeSiO$_4$中轨道与磁/晶格自由度，并定量、自洽地给出一致于第一性原理的轨道能隙及其压力依赖；磁弹耦合高度各向异性且以面内为主导；短程磁序与长程有序分属不同能量尺度，Grüneisen标度失效可作为判别依据。

Abstract: The relevance of spin, lattice and orbital degrees of freedom in the quasi-2D Ising-like magnet Li$_2$FeSiO$_4$ is demonstrated by thermal expansion and specific heat measurements supported by numerical studies. By means of thermodynamic and Grüneisen analysis, we separate the orbital signatures and quantify the energy gap and its pressure dependence from the experimental data as $Δ\sim 300 - 350$ K and $\partial \ln Δ/\partial p_{\rm c} \approx -4.6$ %/GPa). Both values excellently agree with the theoretical DFT predictions. The thermal expansion anomalies associated with the onset of long-range magnetic order imply significant magneto-elastic coupling and are used to determine the anisotropic uniaxial strain dependencies of $T_{\rm N}$. They are dominated by the in-plane (i.e., $c$ axis) response $(\partial \ln{ T_{\rm N}} )/\partial p_{\rm c} \simeq 15$ %/GPa while the (negative) $a$ axis and (positive) out-of-plane ($b$ axis) pressure dependencies are much smaller. Short-range magnetic order extends well above $T_{\rm N}$ but is not driven by the same energy scale as $T_{\rm N}$ as evidenced by the observation of non-monotonic in-plane distortion, sign-change of the Grüneisen parameter, and failure of Grüneisen scaling.

</details>


### [127] [Collinear and Coplanar Altermagnetism in a Penrose Quasicrystal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35855)
*Bai-He Yao,Zhi-Yan Shao,Ye Cao,Fan Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 将准晶共线磁性分类推广到一般磁态，在Penrose格子上发现属于D₅群二维不可约表示的所有磁态（共线或共面）均为交错磁（AM），其零磁化由群论正交定理保护，而非传统旋转/镜面对称，并被自旋分辨ARPES与自旋电导所验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作仅给出准晶中共线磁性的群论分类，局限于共线情形，无法涵盖共面等一般磁态。作者希望将分类推广到一般磁态，并厘清准晶中零磁化与交错磁性的对称性来源。

Method: 以Penrose格子为例，基于D₅点群表示论分析磁态对称性；采用单轨道Penrose-Hubbard模型做线性响应理论计算，统计参数空间中的磁态分布；再用Ginzburg-Landau理论分析二维不可约表示下两个基磁态极化方向（平行或垂直）的选取。

Result: 所有属于D₅二维不可约表示的磁态（含共线、共面）均为AM；其零磁化由"不同不可约表示基函数相互正交"的群论正交定理保护，而非整体翻转自旋的旋转或镜面对称。线性响应计算显示二维不可约表示态占据参数空间最大部分；Ginzburg-Landau分析表明两基磁态极化可平行或垂直，分别导致共线或共面磁态；缺乏PT对称与正交补偿机制共同促成AM，预期在自旋分辨ARPES的自旋劈裂和自旋电导中可观测。

Conclusion: 准晶中由二维不可约表示承载的共线与共面磁态普遍表现为交错磁性，其机制为群论正交补偿而非传统自旋翻转对称，且该现象可推广至其他准晶。

Abstract: We generalize the previous group-theory based classification of collinear magnetism in the quasicrystal (QC) to general magnetic states. Taking the Penrose lattice as an example, we find that all magnetic states belonging to 2D irreducible representations (IRRPs) of the $D_5$ point group, including collinear states and coplanar ones, correspond to altermagnetism (AM). Unlike conventional collinear AM wherein zero magnetism is protected by, e.g. rotation or mirror reflection, which globally flip the spin in the magnetic pattern, the zero magnetism in these 2D-IRRP states is instead protected by a special group-theory-orthogonality theorem that basis functions belonging to different IRRPs are always mutually orthogonal. Taking the single-orbital Penrose-Hubbard as an example, our linear-response theory based calculations show that the largest portion of the parameter space is occupied by these 2D-IRRP states, and our further Ginzburg-Landau theory based analysis reveals that the polarization directions of the two basis magnetic states in the 2D IRRPs can be either parallel or perpendicular, leading to collinear or coplanar magnetic states. The special group-theory-orthogonality compensation mechanism and the absence of the PT symmetry finally leads to AM states, which are to be verified by the spin split in the spin-dependent angle-resolved-photoemission-spectrum and the spin conductance. These intriguing collinear and coplanar AM can also emerge in other QCs.

</details>
