### [128] [Orbital Hall response of spin-1 fermions with a curved central band](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33211)
*J. H. Warnes,Dayana Joy,Pankaj Bhalla,Rhonald Burgos Atencia*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究含线性与二次动量项的三带（spin-1）低能模型中的轨道霍尔效应，用量子动理学方法推导内禀轨道霍尔响应，并揭示其由动量依赖的轨道特征与带间跃迁共同决定，微分响应可作为低能结构的直接探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨道霍尔效应研究多局限于两带模型，缺乏对具有弯曲中心带的多带体系中轨道输运微观机制的清晰理解；需建立一个能分辨不同带间通道贡献的最小模型框架。

Method: 采用包含线性与二次动量项的三带低能哈密顿量（线性项解除色散带简并）；基于量子动理理学方法推导内禀轨道霍尔响应，并以电场诱导的带间相干为出发点进行微观分解；利用spin-1结构分离各带间通道贡献，并分析微分轨道霍尔响应的标度行为与紫外截断依赖性。

Result: 轨道霍尔电导源于本征态动量依赖轨道特征与带间跃迁的相互作用；spin-1结构使不同带间通道贡献可被单独识别；微分轨道霍尔响应呈现分别对应线性带与二次带的不同标度奇异性，且与紫外截断无关。

Conclusion: 建立了spin-1费米子多带量子几何与横向轨道响应之间的联系，提供了超越两带模型研究轨道输运的简洁框架。

Abstract: We investigate the orbital Hall effect in a three-band system described by a low-energy Hamiltonian containing both linear and quadratic momentum-dependent contributions. The linear term lifts the degeneracy between the dispersive bands, providing a minimal setting to study orbital transport in a multiband system with a curved central band. Within a quantum kinetic approach, we derive the intrinsic orbital Hall response and analyze its microscopic origin in terms of interband coherence induced by an external electric field. We show that the orbital Hall conductivity arises from the interplay between the momentum-dependent orbital character of the eigenstates and transitions between different bands. The spin-1 structure provides a particularly transparent platform in which the contributions associated with different interband channels can be identified separately. We further show that the differential orbital Hall response provides a direct probe of the low-energy structure, exhibiting distinct scaling singularities associated with the linear and quadratic bands, respectively, while remaining independent of the ultraviolet cutoff. Our results establish a connection between the multiband quantum geometry of spin-1 fermions and their transverse orbital response, and provide a simple framework for investigating orbital transport beyond two-band models.

</details>


### [129] [Unified First-Principles Theory of Finite-Wave-Vector Photocurrents in Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33264)
*Bumseop Kim,Sayed Ali Akbar Ghorashi,Yicong Chen,Ritesh Agarwal,Andrew M. Rappe*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文建立第一性原理理论，研究空间结构光（相位与强度梯度）如何在晶体中驱动二阶直流光电流，并用单层GeS验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间结构光除偏振和频率外还携带独立的相位与包络变化，但其如何驱动非线性直流光电流缺乏第一性原理描述。

Method: 将同频、不同波矢两光场分量的二阶直流响应按波矢展开到一阶，识别两个四阶张量：ξ（耦合相位梯度，含平面波光子拖曳极限）与ζ（耦合强度梯度）；对单层GeS做Wannier插值计算，并对高斯光束做多极分析分解。

Result: 单层GeS存在对称性允许的有限波矢响应，且在传统体光伏效应消失的中心对称结构中仍保留；高斯光束下相位梯度贡献在光束中心两侧产生同向电流，强度梯度贡献产生反向电流；光学修正可分解为电四极、轨道磁偶极与带色散贡献。

Conclusion: 光的空间结构可作为非线性光电流产生的独立调控变量，且该框架提供了在实际材料中预测此类响应的第一性原理途径。

Abstract: Spatially structured light carries independent phase and envelope variations in addition to its polarization and frequency. We develop a first-principles theory of second-order dc photocurrents (especially shift and injection) that resolves how these optical degrees of freedom drive distinct dc responses in crystals. The second-order dc response couples two optical field components at the same frequency but with generally different wave vectors. Expanding the response to first order in these wave vectors identifies two rank-four material tensors: $ξ$, which couples to the optical phase gradient and contains the plane-wave photon-drag limit, and $ζ$, which couples to the intensity gradient and describes a spatially varying dc current. Wannier-interpolated calculations for monolayer GeS show symmetry-allowed finite-wave-vector responses that survive at a centrosymmetric structure where conventional bulk photovoltaic effect vanishes. For a Gaussian beam with a uniform phase gradient, the phase-gradient contribution drives current in the same direction on both sides of the beam center, whereas the intensity-gradient contribution drives currents in opposite directions, showing that distinct photocurrent patterns can be selected through the wavefront and envelope of light. A multipolar analysis further resolves the optical corrections into electric-quadrupole, orbital magnetic-dipole, and band-dispersion contributions. Together, these results show how the spatial structure of light can serve as an independent control variable for nonlinear photocurrent generation and provide a first-principles route to predict this response in real materials.

</details>


### [130] [Chern non-trivial two-dimensional square-net nodal line semimetal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33303)
*Kendall Ramos,Salvador Barraza-Lopez,Elias Andrade*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在双方格正方网格 $p_xp_y$ 紧束缚模型中引入对称性约束、动量依赖且具有有限程实空间跃迁表示的SOC，两个分量共同打开节点线能隙，其中一分量破坏时间反演对称性，得到占据带陈数 $C=2$ 的陈绝缘体；纳米带计算显示每边缘两条净手性模式；并证明守恒自旋子空间幺正等价、陈数相等，从而排除该假设内的 $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探求正方网格二维体系中实现陈拓扑的唯象途径，并厘清决定可达绝缘相（陈绝缘体 vs. $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 拓扑绝缘体）的对称性约束。

Method: 构建双方格 $p_xp_y$ 正方网格紧束缚模型；引入满足对称性要求、动量依赖的SOC，并给出其有限程实空间跃迁表示；分析能隙机制（节点线打开）与自旋守恒子空间的幺正等价性；结合纳米带能带计算考察边缘态。

Result: SOC两分量联合打开节点线能隙；破坏时间反演的分量驱动体系进入陈数 $C=2$ 的陈绝缘相；纳米带中出现局域于边缘、穿越能隙的边缘态，每边缘有两条净手性模式；守恒自旋扇区幺正等价、陈数相同，故不存在非平庸 $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 相。

Conclusion: 给出正方网格二维体系实现陈拓扑的唯象方案，并明确可及绝缘相的对称性约束条件：在该SOC假设下仅陈绝缘相可实现，$\mathbb{Z}_2$ 相被排除。

Abstract: We investigate a two-dimensional $p_xp_y$ square-net tight-binding model with two sublattices and introduce a symmetry-informed, momentum-dependent spin-orbit coupling (SOC) with a finite-range real-space hopping representation. Its two components jointly gap the nodal line, while one breaks time-reversal symmetry, producing a Chern-insulating phase with an occupied band Chern number $C=2$. Nanoribbon calculations reveal edge-localized states traversing this gap, with two net chiral modes per edge. We further show that the conserved spin sectors are unitarily equivalent and therefore have equal Chern numbers, ruling out a nontrivial time-reversal-protected $\mathbb{Z}_2$ phase within this ansatz. Our results establish a phenomenological route to Chern topology in square-net two-dimensional systems and clarify the symmetry constraints governing the accessible insulating phases.

</details>


### [131] [Dephasing-powered open quantum-dot battery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33408)
*Alok Nath Singh,Rafael Sánchez,Andrew N. Jordan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出一种由局域退相位驱动的开放量子电池：三量子点链一端接热库、另一端强失谐形成高能储能单元，局域退相位自主充能至高ergotropic效率的非平衡稳态，并给出两种能量提取方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索无需外部控制场的自主充能机制，将通常被视为有害的退相干（局域退相位）转化为量子电池的充电资源，并在介观实验平台上实现可提取的储能。

Method: 构建三量子点链模型：一端耦合热库，另一端量子点强失谐作为高能存储元件；利用局域退相位布居高激发态，驱动系统进入非平衡稳态。通过能量存储性能、充电时间与稳定性刻画电池，并设计两种提取协议：（1）仅调制失谐量子点栅压的循环幺正协议；（2）将失谐量子点耦合至电子负载以提取电化学功。

Result: 发现ergotropic效率可趋于1的极限，此时ergotropy趋近能量失谐的三分之一；充电态在退相位源关闭后仍长寿命保持；循环幺正协议可回收几乎全部可用ergotropy；耦合电子负载可提取超过初始ergotropy的电化学功，从而动用部分被动能量。

Conclusion: 局域退相位可作为开放量子电池的自主充电资源，且所得储能在实验可及的介观量子点平台上存在简单可行的能量提取途径。

Abstract: We propose a simple realization of an open quantum battery powered by local dephasing. The setup consists of a triple-quantum-dot chain coupled at one end to a thermal reservoir, with the dot at the opposite end strongly detuned to form a high-energy storage element. Local dephasing populates this highly excited state and autonomously drives the system into a nonequilibrium steady state with high ergotropic efficiency. We find a limit where the ergotropic efficiency approaches unity, while the ergotropy itself approaches one-third of the energy detuning. We characterize the battery through its energy-storage performance, charging time, and stability, showing that the charged state remains long-lived after the dephasing source is switched off. We then demonstrate two complementary protocols for extracting the stored energy. First, nearly all the available ergotropy can be recovered through a simple cyclic unitary protocol involving only modulation of the detuned-dot gate. Second, coupling the detuned dot to an electronic load allows the extraction of electrochemical work exceeding the initial ergotropy, thereby accessing part of the battery's passive energy. Our results establish local dephasing as an autonomous resource for charging open quantum batteries and demonstrate simple routes for extracting the resulting stored energy in experimentally accessible mesoscopic platforms.

</details>


### [132] [Zeptojoule Microwave Calorimetry with Nanosecond Recovery in Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33414)
*Seunghan Lee,Woochan Jung,Jayeon Kim,Dongsung T. Park,Seong Jang,Seongeun Jeon,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Jong-Chul Park,Philip Kim,Kin Chung Fong,Gil-Ho Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文直接标定了石墨烯约瑟夫森结微波量热计，并通过独立测量同一器件热参数，揭示了其灵敏度低于理想预期的微观原因（电子热容与电子-声子耦合偏大）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 石墨烯量热计理论上兼具极高能量灵敏度与快速热恢复，但实验结果远未达到理想估计，其灵敏度差距的微观来源尚不明确。

Method: 用纳秒脉冲对石墨烯约瑟夫森结微波量热计进行直接标定；以时间分辨双脉冲测温法测定本征弛豫时间；结合脉冲宽度依赖量热与测辐射热计测温确定电子热容与电子-声子耦合；并构建热模型进行定量预测。

Result: 在110 mK下，标定脉冲给出特征开关能标18.1 meV（2.90 zJ），本征弛豫时间19.5 ns；电子热容比理想估计大约12.4倍，电子-声子耦合大约50倍。基于实测参数的热模型预测21.2 meV，定量再现了观测到的开关响应。

Conclusion: 研究明确了石墨烯量热计灵敏度差距的微观起源，并建立了面向超快纳米尺度热探测器的自洽标定框架。

Abstract: Graphene calorimeters are predicted to combine extreme energy sensitivity with rapid thermal recovery, yet experiments remain far from ideal estimates. Here, we directly calibrate a graphene Josephson-junction microwave calorimeter using nanosecond pulses and independently determine the thermal parameters of the same operating device. At 110 mK, calibrated pulse measurements yield a characteristic switching-energy scale of 18.1 meV (2.90 zJ), while time-resolved two-pulse thermometry gives an intrinsic relaxation time of 19.5 ns. Pulse-width-dependent calorimetry and bolometric thermometry show that the electronic heat capacity is 12.4 times and the electron-phonon coupling is approximately 50 times larger than ideal estimates. A thermal model using these measured parameters predicts 21.2 meV, quantitatively reproducing the observed switching response. These results identify the microscopic origin of the sensitivity gap in graphene calorimeters and establish a self-consistent calibration framework for ultrafast nanoscale thermal detectors.

</details>


### [133] [Second-Harmonic Bulk Response of Fractional Quantum Hall Fluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33606)
*J. Luke Pimlott,Pablo Reiser,T. Hans Hansson,Habib Rostami*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文用体态(复合玻色子Chern–Simons)理论描述破坏空间反演与旋转对称性的分数量子霍尔流体的二次谐波霍尔响应，与基于非线性边缘色散的边缘理论相区别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有对分数量子霍尔非线性输运的理解多依赖非线性边缘模式色散，缺乏从体态出发的微观描述；作者希望建立不依赖边缘机制的体态框架，以刻画二次谐波霍尔响应。

Method: 将倾斜的有质量Dirac模型投影到单一低能朗道能级，构造最低朗道能级的复合玻色子Chern–Simons理论，保留投影诱导的非线性电磁耦合，并计算非线性霍尔电阻率。

Result: 非线性霍尔电阻率在直流极限下消失；有限频响应保持特征填充因子依赖 ρ^(2)_TLL ∝ ν^(-2)；低频下实部与虚部分别按 (ω/ω_c)^2 与 ω/ω_c 标度（ω_c为回旋频率），且响应相对晶格倾斜轴呈强各向异性；二阶电导率中的Kohn模共振在非线性霍尔电阻率中相消。

Conclusion: 有限频非线性输运可作为分数量子霍尔流体的体态探针，而无需非线性边缘模式色散。

Abstract: We develop a bulk description for the second-harmonic Hall response of fractional quantum Hall fluids with broken inversion and rotational symmetries, distinct from edge theories based on nonlinear edge-mode dispersion. By projecting a tilted massive Dirac model onto a single low-energy band, we construct a composite-boson Chern--Simons theory for the lowest Landau level that retains projection-induced nonlinear electromagnetic couplings. The nonlinear Hall resistivity vanishes in the dc limit, while its finite-frequency response retains the characteristic filling-factor dependence $ρ^{(2)}_{TLL}\proptoν^{-2}$, where $ν$ is the Landau-level filling factor. At low frequency, its real and imaginary parts scale as $(ω/ω_c)^2$ and $ω/ω_c$, respectively, where $ω_c$ is the cyclotron frequency, and the response is strongly anisotropic with respect to the crystalline tilt axis. Notably, the Kohn-mode resonances present in the second-order conductivity cancel in the nonlinear Hall resistivity. These results establish finite-frequency nonlinear transport as a bulk probe of fractional quantum Hall fluids without requiring nonlinear edge-mode dispersion.

</details>


### [134] [Perpendicular Anisotropy and g-Factor of Sputtered Ni Films on Si(111) Studied by Angle-Dependent Ferromagnetic Resonance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33637)
*Dushyant Singh,Tharundev V,Subha Maity,Subhavenkadesh SS,Vigneshwari SR,Krista R. Khiangte*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过AFM、GIXRD、XRR、磁强计和角分辨FMR研究室温直流磁控溅射在Si(111)上生长的312 Å与1011 Å多晶fcc Ni薄膜；用含一、二阶项的单轴各向异性模型拟合共振场角依赖，得到g因子与有效垂直各向异性，并指出仅用面内/垂直共振的传统分析会低估g因子0.02–0.04，垂直线宽给出Gilbert阻尼上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 薄膜厚度对Ni薄膜的磁性参数（g因子、垂直各向异性、阻尼）的影响及其准确测定方法值得澄清；特别是仅取面内与垂直两个取向共振场的传统分析可能引入系统偏差，需要角分辨测量加以检验。

Method: 室温直流磁控溅射在Si(111)上生长312 Å和1011 Å厚Ni膜；用AFM、掠入射XRD、X射线反射、磁强计和角分辨铁磁共振表征；以含一阶与二阶项的单轴各向异性模型拟合共振场面内至面外法向的角依赖关系。

Result: 两膜均为表面光滑的多晶fcc Ni，具有体材料(111)晶面间距与约480 emu/cm³的饱和磁化强度，且均为面内磁化；拟合得g=2.160±0.005（薄膜）和2.101±0.014（厚膜），有效垂直各向异性分别为(6.0±0.4)×10⁵与(4.5±0.6)×10⁵ erg/cm³；仅用面内与垂直共振的传统分析给出的g因子偏低0.02–0.04；垂直线宽给出Gilbert阻尼上限α≤0.030（薄膜）和≤0.038（厚膜）。

Conclusion: 角分辨FMR结合含二阶项的单轴各向异性模型是准确提取Ni薄膜g因子与垂直各向异性的必要手段；传统两点法存在系统误差，而所测薄膜阻尼较低。

Abstract: Ni films with thicknesses of 312 and 1011\,Å were grown on Si(111) by dc magnetron sputtering at room temperature and studied by atomic force microscopy, grazing-incidence X-ray diffraction, X-ray reflectivity, magnetometry and angle-dependent ferromagnetic resonance. Both films are polycrystalline fcc Ni with smooth surfaces, a bulk-like (111) lattice spacing and a saturation magnetization of ${\approx}480$\,emu\,cm$^{-3}$, and both are magnetized in plane. The angular dependence of the resonance field, from the film plane to the film normal, is described by a uniaxial anisotropy model with first- and second-order terms. From this model, $g = 2.160\pm0.005$ and $2.101\pm0.014$ and effective perpendicular anisotropies of $(6.0\pm0.4)\times10^{5}$ and $(4.5\pm0.6)\times10^{5}$\,erg\,cm$^{-3}$ were obtained for the thinner and thicker film, respectively. The conventional analysis of only the in-plane and perpendicular resonances gives $g$-factors that are lower by 0.02--0.04. The perpendicular linewidths give upper limits for the Gilbert damping of $α\le 0.030$ and $\le 0.038$.

</details>


### [135] [Emergent parafermions with tunable localization in a quantum circuit network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33815)
*D. B. Karki,Andrew K. Mitchell*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 三角三量子点器件中几何阻挫诱导出共享的Z3 parafermion，可通过栅压调控迁移至任意两量子点对，并在Kondo极限与准弹道区均稳定存在，为可扩展的可重构非阿贝尔激发网络奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合金属-半导体量子点电路模拟器是实现Kondo任意子的平台，其源于阻挫Kondo屏蔽驱动的临界点，此前仅在单点或双点线性电路中实验实现。作者希望将这一机制拓展到具有几何阻挫的多点体系，探索可调控、可扩展的非阿贝尔激发实现方案。

Method: 研究三角排布的三量子点器件，利用几何阻挫产生三个点共享的涌现Z3 parafermion；通过施加栅极电压调控其局域位置，并在弱隧穿Kondo极限与强透射准弹道区两种参数区间分析临界态的性质，同时考察其与外部引线及其他量子点强耦合下的稳定性。

Result: 三角三量子点中几何阻挫产生共享的Z3 parafermion；通过栅压可将其可调地迁移到任意一对量子点上；该临界态在弱隧穿Kondo极限和强透射准弹道区均得到支持；且在外部引线及其他量子点的强耦合下仍能存活。

Conclusion: 三角三量子点器件提供了可调控、可重构的Z3 parafermion平台，其临界态的鲁棒性构成了实现和操控非阿贝尔激发的可扩展网络架构基础。

Abstract: \noindent Quantum circuit simulators with hybrid metal-semiconductor dots provide a versatile platform to realize Kondo anyons. These arise at critical points driven by frustrated Kondo screening of dot charge degrees of freedom and have been demonstrated experimentally in linear circuits of one or two dots. Here we study a triangular triple dot device, where geometric frustration gives rise to an emergent $\mathbb{Z}_3$ parafermion shared among the three dots. We show that the parafermion can be tunably relocated to any pair of dots by applying gate voltages, and is supported in both the weak-tunneling `Kondo limit' and the strong transmission `quasiballistic' regime. The critical states survive strong coupling to external leads and other dots, establishing the basis for a scalable and reconfigurable network architecture in which non-Abelian excitations can be realized and manipulated.

</details>


### [136] [Momentum-selective graphene spectroscopy of moiré excitons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33989)
*Katsunori Wakabayashi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出用石墨烯能量转移作为近场动量选择性探针，构建WSe₂/MoSe₂异质双层莫尔激子的跃迁极化P_n(qv)，证明石墨烯速率与远场光学速率对不同局域态给出不同排序，可用作莫尔激子光谱学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远场光致发光只能探测激子动量内容中光锥附近的一角，无法获得面内大动量信息；而向近邻石墨烯的能量转移可将面内动量积分至1/z（间隔层厚度的倒数），因此有望作为互补的动量选择性探针。

Method: 基于配准依赖的矢量偶极织构，为WSe₂/MoSe₂异质双层中每个局域莫尔激子构造跃迁极化P_n(qv)，并同时用于光学与石墨烯两个"浴"；在C₃对称领头谐波模型中分析手性p₊、d₋态，其织构相位符号由第一性原理确定。

Result: 两种浴对态的排序不同：石墨烯速率比与光学速率比不同，且其排序随间隔层厚度反转；z=10 nm时距离指数散布超过两个单位；这些比值与跃迁偶极前因子无关。手性p₊、d₋态在正入射下为暗态，但向石墨烯转移速率与亮基态相当，且依赖远场不可见的织构相位符号。

Conclusion: 石墨烯近邻效应可作为莫尔激子的动量选择性近场探针，并据此提出一种实用光谱学方案；器件实现需满足手性伙伴保护条件，即分裂大于对称性破缺混合。

Abstract: Far-field photoluminescence probes only the light-cone corner of an exciton's momentum content, whereas energy transfer to nearby graphene integrates in-plane momenta up to the inverse spacer thickness $1/z$. For a WSe$_2$/MoSe$_2$ heterobilayer we build the transition polarization $\bm{P}_n(\qv)$ of each localized moiré exciton from the registry-dependent vector dipole texture and use each $\bm{P}_n$ for both baths. The two baths rank the states differently: the graphene-rate ratios differ from the optical ones, their ordering inverts with spacer thickness, and the distance exponents at $z=10$\,nm spread by more than two units. These ratios are independent of the transition-dipole prefactor. Within the $C_3$-symmetric leading-harmonic model the chiral $p_+$ and $d_-$ states are also dark at normal incidence yet transfer to graphene at rates comparable to the bright ground state. Their graphene rate depends on the sign of the texture phase, a quantity invisible in far-field emission, which we determine from first principles. In a device this prediction requires a splitting, larger than the symmetry-breaking mixing, that protects the chiral partners. Graphene proximity thus acts as a momentum-selective near-field probe of moiré excitons, and we propose its use as a practical spectroscopy.

</details>


### [137] [Andreev Reflection at a Graphene Normal-Superconductor Interface: A Lattice-Continuum Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34270)
*Dar Faheem Ul Haq,S. S. Z. Ashraf*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文比较了石墨烯正常-超导界面Andreev反射的二次型连续模型与最近邻紧束缚晶格模型，发现两者在动量-群速度关系（三角翘曲）与界面匹配上存在本质差异，连续模型的有效性需同时考虑各能量尺度与边界辅助模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低能连续模型（谷展开、二次色散）被广泛用于描述石墨烯Andreev反射，但其适用于界面问题的确切条件缺乏检验，尤其是三角翘曲对动量与群速度关系的影响以及边界条件数目差异。

Method: 对化学势与配对势的突变界面（跃迁不变）分别建立二次型连续模型与最近邻紧束缚模型；晶格模型给出四个独立边界条件，反射概率与独立Kwant计算逐通道对照；考察ξ=+1谷反射概率峰角随(μ_N,μ_S,Δ_0,E)/t_0的变化，并在固定比例下整体降低能量尺度。

Result: 参数(μ_N,μ_S,Δ_0,E)/t_0=(0.5,1,0.1,0.05)时，晶格模型Andreev反射概率峰值位于19.9°，连续模型仅为3.4°；随能量尺度按比例整体缩小，中心角响应与电导逐渐趋于晶格结果；连续边界问题还需超出谷展开有效范围的短波辅助模。

Conclusion: 差异不能仅归因于体色散；小E/μ不足以保证连续模型有效，必须同时评估相关能量尺度、两区域中的模式以及界面匹配假设。

Abstract: We compare quadratic continuum and nearest-neighbour tight-binding models of Andreev reflection at a clean graphene normal-superconductor interface. For abrupt changes in chemical potential and pairing potential, with unchanged hopping, the lattice model yields four independent boundary conditions. Its reflection probabilities agree with independent Kwant calculations to numerical precision for the channels examined.
  We investigate trigonal warping and its effect on the relation between momentum and group velocity. For $(μ_N,μ_S,Δ_0,E)/t_0=(0.5,1,0.1,0.05)$, the Andreev-reflection probability for the $ξ=+1$ valley peaks at $19.9^\circ$ in the lattice model, versus $3.4^\circ$ in the quadratic continuum model. These parameters lie outside the expected validity regime of the low-energy approximation. As all energy scales are reduced at fixed ratios, the central angular response and conductance become more consistent with lattice results.
  The quadratic continuum boundary problem also requires short-wavelength auxiliary modes outside the validity range of the valley expansion. Thus, differences cannot be attributed to bulk dispersion alone. A small excitation energy relative to chemical potential does not ensure continuum validity; relevant energy scales, modes in both regions, and interface matching assumptions must also be considered.

</details>


### [138] [Geometric tailoring of domain wall traps and diodes in ferromagnetic nanostripes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34526)
*Dmytro Karakuts,Kostiantyn V. Yershov,Denis D. Sheka*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过截面几何调制（纳米条带收缩与楔形扩张），实现畴壁陷阱与畴壁二极管，并用双轴铁磁体模型与集体变量法理论解释，与微磁模拟吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何裁剪是调控磁性的核心手段，但截面几何变化如何影响场驱动畴壁动力学尚需系统研究，并探索可实现的畴壁功能器件。

Method: 采用截面几何调制（几何渐变）设计纳米条带收缩（畴壁陷阱）与楔形扩张（畴壁二极管）；结合微磁模拟，并建立双轴铁磁体模型与集体变量方法进行理论计算。

Result: 畴壁可被截面调制有效钉扎，去钉扎场强烈依赖截面梯度；弱场下畴壁在钉扎位点附近局域振荡，强场下钉扎失效，呈现典型二极管特性；理论计算与微磁模拟定量一致；并给出效应最显著的参数区间。

Conclusion: 截面几何调制可有效构建畴壁陷阱与畴壁二极管，去钉扎场由几何梯度调控；该机制在现实样品参数范围内可实现，为几何裁剪磁畴壁器件提供理论与设计依据。

Abstract: Geometric tailoring of magnetic responses is a central theme in modern magnetism. By employing cross-sectional geometric modulation, we investigate how geometric variations influence the dynamics of field-driven domain walls. This approach enables the realization of a domain-wall trap, achieved through a nanostripe constriction, and a domain-wall diode, realized via a wedge-shaped expansion. We demonstrate that domain walls can be efficiently pinned by cross-sectional modulation, with the depinning field exhibiting strong dependence on the cross-sectional gradient. Under weak applied fields, the domain wall undergoes localized oscillations near the pinning site; under strong fields, this pinning breaks down, reproducing the typical diode characteristics. Theoretical calculations based on a biaxial ferromagnet model and a collective-variable approach are in excellent agreement with micromagnetic simulations. Finally, we delineate the parameter regime for realistic samples in which these predicted effects are most pronounced.

</details>


### [139] [Synthetic staggered fields switch Neel vectors probed by magnetic resonance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34624)
*Yifang Hao,Zhiyi Che,Jack Brierley,Aakanksha Sud,Satoshi Iihama,Rishi Siddani,Chenghao Yang,Charlie W. F. Freeman,Troy Dion,Daniel Prestwood,Zekun Xue,Tianyi Wei,Murat Cubukcu,Craig Barton,Shunsuke Fukami,Shigemi Mizukami,Hidekazu Kurebayashi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文利用磁共振实现对尼尔矢量态的直接电学探测：通过电流驱动的"合成交错场"实现合成反铁磁体中180°尼尔矢量翻转，翻转引起进动相位π突变，从而在相位敏感检测中产生滞回整流电压；缺陷纳入的微米磁模拟解释了碎片化畴态，并给出室温翻转的最低电流密度7.4×10^9 A/m²及其与面内单轴各向异性的标度关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反铁磁体宏观磁化消失，使其有序态的电学探测成为反铁磁自旋电子学的关键难题。磁共振虽已展示亚太赫兹自旋泵浦与非线性磁振子动力学，但在电接触的片上器件中应用仍待探索。

Method: 采用磁共振结合相位敏感检测方案，直接电学探测尼尔矢量态；以电流驱动作用于各磁性亚层的反向有效场（即"合成交错场"）实现合成反铁磁体的180°翻转；通过角分辨共振分析判别尼尔矢量方向；并用引入缺陷的微米磁模拟描述激发后的畴结构。

Result: 180°翻转使进动相位在时间上发生π相移，产生滞回整流电压；角分辨共振分析显示超出宏观自旋模型的图像，微米磁模拟揭示电流激发后的碎片化畴态；提取的翻转电流密度为7.4×10^9 A/m²，为目前报道的室温尼尔矢量翻转最低值，且实验与数值均表明其随面内单轴各向异性标度变化。

Conclusion: 合成交错场与基于共振的方法为电驱动反铁磁织构的纳米尺度表征提供研究平台，可指导高频片上自旋电子架构的发展。

Abstract: Electrical detection of antiferromagnetic order is a critical challenge for antiferromagnetic spintronics owing to their vanishing macroscopic magnetisation. While magnetic resonance has demonstrated sub-terahertz antiferromagnetic spin pumping and nonlinear magnon dynamics, its application to electrically contacted on-chip devices has room for further exploration. Here we show that magnetic resonance enables direct electrical detection of Néel vector states. A $180^\circ$ reversal in synthetic antiferromagnets is realised by current-driven effective fields with opposite polarisation acting on each magnetic sublayer, which we term synthetic staggered fields. The switching induces a $π$ phase shift of precessional motion in time, producing hysteretic rectified voltages in our phase-sensitive detection scheme. Angular-dependent resonance analysis offers further insight into the Néel vector directions, pointing to a model beyond the macrospin formalism. We address this by defect-incorporated micromagnetic simulations that reveal fragmented domain states after current excitation. An extracted current density of $7.4\times10^{9}$ A/m$^2$ is, to our knowledge, the lowest reported value for the room-temperature Néel vector switching and we show that it scales with the in-plane uniaxial anisotropy both experimentally and numerically. Synthetic staggered fields and the resonance-based methodology provide a research platform for nanoscale characterisation of electrically driven antiferromagnetic textures, guiding the development of high-frequency on-chip spintronic architectures.

</details>


### [140] [Helicity-Selective Electron-Phonon Coupling Between $PT$-Partner States in $2H$-bilayer MoS$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34830)
*Fred Sun,Jia-An Yan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文理论研究2H双层MoS2中手性声子与PT简并电子双态的耦合，揭示PT虽抑制声子单态手性，但手性声子双态可类赝自旋地与PT配对电子态螺旋耦合，并分析PT与C3共同作用下的选择定则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转对称性对手性声子的约束已有研究，但其他对称性（尤其是PT对称性）如何影响手性声子相互作用仍鲜有探讨。

Method: 结合理论与计算方法，以2H双层MoS2为载体，研究手性声子与PT简并电子双态的耦合，并分析PT与C3对称性协同下的选择定则。

Result: PT对称性会抑制声子单态的手性；然而手性声子双态可通过类似赝自旋的机制与PT配对电子态发生螺旋相互作用。计算结果表明手性声子可用于分辨PT体系中的谷内电子态。

Conclusion: 手性声子与PT简并电子态存在可用的耦合通道，为利用手性声子在PT体系中实现谷内电子态的分辨提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Rotational symmetry ($C_n$) constrains chiral phonons, but how other symmetries affect their interactions remains largely unexplored. We perform a theoretical and computational study of chiral phonon coupling to parity-time ($PT$)-degenerate electron doublets in $2H$-bilayer MoS$_2$. Although $PT$ suppresses phonon singlet chirality, chiral phonon doublets can helically interact with $PT$-partner electronic states via a mechanism similar to pseudospin. Selection rules are then analyzed with the interplay of $PT$ and $C_3$. Our computational results of $2H$-bilayer MoS$_2$ suggest chiral phonons may be used to resolve intravalley electronic states in $PT$ systems.

</details>


### [141] [Nanoscale mapping of native defects in Td-MoTe2 and their impact on graphene-based van der Waals heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34924)
*Wojciech Ryś,Iaroslav Lutsyk,Mindaugas Lukosius,Przemysław Przybysz,Paweł Dąbrowski,Maciej Rogala,Paweł Krukowski,Paweł J. Kowalczyk*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用STM/STS研究了Td-MoTe2及其与石墨烯范德华异质结中的Te空位缺陷，发现该缺陷为电学弱扰动，对低能电子结构影响有限，异质结对界面缺陷本征容忍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原生缺陷（尤其是碲空位）对Td-MoTe2低能电子结构及石墨烯/MoTe2异质结界面性质的影响尚不清楚，需从原子尺度直接探测其光谱效应。

Method: 采用扫描隧道显微镜（STM）观察表面缺陷形貌，结合扫描隧道谱（STS）测量dI/dV谱，对比原始MoTe2与石墨烯/Td-MoTe2异质结在缺陷处与完整区域的电子结构。

Result: STM显示高密度圆形空心表面缺陷，归属为Te空位；原始MoTe2的STS呈半金属V形dI/dV谱并含微弱低能态；空位仅抑制费米能级以下隧穿电导，未产生新的尖锐缺陷共振；异质结中石墨烯保留MoTe2的这些谱学特征，且无显著掺杂或电荷转移。

Conclusion: 孤立Te空位对石墨烯低能电子结构属光谱学弱扰动，其效应源于Mo-Te谱权重的重新分布而非新电子态；石墨烯覆盖层进一步屏蔽缺陷影响，使石墨烯/Td-MoTe2异质结本征上对界面缺陷具有容忍性。

Abstract: We investigate native defects and their impact on the electronic structure of Td-MoTe2 and graphene/Td-MoTe2 van der Waals heterostructures using scanning tunnelling microscopy and spectroscopy. STM reveals a high density of circular, hollow surface defects that we attribute to Te vacancies. STS on pristine MoTe2 shows semimetallic, V-shaped dI/dV spectra with subtle low-energy states. The vacancies suppress the tunnelling conductance below the Fermi level without introducing new sharp defect-related resonances, consistent with a vacancy-induced redistribution of Mo-Te spectral weight rather than the appearance of new electronic states. In the heterostructure, graphene preserves these MoTe2 fingerprints with no noticeable graphene doping or charge transfer. Our results demonstrate that isolated native Te vacancies act as spectroscopically weak perturbations to the low-energy electronic structure of graphene. The graphene overlayer further screens their impact on the electronic structure, making graphene/Td-MoTe2 heterostructures intrinsically tolerant to such interface defects.

</details>


### [142] [Dynamic helimagnetism via charged spin current](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34927)
*Masaru Onoda*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 从s-d交换哈密顿量推导局域自旋与载流电子耦合的有效运动方程，在一维周期自旋链中预言两种电流驱动螺旋相：纯自旋流稳定的静态螺旋，以及加入少量电荷流后出现的相干进动螺旋。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在无内禀Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya（DM）相互作用的材料中，能否仅凭电流（自旋流/电荷流）产生并控制螺旋磁序。

Method: 基于s-d交换哈密顿量进行理论与数值研究，导出局域自旋的有效运动方程；应用于一维周期排布的自旋链；对同一方程做直接数值积分；并用启发式构造的有效拉格朗日量选择进动螺旋的波矢。

Result: 给定螺旋波矢时，进动角频率ω与向难轴的倾斜比Δ可由运动方程解析给出；进动螺旋波矢q_helix由有效拉格朗日量确定；数值积分在产生相干螺旋时复现全部三个观测量；螺旋手性由电流产生的长程DM型耦合决定，而非内禀DM相互作用。

Conclusion: 螺旋螺距、进动频率与倾斜比均可由电流幅度连续调控，因此这类螺旋可作为自旋流的探针；无体或界面DM相互作用的材料也可能承载电流控制的螺旋序。

Abstract: We present a theoretical and numerical study of classical, localized spins coupled to conduction electrons that carry both charge and spin currents, deriving the effective equations of motion from the s-d exchange Hamiltonian. Applied to a periodically arranged one-dimensional spin chain, these equations yield two current-driven helical phases: a static helix stabilized by a pure spin current alone, and a coherently precessing helix that appears once a small charge-current component is added. For a given helical wavenumber, the precession angular frequency $ω$ and the canting ratio $Δ$ toward the hard axis follow analytically from the equations of motion, while the wavenumber $q_{\rm helix}$ of the precessing helix is selected by a heuristically constructed effective Lagrangian. Direct numerical integration of the same equations of motion reproduces all three observables wherever it yields a coherent helix. The helix chirality is set by a long-range, current-generated Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya (DM)-type coupling rather than an intrinsic DM interaction, so materials without bulk or interfacial DM interactions could also host current-controlled helical order. Because the helical pitch, precession frequency, and canting ratio are all continuously tunable by the current amplitudes, these helices could serve as probes of spin currents.

</details>


### [143] [High-Q, high-power spin Hall nano-oscillators with thick NiFe layers enabled by SiC substrates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35245)
*Maha Khademi,Akash Kumar,Martina Ahlberg,Artem Litvinenko,Xiaotian Zhao,Saroj P. Dash,Johan Åkerman*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过采用高导热SiC衬底进行热学衬底工程，在W/NiFe纳米收缩型自旋霍尔纳米振荡器中实现了厚铁磁层（最优7 nm）的稳定自激振荡，单器件平均输出功率达13.1 pW、平均品质因数Q=23,000，为单个自由运行SHNO同时报道的最高输出功率与相干性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SHNO在局域自旋激发、类脑计算与基于振荡器的伊辛机中有应用前景，但需提升输出功率与相干性；增厚铁磁层是可行途径，却会带来更高的驱动电流、加剧焦耳热并劣化器件性能，形成瓶颈。

Method: 以同等结构的W/NiFe纳米收缩型SHNO为对象，对比Al2O3、Si与SiC三种衬底，利用SiC的高热导率抑制焦耳热，系统考察NiFe层厚度对自激振荡稳定性的影响。

Result: SiC衬底可在NiFe厚度达10 nm时仍维持稳定自激振荡，最佳性能出现在7 nm；最优SiC器件平均输出功率13.1 pW，平均品质因数Q=23,000，为单个自由运行SHNO中同时实现的最高输出功率与相干性。

Conclusion: SiC衬底与热学衬底工程是通向厚铁磁层自旋电子振荡器、实现纳米尺度器件相干微波产生的有效路径。

Abstract: Spin Hall nano-oscillators (SHNOs) are nanoscale microwave sources with potential applications in local spin excitation, neuromorphic computing, and oscillator-based Ising machines. Further improvements in output power and coherence are essential for these applications, and increasing the thickness of the ferromagnetic layer offers a promising route toward this goal. However, this comes at the cost of higher drive currents, which increase Joule heating and can degrade device performance. Here, we show that this bottleneck can be overcome by SiC substrates. By comparing otherwise identical W/NiFe nanoconstriction SHNOs on Al2O3, Si, and SiC, we find that the high thermal conductivity of SiC enables stable auto-oscillations in NiFe layers as thick as 10 nm, while the optimum performance occurs at 7 nm. The best SiC device reaches an average output power of 13.1 pW and a mean quality factor of Q = 23,000, representing the highest reported simultaneous output power and coherence for a single free-running SHNO. These results establish SiC and thermal substrate engineering as a route to thick-ferromagnet spintronic oscillators for coherent microwave generation in nanoscale devices.

</details>


### [144] [Higher-harmonic spin-wave excitation by a superconducting antenna with vortices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35268)
*Anton Pokusinskyi,Paweł Gruszecki,Julia Kharlan,Oleksandr Dobrovolskiy,Jarosław W. Kłos*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文通过时变Ginzburg–Landau模拟与微波近场谱分析，证明Shubnikov相中超导微带天线的近谐涡旋振荡可产生高阶谐波微波场，从而激发磁层中的高次谐波自旋波，且磁层本身保持线性响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导与磁性杂化体系中，直流驱动的Abrikosov涡旋（磁通量子）与自旋波的相互作用已有研究，但受限的、近似简谐的涡旋振荡能否产生更高频自旋波模式尚未被探索；同时传统谐波产生机制依赖磁性层的非线性磁化动力学，需寻找替代的非线性来源。

Method: 采用时间依赖Ginzburg–Landau（TDGL）建模描述超导微带天线中涡旋的受限振荡及屏蔽电流重分布，并对微波近场进行频谱分析，提取高次谐波成分及其与磁层自旋波模式的耦合。

Result: 近似正弦的涡旋运动及伴随的超导屏蔽电流重分布产生高阶谐波，并耦合到自旋波模式；该过程属于"空间—时间转导"，即将空间非均匀的磁场分布转换为高时间谐波，而磁性层始终保持线性接收者角色，超导天线才是非线性微波场的来源。

Conclusion: 磁通量子可作为频率转换的主动非线性元件，拓展超导—磁振子杂化体系中可及的自旋波频率范围，为非线性激发调控提供新途径。

Abstract: The interaction between superconductivity and magnetism provides new routes for controlling nonlinear excitations in hybrid systems. While dc-driven Abrikosov vortices (fluxons) are known to interact with spin waves, the ability of confined, nearly harmonic vortex oscillations to generate higher-frequency spin-wave modes remains unexplored. Here, by combining time-dependent Ginzburg--Landau modeling with spectral analysis of the microwave near field, we demonstrate higher-harmonic spin-wave excitation driven by a superconducting microstrip antenna in the Shubnikov phase. Unlike conventional harmonic-generation mechanisms associated with nonlinear magnetization dynamics, the magnetic layer remains a linear receiver, whereas the superconducting antenna generates the nonlinear microwave field. We show that nearly sinusoidal vortex motion and the accompanying redistribution of superconducting screening currents produce higher harmonics that couple to spin-wave modes. This response arises from spatial-to-temporal transduction, whereby harmonic vortex motion and the associated redistribution of screening currents convert a spatially nonuniform magnetic-field profile into higher temporal harmonics. Magnetic flux quanta thus act as active nonlinear elements for frequency conversion, extending the accessible spin-wave frequency range in superconducting--magnonic hybrids.

</details>


### [145] [Information-theoretic entropy imaging of quantum matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35790)
*Chen Wu,Leonard W. Cao,Lingyuan Lyu,Liam Cohen,Jiahui Nie,Can Uzundal,Yujia Hu,Quanjin Wang,Ziying Yan,Sneh Pancholi,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Feng Wang,Andrea F. Young,Tarun Grover,Monica T. Allen*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出"熵成像"方法，利用无损压缩得到的可计算信息密度(CID)量化量子材料中涌现的空间组织，并在石墨烯量子霍尔转变、分数量子霍尔、moiré体系上验证其判别力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相变产生的复杂空间组织在空间平均探针下不可见，而现有定量刻画这类组织的指标有限，缺乏能直接从实验图像中提取信息复杂度的手段。

Method: 以无损压缩为核心的信息论技术"entropy imaging"，将空间纹理映射为可计算信息密度CID（大系统极限下趋近Shannon熵率）；应用于石墨烯的毫开尔文微波阻抗显微图像，并用无序Hofstadter模型、分数量子霍尔转变、MoSe2/WS2 moiré带边交叉作对照，同时分析CID几何信息与关联长度、多重分形统计的关系。

Result: CID在量子霍尔转变附近出现显著极大值，度量临界景观的信息丰富空间组织；无序Hofstadter模型复现该行为，并可从图像序列识别量子临界点而无需对无序实现做系综平均；框架亦追踪分数量子霍尔转变并分辨孤立分数puddle；MoSe2/WS2 moiré的平庸带边交叉呈定性不同行为；CID编码的几何信息不可约化为关联长度，甚至可区分多重分形统计相同的纹理。

Conclusion: 无损压缩可作为量化量子材料涌现空间组织的通用框架，具备跨成像模态与物理系统的可移植性。

Abstract: Phase transitions can generate intricate spatial organization invisible to spatially averaged probes, yet quantitative measures of this organization remain limited. Here we develop "entropy imaging," an information-theoretic technique that harnesses lossless compression to quantify spatial complexity, predictability, and order in quantum materials. Using this approach, emergent spatial textures can be imaged and converted into an experimentally-accessible observable: the computable information density (CID), a compression-based measure of information complexity that approaches the Shannon entropy rate in the large-system limit. Applied to millikelvin microwave impedance microscopy of graphene, the method reveals a pronounced maximum in the CID near quantum Hall transitions, a quantitative measure of the information-rich spatial organization of the critical landscape. A disordered Hofstadter model reproduces this behavior and shows that the CID can identify the quantum critical point from a sequence of images, without ensemble averaging over disorder realizations. The same framework tracks fractional quantum Hall transitions and resolves isolated fractional puddles that coexist with a disordered background. By contrast, the trivial band-edge crossover in a MoSe2/WS2 moiré heterobilayer exhibits qualitatively different behavior, suggesting that the pronounced CID enhancement observed at the quantum Hall critical point is not simply a generic feature of a conductive-insulating crossover. We further show that the geometric information encoded in CID is not reducible to a correlation length, and it can even distinguish spatial textures with identical multifractal statistics. Together, these results establish lossless compression as a framework for quantifying emergent spatial organization in quantum materials, with portability across imaging modalities and physical systems.

</details>


### [146] [Wrinkling dynamics of thin crystalline sheets and its dependency on topological defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35786)
*Emma Radice,Ingo Nitschke,Marco Salvalaglio,Axel Voigt*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 采用Surface Phase-Field-Crystal-Helfrich (SPFCH)模型研究单轴压缩下薄晶态薄膜的起皱动力学，同时追踪晶格缺陷（位错、晶界）与起皱缺陷（wrinklon），揭示面内/面外弛豫耦合对起皱起始与粗化的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 薄晶态薄膜在压缩下的起皱涉及晶格有序、面外变形与拓扑缺陷的耦合，但晶格缺陷（位错、晶界）与起皱缺陷（wrinklon）如何共同影响起皱起始、波长选择与粗化行为尚不清楚，缺乏能同时解析两类缺陷演化的统一模型。

Method: 构建Surface Phase-Field-Crystal-Helfrich (SPFCH)模型，耦合晶态序参量、面外形变与Helfrich型弯曲能，对单轴压缩下的薄晶态薄片进行动力学模拟；通过解析两类缺陷（六角晶格中的位错/晶界，以及起皱图样中的wrinklons）的演化与相互作用，分析面内与面外弛豫机制之间的竞争。

Result: 晶格缺陷不改变粗化的标度律；但会定量地影响起皱起始条件与出现的起皱波长，促使起皱图样更不规则、wrinklon密度更高，并改变长时间尺度的形貌演化。

Conclusion: 面内塑性/缺陷介导的弛豫与面外弯曲弛豫的耦合是调控晶态薄膜起皱的关键：晶格缺陷主要改变起皱起始与波长选择、图样规则性及长期形貌演化，而对粗化标度律影响甚微。

Abstract: We investigate the wrinkling dynamics of thin crystalline sheets under uniaxial compression using a Surface Phase-Field-Crystal-Helfrich (SPFCH) model, which captures the coupling between crystalline order, out-of-plane deformations, and topological defects. In particular, the model allows us to track and characterize two distinct classes of defects, namely those forming in the underlying hexagonal crystal lattice, such as dislocations and grain boundaries, and defects in the wrinkling patterns, known as wrinklons. By resolving the evolution and interactions of these defects, we investigate how the interplay between in-plane and out-of-plane relaxation mechanisms influences wrinkling onset and the coarsening regime. Our results indicate that crystalline defects do not affect coarsening scaling, but they quantitatively affect the onset of wrinkling and the emerging wrinkling wavelength, promote more irregular wrinkle patterns with a higher density of wrinklons, and modify long-timescale morphological evolution.

</details>


### [147] [Modeling the CNOT gate in Germanium double quantum dots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35799)
*Mauricio J. Rodriguez,Yann-Michel Niquet*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文在真实Ge/GeSi空穴自旋量子比特器件中建模了由交换相互作用介导的CNOT门，采用紧凑的缀饰基（singlet/triplet多体态）高效框架进行含时模拟，量化了非绝热性、AC Stark位移与电荷噪声对保真度的限制及对屏障控制、微波驱动和电荷噪声稳定性的要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ge/GeSi空穴自旋量子比特是颇具前景的两比特门平台，但交换介导CNOT门的保真度限制因素（非绝热性、驱动条件、电荷噪声）缺乏在真实器件中的系统量化；常规组态相互作用方法计算代价过高，难以进行快速含时模拟。

Method: 构建基于多体单态与三重态紧凑缀饰基的高效计算框架，对交换相互作用介导的CNOT门进行快速含时仿真；在理想无噪声条件下分析非绝热性与驱动相关误差，并进一步引入准静态电荷噪声（电荷俘获与纳米尺度电荷运动）评估其影响。

Result: 隧穿势垒斜坡的非绝热性（被交换相互作用的横向分量增强）限制了CNOT门速度；条件共振的AC Stark位移及对互补跃迁的离共振驱动显著降低保真度；准静态电荷噪声中，量子比特附近电荷俘获会严重破坏条件操作，而纳米尺度电荷运动导致的保真度损失敏感依赖于对应偶极的位置与强度。

Conclusion: 研究量化了Ge/GeSi空穴自旋量子比特实现高保真两比特操作对势垒控制、微波驱动和电荷噪声稳定性的具体要求，为器件与脉冲优化提供指导。

Abstract: We model a controlled-NOT (CNOT) gate mediated by exchange interactions in a realistic Ge/GeSi hole spin-qubit device. We use for that purpose a computationally efficient framework based on a compact dressed basis of many-body singlet and triplet states, which enables fast time-dependent simulations that would be prohibitive within a conventional configuration-interaction approach. We discuss the dominant sources of infidelity in ideal (noise-free) devices. On the one hand, the nonadiabaticity of the tunnel barrier ramps, enhanced by the transverse component of the exchange interaction, imposes limits on the speed of the CNOT gate. On the other hand, the AC Stark shift of the conditional resonance and the off-resonant driving of the complementary transition can significantly degrade the gate fidelity and call for a careful optimization of the driving conditions. Finally, we investigate the impact of quasi-static charge noise on the fidelity of the CNOT gate. We find that the capture of charges near the qubits can strongly disrupt the conditional operation, while the nanometer-scale motion of charges results in a loss of fidelity that sensitively depends on the position and strength of the corresponding dipole. Our results quantify the requirements on barrier control, microwave driving, and charge-noise stability for high-fidelity two-qubit operations in Ge/GeSi hole-spin qubits.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.dis-nn'></div>

# cond-mat.dis-nn [[Back]](#toc)

### [148] [Controlling transport with structured long-range hopping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33393)
*Boris Gurevich,Michael H. Kolodrubetz*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 研究一维无序链中最近邻跃迁与长程跃迁共存时的输运行为，揭示均匀长程跃迁振幅在弱无序区强烈抑制退局域化，而长程振幅随机化可消除该抑制区，从而增强输运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无序与长程跃迁的联合效应在低维量子系统的局域化与输运中起关键作用，但二者的微观互作机制及长程耦合振幅的具体形式（均匀或随机）如何影响输运效率尚缺乏清晰刻画。

Method: 构建一维无序链模型：对角无序加最近邻跃迁，再叠加长程跃迁项，长程耦合振幅取均匀或随机两种形式；数值表征输运效率，并在Anderson本征态基底上给出解析解以揭示微观机制。

Result: 均匀长程跃迁振幅下，最近邻跃迁在相当宽的弱无序范围内强烈抑制退局域化；此前报道的"无序增强输运"区可理解为从该强抑制态中恢复退局域化；向长程跃迁振幅引入随机性可消除低无序强抑制区，提供额外输运增强机制并定性改变系统对无序的响应。

Conclusion: 输运并非无序强度的单调函数：均匀长程耦合制造的低无序抑制区是"无序增强输运"现象的来源；长程振幅的随机性是调制该抑制区、增强输运并改变无序响应的有效控制参量。

Abstract: The combined effects of disorder and long-range hopping play an important role in determining localization and transport in low-dimensional quantum systems. We study transport in a one-dimensional disordered chain with nearest-neighbor hopping supplemented by a long-range hopping term. The long-range coupling is characterized by site-dependent amplitudes that may be either uniform or random. We investigate the interplay between diagonal disorder and long-range hopping, characterizing the resulting transport efficiency. We find that nearest-neighbor hopping in the presence of uniform long-range hopping amplitudes strongly suppresses delocalization over a range of weak disorder. The disorder-enhanced transport regime, established in previous studies, can be understood here as the recovery of delocalization from this strongly suppressed regime. An analytical solution in the Anderson eigenstate basis reveals the microscopic origin of this behavior. In particular, we show that the low-disorder regime of strongly suppressed delocalization can be eliminated by introducing randomness into the long-range hopping amplitudes, providing an additional mechanism for enhancing transport and qualitatively changing the response of the system to disorder.

</details>


### [149] [Dynamic Pseudogap Model: Analytic Results vs Monte Carlo Numerics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33436)
*Michael V. Sadovskii*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文将动态赝能隙模型（电子被具有有限嵌套矢量Q、振荡频率ω₀与阻尼γ的随机时变高斯场散射）的解析谱密度结果与蒙特卡洛（MC）精确数值解进行系统对比，涵盖公度（实）与非公度（复）涨落及宽参数范围，检验解析方法的适用边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有动态赝能隙模型的解析结果依赖若干近似（如对易近似下忽略Magnus型修正），其有效性与定量精度尚未被独立验证，需要以无近似的数值解为基准厘清解析方法的适用范围。

Method: 格林函数采用两种解析表示：带历史依赖Bogoliubov能隙的双Poisson级数，以及精确静态连分数解；同时构建MC精确数值解作为基准。在相干区由双级数描述动态边带形成，随相干性丧失过渡到连分数刻画的准静态涨落能隙区；并考察公度与非公度涨落两种情形。

Result: MC解克服了解析方法（如对易近似中忽略Magnus修正）的局限，确立了各解析方法的适用极限，并证实连分数解在静态极限下是精确的；总体而言，解析表达式在其适用范围内与MC数值结果吻合较为满意，定性物理图像成立。

Conclusion: 解析理论在其适用域内可靠地刻画了动态赝能隙的形成与谱密度演化，其边界由MC基准定量界定；连分数解在静态极限下严格成立，双级数表示在相干区有效。

Abstract: Recently proposed dynamic pseudogap model analytical results for spectral density of an electron scattered by random time-dependent Gaussian field representing fluctuations with finite nesting vector ${\bf Q}$, characteristic oscillation frequency $ω_0$ and damping $γ$, leading to pseudogap formation, are compared with exact numerical solution by Monte Carlo (MC) approach. We consider both commensurate (real) and incommensurate (complex) fluctuations, for a wide range of parameters of the model. Electron Green's function is approximated either by a double Poisson series with history-dependent Bogoliubov gaps or by exact static continued fraction solution. In the coherent regime double-series representation provides description of dynamic sideband formation in the spectral density. As coherence is progressively lost, the theory crosses over to the quasistatic fluctuating-gap regime described by the exact continued-fraction solution. The exact MC solution overcomes the limitations of analytic approach, such as neglect of Magnus-type corrections in commuting approximation, and establish the limits of analytic approach, as well as confirming the exactness of continued fraction solution in static limit. In general, analytic expressions demonstrate rather satisfactory agreement with exact MC numerics within their limits, confirming the overall qualitative physical picture of dynamic pseudogap formation.

</details>


### [150] [Matter that learns what matters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34428)
*Natalia G. Berloff*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文在模型光学网络中展示材料可通过持续性的"记录"实现对信息源的注意力选择，并推导保证学习稳定性的边界与学习规则，提出"读取方式应决定写入方式"的原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究材料能否"学会"判断哪些信息源值得关注，即注意力机制能否在物理系统中自发形成，而无需预先识别有用来源。

Method: 构建模型光学网络：每个链路中行为结果训练出持久记录，该记录偏置是否查询某源以及该源对存储答案的修改强度；通过共振读出分析学习稳定性，推导相关信号被采纳、无关信号被拒绝、答案可被修订的边界，并据此设计限制强化的学习规则；引入在失败动作后增强负向写入的局部状态机制。

Result: 强化可能将记录推过共振点使其影响减弱；所推导边界指导的学习规则在模拟中能获得有用记录并保持其功能性；相关性变化时旧记录会阻碍自身被取代，而局部状态机制可释放过时记录并更常完成学习序列。

Conclusion: 材料的经验塑造了其未来学习可用的信息；记忆的读取方式应决定其写入方式，这是维持功能性与可修订性的关键设计原则。

Abstract: Can a material learn what deserves its attention? We show that it can in a model optical network, in which action outcomes train a persistent record in each link. The record biases whether a source is consulted and sets how strongly it changes a stored answer, so useful sources gain attention and influence without being identified in advance. The network's experience thus shapes the information available for its future learning. Resonant readouts make this learning fragile: reinforcement can carry a record past resonance, where it persists but its influence weakens. We derive bounds within which relevant signals guide the answer, irrelevant signals are rejected and stored answers remain open to revision. These bounds guide a learning rule that limits reinforcement as records form; in simulations, it acquires useful records and keeps them functional. When relevance changes, an obsolete record can block its own replacement. A local state that strengthens negative writing after unsuccessful actions releases obsolete records and completes the learning sequence more often. How a memory is read should decide how it is written.

</details>


### [151] [Symmetry-driven in-situ physical adjoint computing beyond reciprocity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34637)
*Cunshen Li,Rong Li,Jiongning Che,Cheng-Zhen Wang,Liang Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 提出对称性驱动的原位物理伴随计算（Sym-iPAC），利用三类转置对称在非互易系统原始硬件上获取伴随响应，突破传统原位物理伴随优化对互易性的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非互易性通过定向传输增强波调控与信息处理能力，但传统原位物理伴随优化依赖互易性来获取伴随响应，在非互易系统中失效。因此需要一种不依赖互易性、仍能在原始硬件上得到伴随梯度的方案。

Method: 提出Sym-iPAC框架，通过三类转置对称获取伴随响应：局部几何置换、复数规范重加权，以及由Schur约化揭示的有效端口动力学中的潜对称；并在可编程非互易微波网络中进行实验验证。

Result: 实验验证了上述转置关系，成功获得伴随梯度，并实现闭环优化。

Conclusion: 实验可及的转置对称成为超越互易性、实现原位物理伴随计算的可行途径。

Abstract: Nonreciprocity enriches wave control and information processing through directional transport, yet challenges conventional in-situ physical adjoint optimization, which relies on reciprocity to acquire adjoint responses. Here we demonstrate symmetry-driven in-situ physical adjoint computing (Sym-iPAC) in nonreciprocal systems, which enables access to adjoint responses on the original hardware through three classes of transpose symmetry: local geometric permutations, complex gauge reweighting, and latent symmetry in effective port dynamics revealed by Schur reduction. Experiments with programmable nonreciprocal microwave networks validate these transpose relations and yield adjoint gradients, enabling closed-loop optimization. These results establish experimentally accessible transpose symmetry as a route to in-situ physical adjoint computing beyond reciprocity.

</details>
